NORDA - SELECTED UX/UI CODE COMPONENTS
======================================
Source: Original Lucian UX/UI Prompt collection
Selection: 75 components selected specifically for NorDa's public website, digital products/templates, support chat/AI, and admin dashboard.
Content: Original source blocks preserved (HTML + CSS + JavaScript). The code and original English descriptions have not been translated or rewritten.

IMPORTANT
---------
- These are original UI demo snippets/components, not a complete NorDa website or ready-made integration.
- Many components depend on the source theme tokens in tokens.css (--ux-* variables) and the shared .ux base styles. Map those tokens to NorDa's existing design system before integration.
- Integrate only the components needed by the relevant page. Check dependencies and test responsiveness/accessibility before production use.
- Original IDs are retained so each component can be matched to its source/demo.

NORDA COMPONENT 01 / 75 - ID 295 - Contact form that adapts to the request
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #295 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#295 -->
<!--
Element: Contact form that adapts to the request
ID: 295 · Category: Forms & Input · Tags: contact-form, conditional, form, attachment, support
What it does: Choose Sales, Support or Partnership at the top of a contact form and the pill slides over while the fields below fold and unfold into questions for that type. In Support, pressing Attach screenshot adds a file chip whose upload bar fills. The ring beside the message box fills with the characters you've typed, and Send moves you to a received card with a ticket number.
How to use it: Switch the request type, write a message and send it
Footprint: fixed size, tall
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#295
-->
<style>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sg = root.querySelector('.u295-sg'), pl = root.querySelector('.u295-pl'), DY = root.querySelectorAll('.u295-dy'), tx = root.querySelector('.u295-tx'), rgf = root.querySelector('.u295-rg .f'), go = root.querySelector('.u295-go'), at = root.querySelector('.u295-at'), fl = root.querySelector('.u295-fl'), flb = fl.querySelector('em b'), fls = fl.querySelector('small'), no = root.querySelector('.u295-no');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var TP = ['Sales', 'Support', 'Partnership'], MAX = 200, cur = -1;
  var T = TP.map(function (t, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u295-tp'; b.setAttribute('role', 'radio'); b.textContent = t;
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { pick(i); });
    sg.appendChild(b); return b;
  });
  function pick(i) {
    if (i === cur) return; cur = i;
    T.forEach(function (b, k) { b.setAttribute('aria-checked', k === i); });
    pl.style.transform = 'translateX(' + i * 100 + '%)';
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(DY, function (d, k) { d.classList.toggle('is-on', k === i); d.setAttribute('aria-hidden', k !== i); d.inert = k !== i; });
  }
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u295-ch'), function (g) {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(g.children, function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { Array.prototype.forEach.call(g.children, function (o) { o.setAttribute('aria-pressed', o === b); }); }); });
  });
  at.addEventListener('click', function () {
    root.classList.add('is-file'); fl.classList.add('is-on'); flb.style.width = '0';
    var t0 = performance.now(), ms = rm ? 1 : 1000;
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { flb.style.width = '100%'; });
    (function f(now) { var k = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / ms); fls.textContent = Math.round(240 * k) + ' / 240 KB'; if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f); })(t0);
  });
  fl.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click', function () { root.classList.remove('is-file'); fl.classList.remove('is-on'); at.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
  tx.addEventListener('input', function () {
    var n = tx.value.length, k = n / MAX; rgf.style.strokeDashoffset = (56.5 * (1 - k)).toFixed(1); rgf.style.stroke = k > .9 ? '#F59E0B' : '';
    go.disabled = tx.value.trim().length < 2;
  });
  go.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (go.disabled) return;
    no.textContent = '#' + ['A', 'B', 'C'][cur] + '-' + (2040 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 60));
    root.classList.add('is-sent');
  });
  pick(0);
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 02 / 75 - ID 244 - Form validation that takes you to errors
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #244 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#244 -->
<!--
Element: Form validation that takes you to errors
ID: 244 · Category: Forms & Input · Tags: validation, form, error-summary, accessibility, signup
What it does: Press Sign up at the bottom of a sign-up form and a summary such as “4 fields need attention” drops in at the top, while the wrong fields shake with red outlines and their hints unfold. The form glides up to the first wrong field and puts the cursor there, and pressing an item in the summary jumps straight to that field. Fixed fields fold their hint and gain a check as the summary count drops, and once everything is fixed the sign-up completes.
How to use it: Press Sign up at the bottom, then fix the wrong fields one by one
Footprint: fixed size, tall
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#244
-->
<style>

.u244-sc{position:absolute;inset:0;overflow-y:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain;scrollbar-width:none;padding:24px 20px 26px}

<div class="ux u244">
  <form class="u244-sc" novalidate aria-label="Sign-up form">
    <h3>Sign up</h3><p>Takes about a minute</p>
    <div class="u244-sm" role="alert"><div><div class="u244-sb"><b><i>!</i><span class="u244-ct"></span></b><ul></ul></div></div></div>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sc = root.querySelector('.u244-sc'), fs = root.querySelector('.u244-fs'), ul = root.querySelector('.u244-sb ul'), ct = root.querySelector('.u244-ct'), ag = root.querySelector('.u244-ag'), go = root.querySelector('.u244-go');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var uid = 'u244' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  var F = [['Name', 'text', '', 'Please enter your name'], ['Email', 'email', 'minji@', 'That doesn’t look like an email (e.g. name@site.com)'], ['Password', 'password', 'abc', 'Use 8+ characters with at least one number'], ['Phone (optional)', 'tel', '', '']];
  var RULE = [function (v) { return v.trim().length > 0; }, function (v) { return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(v); }, function (v) { return v.length >= 8 && /\d/.test(v); }, function () { return true; }];
  var tried = false;
  var fields = F.map(function (f, i) {
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'u244-fd';
    d.innerHTML = '<label></label><div class="u244-bx"><input class="u244-in" autocomplete="off"><span class="u244-ok" aria-hidden="true"></span></div><div class="u244-ms"><div><span></span></div></div>';
    var inp = d.querySelector('input'); inp.type = f[1]; inp.value = f[2]; inp.id = uid + i;

    var ms = d.querySelector('.u244-ms span'); ms.textContent = f[3]; ms.id = uid + 'm' + i; inp.setAttribute('aria-describedby', ms.id);
    inp.addEventListener('input', function () { if (tried) state(); });
    fs.appendChild(d); return { el: d, inp: inp };
  });
  function bad() { var b = []; fields.forEach(function (f, i) { if (!RULE[i](f.inp.value)) b.push(i); }); if (ag.getAttribute('aria-checked') !== 'true') b.push(4); return b; }
  function goTo(el, fn) {
    var y = Math.max(0, el.offsetTop - 70), y0 = sc.scrollTop, t0 = performance.now(), ms = rm ? 0 : 520;
    (function f(now) { var k = ms ? Math.min(1, (now - t0) / ms) : 1, e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3); sc.scrollTop = y0 + (y - y0) * e; if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f); else if (fn) fn(); })(t0);
  }
  var listed = [];
  function state() {
    var b = bad();
    fields.forEach(function (f, i) { var wrong = b.indexOf(i) >= 0; f.el.classList.toggle('is-bad', wrong); f.el.classList.toggle('is-good', !wrong && f.inp.value.length > 0); f.inp.setAttribute('aria-invalid', wrong); });
    ag.classList.toggle('is-bad', b.indexOf(4) >= 0);
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(ul.children, function (li) { li.classList.toggle('is-fixed', b.indexOf(+li.dataset.i) < 0); });
    ct.textContent = b.length + ' fields need attention';
    root.classList.toggle('is-err', b.length > 0);
    return b;
  }
  function shake(el) { el.classList.remove('is-shake'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('is-shake'); }
  sc.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (go.classList.contains('is-done')) return;
    tried = true; var b = state();
    if (!b.length) { go.classList.add('is-done'); go.textContent = 'You’re in! Welcome'; return; }
    ul.innerHTML = '';
    b.forEach(function (i) {
      var li = document.createElement('li'); li.dataset.i = i; var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = '#'; a.textContent = i < 4 ? F[i][0].replace(/ \(.*\)/, '') : 'Terms';
      a.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.preventDefault(); var t = i < 4 ? fields[i] : null; goTo(t ? t.el : ag, function () { (t ? t.inp : ag).focus({ preventScroll: true }); }); });
      li.appendChild(a); ul.appendChild(li);
    });
    b.forEach(function (i) { shake(i < 4 ? fields[i].el : ag); });
    var first = b[0];
    setTimeout(function () { goTo(first < 4 ? fields[first].el : ag, function () { (first < 4 ? fields[first].inp : ag).focus({ preventScroll: true }); }); }, rm ? 0 : 200);

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var items = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u044-it'));
  var qs = items.map(function (it) { return it.querySelector('.u044-q'); });
  var uid = 'u044-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  items.forEach(function (it, i) {
    var q = qs[i], body = it.querySelector('.u044-body');
    q.id = uid + '-q' + i; body.id = uid + '-a' + i;
    q.setAttribute('aria-controls', body.id); body.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', q.id);
  });
  function set(it, open) {
    it.classList.toggle('open', open);
    it.querySelector('.u044-q').setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
    it.querySelector('.u044-a a').tabIndex = open ? 0 : -1;
  }
  items.forEach(function (it, i) {
    set(it, it.classList.contains('open'));
    qs[i].addEventListener('click', function () {
      var willOpen = !it.classList.contains('open');
      items.forEach(function (o) { if (o !== it) set(o, false); });
      set(it, willOpen);
    });
    qs[i].addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      var n = e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? i + 1 : e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? i - 1 : e.key === 'Home' ? 0 : e.key === 'End' ? qs.length - 1 : -2;
      if (n === -2) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      qs[(n + qs.length) % qs.length].focus();
    });
    it.querySelector('.u044-a a').addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
  });
})();
</script>

<div class="ux u027">
  <div class="u027-bar">
    <span class="u027-logo"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Lumen</span>
    <nav class="u027-nav" aria-label="Main menu"><a href="#">Home</a><a href="#">Explore</a><a href="#">Saved</a></nav>
    <div class="u027-field">
      <button class="u027-go" type="button" aria-label="Open search" aria-expanded="false"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="M16 16l4.2 4.2"/></svg></button>
      <input class="u027-in" type="text" role="combobox" aria-expanded="false" aria-autocomplete="list" placeholder="Search templates, colors, people" tabindex="-1" autocomplete="off">
      <button class="u027-x" type="button" aria-label="Close search" tabindex="-1"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="u027-pop">
    <p class="u027-sec">Recent</p>
    <div class="u027-recent"><button type="button">Dashboard</button><button type="button">Motion</button><button type="button">Dark theme</button></div>
    <p class="u027-sec">Suggested</p>
    <ul class="u027-list" role="listbox" aria-label="Search results"></ul>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var go = root.querySelector('.u027-go');
  var input = root.querySelector('.u027-in');
  var x = root.querySelector('.u027-x');
  var list = root.querySelector('.u027-list');
  var uid = 'u027-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  list.id = uid;
  input.setAttribute('aria-controls', uid);
  var DATA = [
    ['Motion guide 2026', 'Doc', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#8B6CFF,#5B8CFF)'],
    ['Color palette · Dawn', 'Collection', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FF8A65,#FF5A6E)'],
    ['Mobile onboarding screens', 'Template', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#22C3A6,#0EA5E9)'],
    ['Seoyeon Kim · Motion designer', 'Person', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FFC24B,#FF8A3D)'],
    ['Color contrast checklist', 'Doc', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#A796E8,#6A57F2)'],
    ['Checkout screen kit', 'Template', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#3D8BFF,#22C3A6)']
  ];
  var shown = [], act = -1;
  function esc(s) { return s.replace(/[&<>]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;' }[c]; }); }
  function render() {
    var q = input.value.trim();
    shown = DATA.filter(function (d) { return !q || d[0].indexOf(q) >= 0 || d[1].indexOf(q) >= 0; }).slice(0, 3);
    act = shown.length ? 0 : -1;
    if (!shown.length) { list.innerHTML = '<li class="u027-empty">No results for ‘' + esc(q) + '’</li>'; input.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant'); return; }
    list.innerHTML = shown.map(function (d, i) {
      var t = esc(d[0]);
      if (q) t = t.split(esc(q)).join('<mark>' + esc(q) + '</mark>');
      return '<li class="u027-opt" role="option" id="' + uid + '-' + i + '" aria-selected="' + (i === act) + '" style="transition-delay:' + (i * 40) + 'ms"><i style="--c:' + d[2] + '" aria-hidden="true"></i><span><b>' + t + '</b><small>' + d[1] + '</small></span></li>';
    }).join('');
    mark();
  }
  function mark() {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(list.children, function (li, i) { if (li.setAttribute) li.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(i === act)); });
    if (act >= 0) input.setAttribute('aria-activedescendant', uid + '-' + act); else input.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant');
  }
  function open(focus) {
    root.classList.add('is-open');
    go.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    input.tabIndex = 0; x.tabIndex = 0;
    render();
    if (focus !== false) setTimeout(function () { input.focus(); }, 120);
  }
  function close() {
    root.classList.remove('is-open');
    go.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    input.tabIndex = -1; x.tabIndex = -1;
    input.value = '';
    go.focus();
  }
  go.addEventListener('click', function () { root.classList.contains('is-open') ? input.focus() : open(); });
  x.addEventListener('click', close);
  input.addEventListener('input', render);
  input.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); close(); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' && shown.length) { e.preventDefault(); act = (act + 1) % shown.length; mark(); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' && shown.length) { e.preventDefault(); act = (act - 1 + shown.length) % shown.length; mark(); }
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && act >= 0 && !e.isComposing && e.keyCode !== 229) { e.preventDefault(); input.value = shown[act][0]; render(); }
  });
  list.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var li = e.target.closest('.u027-opt');
    if (!li) return;
    var i = Array.prototype.indexOf.call(list.children, li);
    input.value = shown[i][0];
    render();
    input.focus();
  });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u027-recent button'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { input.value = b.textContent; render(); input.focus(); });
  });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u027-nav a'), function (a) {
    a.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
  });
  open(false);
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 05 / 75 - ID 212 - Hide-on-scroll header
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #212 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#212 -->
<!--
Element: Hide-on-scroll header
ID: 212 · Category: Navigation · Tags: header, navbar, scroll, sticky, navigation
What it does: At the top, a roomy transparent header sits over the page; scroll down and it slides up out of the way to give you room to read. Scroll up even a little and it comes back down as a slim header with a blurred background and shadow, while a thin bar beneath it fills as you read. Back at the very top it turns transparent again.
How to use it: Scroll down inside the card, then scroll up a little
Footprint: fixed size, square

  <header class="u212-hd"><b><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Relay</b><span>Product</span><span>Pricing</span><span>Blog</span><strong>Get started</strong><span class="u212-pg" aria-hidden="true"></span></header>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sc = root.querySelector('.u212-sc'), bd = root.querySelector('.u212-bd'), pg = root.querySelector('.u212-pg');
  var P = ['If you try to make it perfect from the start, launch keeps slipping. Ship the smallest useful thing and refine it as people respond.', 'Keep meetings short and notes long. When the reason behind a decision is written down, the next person won’t repeat the same debate.', 'Automate the third time you repeat something. Automate too early and you cement what is still changing.', 'Finally, set the launch date first. A date is what lets you choose what to cut.'];
  P.forEach(function (t, i) { var p = document.createElement('p'); p.textContent = t; bd.appendChild(p); if (i === 1) bd.appendChild(document.createElement('i')); });
  var last = 0, acc = 0;
  sc.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
    var y = sc.scrollTop, d = y - last; last = y;
    acc = (d > 0) === (acc > 0) ? acc + d : d;
    root.classList.toggle('is-solid', y > 20);
    if (y < 60) root.classList.remove('is-hide');
    else if (acc > 8) root.classList.add('is-hide');
    else if (acc < -6) root.classList.remove('is-hide');
    pg.style.width = (y / Math.max(1, sc.scrollHeight - sc.clientHeight) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%';
  }, { passive: true });
})();
</script>

    <p>We visited the spaces of people who work quietly</p>
    <div class="u045-row" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
  </div>
  <div class="u045-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Full menu" aria-hidden="true">
    <small>MENU</small>
    <ul class="u045-nav">
      <li><a class="u045-ln" href="#" style="--i:0" aria-current="page"><em>01</em><span>Magazine</span></a></li>
      <li><a class="u045-ln" href="#" style="--i:1"><em>02</em><span>Spaces</span></a></li>
      <li><a class="u045-ln" href="#" style="--i:2"><em>03</em><span>People</span></a></li>
      <li><a class="u045-ln" href="#" style="--i:3"><em>04</em><span>Subscribe</span></a></li>
    </ul>
    <div class="u045-foot"><a href="#">Instagram</a><a class="go" href="#">Sign in</a></div>
  </div>
  <div class="u045-top">
    <span class="u045-logo"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Atelier</span>
    <button class="u045-ham" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Open menu"><i></i><i></i><i></i></button>
  </div>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var ham = root.querySelector('.u045-ham');
  var sheet = root.querySelector('.u045-sheet');
  var page = root.querySelector('.u045-page');
  var focusables = Array.prototype.slice.call(sheet.querySelectorAll('a'));
  var open = false;
  function origin() {
    sheet.style.setProperty('--cx', (ham.offsetLeft + ham.parentNode.offsetLeft + ham.offsetWidth / 2) + 'px');
    sheet.style.setProperty('--cy', (ham.offsetTop + ham.parentNode.offsetTop + ham.offsetHeight / 2) + 'px');
  }
  function set(v) {
    open = v;
    origin();
    root.classList.toggle('is-open', v);
    ham.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(v));
    ham.setAttribute('aria-label', v ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu');
    sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!v));
    page.inert = v;
    focusables.forEach(function (a) { a.tabIndex = v ? 0 : -1; });
  }
  ham.addEventListener('click', function () { set(!open); });
  focusables.forEach(function (a) {
    a.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (a.classList.contains('u045-ln')) {
        root.querySelectorAll('.u045-ln').forEach(function (x) { x.removeAttribute('aria-current'); });
        a.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
      }
      set(false); ham.focus();
    });
  });
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!open) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); set(false); ham.focus(); return; }
    if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;
    var list = [ham].concat(focusables), i = list.indexOf(document.activeElement);
    if (i < 0) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    list[(i + (e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1) + list.length) % list.length].focus();
  });
  set(false);
})();
</script>

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #039 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#039 -->
<!--
Element: Sliding pill tabs
ID: 039 · Category: Menus & Tabs · Tags: tabs, segmented, pill, slide, stagger
What it does: Press a tab and the black pill stretches and glides over to it, while the list below slides in row by row from the direction you moved.
How to use it: Press the tabs back and forth. With a keyboard, use the left and right arrow keys
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#039

<style>
.u039{position:relative;width:340px;height:380px}
.u039-tabs{position:relative;display:flex;padding:5px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line)}
.u039-pill{position:absolute;left:0;top:5px;bottom:5px;width:var(--w,80px);border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-ink);transform:translateX(var(--x,5px));box-shadow:0 8px 16px -10px var(--ux-shadow-strong);transition:transform .5s var(--ux-spring),width .5s var(--ux-spring)}
.u039-tab{position:relative;flex:1;height:42px;border:0;padding:0;border-radius:999px;background:none;font-size:14px;font-weight:680;color:var(--ux-ink-2);white-space:nowrap;transition:color .35s var(--ux-ease)}

.u039-tab:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:2px}

.u039-list{position:absolute;inset:0;margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px}
.u039-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px;border-radius:18px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line);transition:transform .55s var(--ux-spring),opacity .35s var(--ux-ease)}
.u039-art{position:relative;width:52px;height:52px;flex:none;border-radius:14px;background:var(--g);overflow:hidden}
.u039-art::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:radial-gradient(circle at 30% 25%,rgba(255,255,255,.45),transparent 55%)}
.u039-row b{display:block;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.02em}
.u039-row small{display:block;margin-top:3px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ux-ink-3)}
.u039-row em{margin-left:auto;font-style:normal;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:650;color:var(--ux-ink-3);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.u039-badge{margin-left:6px;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:6px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;background:var(--ux-accent-soft);color:var(--ux-accent);vertical-align:2px}
</style>
<div class="ux u039">
  <div class="u039-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Library">
    <span class="u039-pill" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    <button class="u039-tab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="true">All</button>
    <button class="u039-tab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false">Music</button>
    <button class="u039-tab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false">Podcasts</button>
    <button class="u039-tab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false">Audiobooks</button>
  </div>
  <div class="u039-panel" role="tabpanel"></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u039-tab'));
  var pill = root.querySelector('.u039-pill');
  var panel = root.querySelector('.u039-panel');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var DATA = [
    [['Midnight Drive', 'Lumen · Album', '3:40', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#8B6CFF,#FF6B8B)'], ['Design Talk #42', 'Motion Lab · Podcast', '48m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#22C3A6,#3D8BFF)'], ['Walking Slowly', 'Audiobook · Chapter 2', '1h 12m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FFB547,#FF7A45)'], ['Neon Garden', 'Kiri · Single', '2:58', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#3D8BFF,#B04FD0)']],
    [['Midnight Drive', 'Lumen · Album', '3:40', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#8B6CFF,#FF6B8B)'], ['Neon Garden', 'Kiri · Single', '2:58', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#3D8BFF,#B04FD0)'], ['Paper Moon', 'Haru · EP', '4:12', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FF8A65,#FFC24B)'], ['Blue Hour', 'Sola · Album', '3:05', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#0EA5E9,#22C3A6)']],
    [['Design Talk #42', 'Motion Lab · New episode', '48m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#22C3A6,#3D8BFF)'], ['People at Work', 'Studio F · Ep. 18', '36m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FF7A93,#8B6CFF)'], ['Code Review Radio', 'Devcast · Ep. 7', '52m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#5B8CFF,#22C3A6)'], ['Morning Briefing', 'Daily · Today', '12m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FFB547,#E5484D)']],
    [['Walking Slowly', 'Audiobook · On chapter 2', '1h 12m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FFB547,#FF7A45)'], ['Small Habits', 'Audiobook · Not started', '6h', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#8B6CFF,#3D8BFF)'], ['Ocean Hours', 'Audiobook · Finished', '4h 30m', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#0EA5E9,#5B8CFF)'], ['Night Library', 'Audiobook · New', '8h', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#B04FD0,#FF6B8B)']]
  ];
  var cur = 0, list = null;
  function movePill(animate) {
    var t = tabs[cur];
    if (!animate) pill.style.transition = 'none';
    pill.style.setProperty('--x', t.offsetLeft + 'px');
    pill.style.setProperty('--w', t.offsetWidth + 'px');
    if (!animate) { void pill.offsetWidth; pill.style.transition = ''; }
  }
  function make(i) {
    var ul = document.createElement('ul');
    ul.className = 'u039-list';
    ul.innerHTML = DATA[i].map(function (r, k) {
      return '<li class="u039-row" style="transition-delay:' + (k * 45) + 'ms"><span class="u039-art" style="--g:' + r[3] + '" aria-hidden="true"></span><span><b>' + r[0] + (i === 0 && k === 0 ? '<span class="u039-badge">Playing</span>' : '') + '</b><small>' + r[1] + '</small></span><em>' + r[2] + '</em></li>';
    }).join('');
    return ul;
  }
  function select(i, focus) {
    if (i === cur && list) return;
    var dir = i > cur ? 1 : -1;
    cur = i;
    tabs.forEach(function (t, k) { t.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(k === i)); t.tabIndex = k === i ? 0 : -1; });
    if (focus) tabs[i].focus();
    movePill(true);
    var next = make(i), old = list;
    list = next;
    if (!old || rm) { if (old) old.remove(); panel.appendChild(next); return; }
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(next.children, function (r) { r.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (dir * 70) + '%)'; r.style.opacity = '0'; });
    panel.appendChild(next);
    void next.offsetWidth;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(next.children, function (r) { r.style.transform = ''; r.style.opacity = ''; });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(old.children, function (r) { r.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (-dir * 60) + '%)'; r.style.opacity = '0'; });
    setTimeout(function () { old.remove(); }, 700);
  }
  tabs.forEach(function (t, i) {
    t.addEventListener('click', function () { select(i); });
    t.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      var d = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0;
      if (d) { e.preventDefault(); select((i + d + tabs.length) % tabs.length, true); }
    });
  });
  var uid = 'u039-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  panel.id = uid;
  tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.setAttribute('aria-controls', uid); });
  list = make(0);
  panel.appendChild(list);
  tabs.forEach(function (t, k) { t.tabIndex = k === 0 ? 0 : -1; });
  movePill(false);
})();
</script>


##############################################################################
NORDA COMPONENT 08 / 75 - ID 103 - Breadcrumb path
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #103 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#103 -->
<!--
Element: Breadcrumb path
ID: 103 · Category: Layout, Schedule & Design · Tags: breadcrumb, native, spatial
What it does: A one-line path through nested folders. Press a parent to climb back up, then pick a subfolder below to go down again.
How to use it: Press “Projects” in the path, then pick a folder below
Footprint: fluid width (follows its container)
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#103
-->
<style>
.u103{width:100%;max-width:400px;min-width:0;font-size:13px}.u103 button{font:inherit;color:inherit;cursor:pointer}.u103 input[type=range]{min-width:0;width:100%;accent-color:var(--ux-accent)}.u103 .sr{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;padding:0;margin:-1px;overflow:hidden;clip-path:inset(50%);white-space:nowrap}.u103{padding:16px 0;max-width:380px}.u103 nav{display:flex;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:3px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ux-line-2);padding-bottom:10px}.u103 button{border:0;background:transparent;min-height:44px;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:5px}.u103 nav button[aria-current=page]{font-weight:700;color:var(--ux-accent)}.u103 nav span{color:var(--ux-ink-3)}.u103 .places{display:flex;gap:12px;padding-top:15px;min-height:74px}.u103 .place{flex:1;text-align:left;border:1px solid var(--ux-line-2);background:var(--ux-surface);display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center}.u103 .place span{font-size:20px;color:var(--ux-accent)}.u103 .empty{margin:14px 10px;color:var(--ux-ink-2);font-size:13px}
</style>
<div class="ux u103"><nav aria-label="Document path"></nav><div class="places"></div></div>
<script>
(()=>{const root=document.currentScript.previousElementSibling,q=s=>root.querySelector(s),qa=s=>[...root.querySelectorAll(s)];const tree={name:'Studio',children:[{name:'Projects',children:[{name:'Aurora',children:[{name:'Draft',children:[]},{name:'Cover',children:[]}]},{name:'Archive',children:[]}]},{name:'Personal',children:[]}]};let path=[tree,tree.children[0],tree.children[0].children[0]];function paint(){q('nav').innerHTML=path.map((x,i)=>`<button data-i="${i}" ${i===path.length-1?'aria-current="page"':''}>${x.name}</button>${i<path.length-1?'<span aria-hidden="true">/</span>':''}`).join('');q('.places').innerHTML=path.at(-1).children.map((x,i)=>`<button class="place" data-child="${i}"><span aria-hidden="true">▱</span>${x.name}</button>`).join('')||'<p class="empty">Empty folder</p>';qa('[data-i]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{path=path.slice(0,+b.dataset.i+1);paint()});qa('[data-child]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{path.push(path.at(-1).children[+b.dataset.child]);paint()})}root.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){path=[tree,tree.children[0],tree.children[0].children[0]];paint();q('[aria-current]').focus()}};paint();})();
</script>

<!--
Element: Stacking toasts
ID: 070 · Category: Alerts & Feedback · Tags: toast, stack, swipe-dismiss, promise, notification
What it does: A new toast rises from the bottom to the front while earlier ones tuck behind it, shrinking. Hover and the stack fans out into a list and stops auto-dismissing; swipe a toast sideways to remove just that one. The “Saving” toast spins and then turns into done.
How to use it: Fire a few toasts with the buttons above, then hover the stack to fan it out. Swipe a toast sideways or close it with ×
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#070

<style>
.u070{position:relative;width:350px;height:370px}
.u070-btns{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
.u070-btns button{height:38px;padding:0 14px;border:0;border-radius:999px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line);font-size:13px;font-weight:720;color:var(--ux-ink);transition:transform .3s var(--ux-spring),box-shadow .3s}
.u070-btns button:hover{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line-2),0 8px 16px -10px var(--ux-shadow-strong)}
.u070-btns button:active{transform:scale(.94)}
.u070-btns button:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:2px}
.u070-btns i{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--c)}
.u070-zone{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:300px;outline:none}
.u070-t{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;min-height:68px;padding:12px 12px 12px 14px;border-radius:20px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 16px 30px -18px var(--ux-shadow-strong);transform-origin:50% 100%;transform:translate(var(--dx,0px),var(--y,0px)) scale(var(--s,1));opacity:var(--o,1);transition:transform .5s var(--ux-spring),opacity .35s;touch-action:pan-y;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none}
.u070-t.drag{transition:none}
.u070-t.enter{transform:translateY(90px) scale(.9);opacity:0}
.u070-t.leave{transform:translate(var(--dx,0px),var(--y,0px)) scale(var(--s,1));opacity:0;pointer-events:none}
.u070-t.leave.side{transform:translate(calc(var(--dir,1) * 420px),var(--y,0px)) rotate(calc(var(--dir,1) * 8deg))}
.u070-t>*{transition:opacity .25s}
.u070-t.back>*{opacity:0}
.u070-ic{width:36px;height:36px;flex:none;border-radius:12px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--c) 15%,transparent);color:var(--c)}
.u070-ic svg{width:18px;height:18px;fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:2.4;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}

@keyframes u070-spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
.u070-tx{flex:1;min-width:0}

.u070-act{height:32px;padding:0 11px;border:0;border-radius:10px;background:var(--ux-surface-2);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:750;color:var(--ux-ink)}

<div class="ux u070">
  <div class="u070-btns">
    <button type="button" data-k="ok" style="--c:var(--ux-mint)"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Saved</button>
    <button type="button" data-k="err" style="--c:var(--ux-danger)"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Error</button>
    <button type="button" data-k="info" style="--c:var(--ux-accent)"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>New comment</button>
    <button type="button" data-k="wait" style="--c:var(--ux-warn)"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Saving…</button>
  </div>
  <div class="u070-zone" role="region" aria-label="Notifications" tabindex="-1">
    <p class="u070-empty">Notifications stack up here</p>
    <div class="u070-live" aria-live="polite"></div>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var zone = root.querySelector('.u070-zone'), empty = root.querySelector('.u070-empty'), live = root.querySelector('.u070-live');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var ICON = { ok: '<svg viewBox="0 0 18 18"><path d="M4 9.4l3.2 3.2L14 5.6"/></svg>', err: '<svg viewBox="0 0 18 18"><path d="M9 5v5M9 13h.01"/></svg>', info: '<svg viewBox="0 0 18 18"><path d="M3.5 4.5h11v7h-6l-3 2.5v-2.5h-2z"/></svg>', wait: '<i></i>' };
  var K = {
    ok: ['var(--ux-mint)', 'Changes saved', 'Spring campaign landing · just now'],
    err: ['var(--ux-danger)', 'Upload failed', 'Check your connection', 'Retry'],
    info: ['var(--ux-accent)', 'Doyun Han left a comment', '“Love this transition!”', 'View'],
    wait: ['var(--ux-warn)', 'Saving…', '3 files · 12.4 MB']
  };
  var list = [], open = false, DUR = 4500, seq = 0;
  function layout() {
    var acc = 0;
    list.forEach(function (t, i) {
      var el = t.el;
      el.classList.toggle('back', !open && i > 0);
      if (open) { el.style.setProperty('--y', -acc + 'px'); el.style.setProperty('--s', 1); el.style.setProperty('--o', 1); acc += el.offsetHeight + 8; }
      else { el.style.setProperty('--y', -(i * 14) + 'px'); el.style.setProperty('--s', (1 - i * 0.06).toFixed(2)); el.style.setProperty('--o', i > 2 ? 0 : 1); }
      el.style.zIndex = 50 - i;
      el.inert = !open && i > 0;
    });
    empty.style.opacity = list.length ? '0' : '1';
  }
  function remove(t, dir) {
    var i = list.indexOf(t);
    if (i < 0) return;
    list.splice(i, 1);
    t.el.classList.add('leave');
    if (dir) { t.el.style.setProperty('--dir', dir); t.el.classList.add('side'); }
    setTimeout(function () { t.el.remove(); }, 450);
    layout();
  }
  function push(k) {
    var d = K[k], el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'u070-t enter';
    el.style.setProperty('--c', d[0]);
    el.setAttribute('role', k === 'err' ? 'alert' : 'status');
    el.innerHTML = '<span class="u070-ic" aria-hidden="true">' + ICON[k] + '</span><span class="u070-tx"><b></b><small></small></span>' + (d[3] ? '<button class="u070-act" type="button"></button>' : '') + '<button class="u070-x" type="button" aria-label="Dismiss"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 14"><path d="M3.5 3.5l7 7M10.5 3.5l-7 7"/></svg></button><span class="u070-bar" aria-hidden="true"></span>';
    el.querySelector('b').textContent = d[1]; el.querySelector('small').textContent = d[2];
    if (d[3]) el.querySelector('.u070-act').textContent = d[3];
    var t = { el: el, left: DUR, id: ++seq, k: k };
    zone.appendChild(el);
    list.unshift(t);
    void el.offsetWidth;
    el.classList.remove('enter');
    layout();
    live.textContent = d[1];
    el.querySelector('.u070-x').addEventListener('click', function () { remove(t); });
    if (d[3]) el.querySelector('.u070-act').addEventListener('click', function () { remove(t); if (k === 'err') push('wait'); });
    if (k === 'wait') {
      t.left = Infinity;
      setTimeout(function () {
        if (!root.isConnected || list.indexOf(t) < 0) return;
        el.style.setProperty('--c', K.ok[0]);
        el.querySelector('.u070-ic').innerHTML = ICON.ok;
        el.querySelector('b').textContent = 'All saved';
        el.querySelector('small').textContent = 'All 3 files uploaded';
        live.textContent = 'All saved';
        t.left = DUR;
      }, rm ? 300 : 1800);
    }
    var sx = 0, dx = 0, down = false;
    el.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { if (e.target.closest('button')) return; down = true; sx = e.clientX; dx = 0; el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); el.classList.add('drag'); });
    el.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (!down) return; dx = e.clientX - sx; el.style.setProperty('--dx', dx + 'px'); el.style.setProperty('--o', Math.max(0.2, 1 - Math.abs(dx) / 260)); });
    var end = function () { if (!down) return; down = false; el.classList.remove('drag'); if (Math.abs(dx) > 90) remove(t, dx > 0 ? 1 : -1); else { el.style.setProperty('--dx', '0px'); layout(); } };
    el.addEventListener('pointerup', end); el.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
  }
  var last = performance.now();
  (function loop(now) {
    if (!root.isConnected) return;
    var dt = now - last; last = now;
    if (!open) list.slice().forEach(function (t, i) {
      if (i > 0 || t.left === Infinity) return;
      t.left -= dt;
      t.el.style.setProperty('--left', Math.max(0, t.left / DUR).toFixed(3));
      if (t.left <= 0) remove(t);
    });
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  })(last);
  function setOpen(v) { if (open === v) return; open = v; layout(); }
  zone.addEventListener('pointerenter', function (e) { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') setOpen(true); });
  zone.addEventListener('pointerleave', function (e) { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && !zone.contains(document.activeElement)) setOpen(false); });
  zone.addEventListener('focusin', function () { setOpen(true); });
  zone.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) { if (!zone.contains(e.relatedTarget)) setOpen(false); });
  zone.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (!e.target.closest('button') && list.length > 1) setOpen(!open); });
  root.querySelectorAll('.u070-btns button').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { push(b.getAttribute('data-k')); }); });
  push('info'); push('ok');
  list.forEach(function (t) { t.left = Infinity; t.el.style.setProperty('--left', 1); });
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var its = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u072-it')), n = root.querySelector('.u072-n');
  var calm = root.querySelector('.u072-calm'), again = root.querySelector('.u072-again'), more = root.querySelector('.u072-more'), log = root.querySelector('.u072-log');
  function count() {
    var k = its.filter(function (it) { return !it.classList.contains('gone'); }).length;
    n.textContent = k ? k + (k === 1 ? ' alert' : ' alerts') : '';
    root.classList.toggle('is-clear', !k);
    calm.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!!k));
    again.tabIndex = k ? -1 : 0;
    return k;
  }
  its.forEach(function (it, i) {
    it.querySelector('.u072-x').addEventListener('click', function () {
      it.classList.add('gone'); it.inert = true;
      var rest = its.filter(function (x) { return !x.classList.contains('gone'); });
      var next = rest.filter(function (x) { return its.indexOf(x) > i; })[0] || rest[rest.length - 1];
      if (count() && next) next.querySelector('.u072-x').focus({ preventScroll: true });
      else setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) again.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 500);
    });
  });
  more.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var on = more.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== 'true';
    more.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(on));
    more.firstChild.textContent = on ? 'Hide ' : 'Details ';
    log.classList.toggle('on', on);
  });
  again.addEventListener('click', function () {
    its.forEach(function (it, i) { setTimeout(function () { if (!root.isConnected) return; it.classList.remove('gone'); it.inert = false; if (i === its.length - 1) count(); }, i * 90); });
    root.classList.remove('is-clear');
    setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) its[0].querySelector('.u072-x').focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 200);
  });
  count();
})();
</script>

<div class="ux u056">
  <div class="u056-sw" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Billing cycle">
    <span class="u056-knob" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    <button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked="true">Monthly</button>
    <button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked="false">Yearly <span class="u056-badge">2 months free</span></button>
  </div>
  <fieldset class="u056-plans">
    <legend class="u056-sr">Plan</legend>
    <span class="u056-ring" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    <label class="u056-p"><input type="radio" name="p" value="0"><span class="u056-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="u056-nm"><b>Basic</b><small>A workspace for one</small></span><span class="u056-pr"><span class="u056-od" data-p="0"></span><small>/ mo</small></span></label>
    <label class="u056-p"><input type="radio" name="p" value="1" checked><span class="u056-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="u056-nm"><b>Pro <em>Best</em></b><small>Teamwork, unlimited history</small></span><span class="u056-pr"><span class="u056-od" data-p="1"></span><small>/ mo</small></span></label>
  </fieldset>
  <ul class="u056-feat" aria-live="polite"></ul>
  <button class="u056-cta" type="button">Start Pro</button>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sw = root.querySelector('.u056-sw'), knob = root.querySelector('.u056-knob'), opts = sw.querySelectorAll('button');
  var badge = root.querySelector('.u056-badge'), ring = root.querySelector('.u056-ring');
  var labels = root.querySelectorAll('.u056-p'), radios = root.querySelectorAll('.u056-p input');
  var ods = root.querySelectorAll('.u056-od'), notes = root.querySelectorAll('.u056-pr small');
  var feat = root.querySelector('.u056-feat'), cta = root.querySelector('.u056-cta');
  var P = [
    { n: 'Basic', m: 12, y: 10, f: ['3 projects', '40 basic templates', '7-day version history'] },
    { n: 'Pro', m: 24, y: 20, f: ['Unlimited projects', 'Invite up to 10 people', 'Unlimited version history', 'Priority support'] }
  ];
  var yearly = false, plan = 1;
  var uid = 'u056' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  radios.forEach(function (r) { r.name = uid; });
  function od(el, v) {
    var s = '$' + v.toLocaleString('en-US');
    if (el.getAttribute('data-len') !== String(s.length)) {
      el.setAttribute('data-len', s.length);
      el.innerHTML = s.split('').map(function (ch, k) {
        if (ch < '0' || ch > '9') return '<span>' + ch + '</span>';
        var c = ''; for (var i = 0; i < 10; i++) c += '<span>' + i + '</span>';
        return '<span class="u056-col" style="--d:' + ch + ';--k:' + k + '">' + c + '</span>';
      }).join('');
    } else {
      var cols = el.querySelectorAll('.u056-col'), dg = s.replace(/\D/g, '');
      cols.forEach(function (c, i) { c.style.setProperty('--d', dg[i]); });
    }
    el.setAttribute('aria-label', s + ' per month');
    el.setAttribute('role', 'text');
  }
  function prices() {
    ods.forEach(function (el, i) { od(el, yearly ? P[i].y : P[i].m); });
    notes.forEach(function (n, i) { n.textContent = yearly ? '/ mo · $' + (P[i].y * 12).toLocaleString('en-US') + ' a year' : '/ mo'; });
  }
  function place() {
    var o = opts[yearly ? 1 : 0], l = labels[plan];
    knob.style.setProperty('--x', (o.offsetLeft - 4) + 'px');
    knob.style.setProperty('--w', o.offsetWidth + 'px');
    ring.style.setProperty('--y', l.offsetTop + 'px');
    ring.style.setProperty('--h', l.offsetHeight + 'px');
  }
  function setCycle(y, focus) {
    yearly = y;
    opts.forEach(function (o, k) { o.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(k === (y ? 1 : 0))); o.tabIndex = k === (y ? 1 : 0) ? 0 : -1; });
    place();
    if (focus) opts[y ? 1 : 0].focus();
    if (y) { badge.classList.remove('wig'); void badge.offsetWidth; badge.classList.add('wig'); }
    prices();
  }
  function setPlan(i) {
    plan = i;
    place();
    feat.innerHTML = P[i].f.map(function (t, k) { return '<li style="--i:' + k + '"><svg viewBox="0 0 18 18" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 9.4l3.2 3.2L14 5.6"/></svg>' + t + '</li>'; }).join('');
    cta.textContent = 'Start ' + P[i].n;
  }
  opts.forEach(function (o, k) {
    o.addEventListener('click', function () { setCycle(k === 1); });
    o.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); setCycle(!yearly, true); }
    });
  });
  radios.forEach(function (r, i) { r.addEventListener('change', function () { setPlan(i); }); });
  badge.addEventListener('animationend', function () { badge.classList.remove('wig'); });
  setCycle(false);
  setPlan(1);
  if (window.ResizeObserver) new ResizeObserver(place).observe(root);
})();
</script>

(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var bd = root.querySelector('.u217-bd'), df = root.querySelector('.u217-df'), sc = root.querySelector('.u217-sc');

  var groups = G.map(function (g) {
    var gp = document.createElement('div'); gp.className = 'u217-gp'; gp.innerHTML = '<div><div class="u217-gh"></div></div>';
    gp.querySelector('.u217-gh').textContent = g[0];
    var rows = g[1].map(function (f) {

      rw.innerHTML = '<div><div class="u217-r"><span></span></div></div>';
      var r = rw.querySelector('.u217-r'); r.firstChild.textContent = f[0];
      [1, 2, 3].forEach(function (k) { var c = document.createElement('span'); c.className = 'u217-c u217-c' + k; if (typeof f[k] === 'number') { c.innerHTML = f[k] ? Y : N; c.firstChild.setAttribute('role', 'img'); c.firstChild.setAttribute('aria-label', f[k] ? 'Included' : 'Not included'); } else c.textContent = f[k]; r.appendChild(c); });
      gp.firstChild.appendChild(rw);
      return { el: rw, same: f[1] === f[2] && f[2] === f[3] };
    });
    bd.appendChild(gp); return { el: gp, rows: rows };
  });
  root.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { var c = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.u217-c'); root.dataset.col = c ? c.className.match(/u217-c(\d)/)[1] : ''; });
  root.addEventListener('pointerover', function (e) { var c = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.u217-c'); root.dataset.col = c ? c.className.match(/u217-c(\d)/)[1] : ''; });
  sc.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { root.dataset.col = ''; });
  var only = false;
  df.addEventListener('click', function () {
    only = !only; df.setAttribute('aria-checked', only);
    groups.forEach(function (g) {
      var left = 0;
      g.rows.forEach(function (r) { var off = only && r.same; r.el.classList.toggle('is-off', off); r.el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', off); if (!off) left++; });
      g.el.classList.toggle('is-off', !left); g.el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', !left);
    });
  });
})();

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var stage = root.querySelector('.u051-stage'), holder = root.querySelector('.u051-bottle');
  var cart = root.querySelector('.u051-cart'), badge = cart.querySelector('b');
  var add = root.querySelector('.u051-add'), cname = root.querySelector('.u051-cname');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var C = [['#FF8A65', '#E5484D', 'Coral'], ['#7DB4FF', '#3D5BFF', 'Blue'], ['#A5F2D3', '#119C72', 'Mint'], ['#4A4A55', '#16151B', 'Charcoal']];
  var uid = 'u051' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7), n = 0, cur = 0, okT = 0;
  function bottle(id) {
    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 110 206"><defs><clipPath id="' + id + 'k"><path d="M38 4h34a4 4 0 0 1 4 4v18c14 6 24 18 24 34v130a12 12 0 0 1-12 12H22a12 12 0 0 1-12-12V60c0-16 10-28 24-34V8a4 4 0 0 1 4-4z"/></clipPath><linearGradient id="' + id + 'h" x1="0" x2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity="0"/><stop offset=".22" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity=".55"/><stop offset=".34" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity="0"/><stop offset=".85" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity=".22"/></linearGradient></defs>' +
      '<g clip-path="url(#' + id + 'k)"><rect class="b0" width="110" height="206"/><rect class="b1" width="110" height="206" y="-206"/><rect x="34" y="0" width="42" height="26" fill="#1C1B22" opacity=".9"/><rect x="10" y="98" width="90" height="30" fill="#fff" opacity=".2"/><rect width="110" height="206" fill="url(#' + id + 'h)"/></g></svg>';
  }
  holder.innerHTML = bottle(uid);
  var b0 = holder.querySelector('.b0'), b1 = holder.querySelector('.b1');
  var defs = holder.querySelector('defs');
  C.forEach(function (c, i) {
    var g = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'linearGradient');
    g.id = uid + 'c' + i; g.setAttribute('x1', 0); g.setAttribute('y1', 0); g.setAttribute('x2', 0); g.setAttribute('y2', 1);
    g.innerHTML = '<stop offset="0" stop-color="' + c[0] + '"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="' + c[1] + '"/>';
    defs.appendChild(g);
  });
  b0.setAttribute('fill', 'url(#' + uid + 'c0)');
  function pick(i) {
    if (i === cur) return;
    cur = i;
    root.style.setProperty('--c1', C[i][0]); root.style.setProperty('--c2', C[i][1]);
    cname.textContent = C[i][2];
    b1.setAttribute('fill', 'url(#' + uid + 'c' + i + ')');
    if (rm) { b0.setAttribute('fill', b1.getAttribute('fill')); return; }
    b1.animate([{ transform: 'translateY(0)' }, { transform: 'translateY(206px)' }], { duration: 600, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1)' }).onfinish = function () {
      b0.setAttribute('fill', b1.getAttribute('fill'));
    };
    holder.animate([{ transform: 'rotate(0)' }, { transform: 'rotate(-4deg)' }, { transform: 'rotate(2deg)' }, { transform: 'none' }], { duration: 700, easing: 'ease-out' });
  }
  root.querySelectorAll('.u051-sw input').forEach(function (inp) {
    inp.name = uid + '-c';
    inp.addEventListener('change', function () { pick(Number(inp.value)); });
  });
  add.addEventListener('click', function () {
    n++;
    add.classList.add('done');
    clearTimeout(okT);
    okT = setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) add.classList.remove('done'); }, 1400);
    var land = function () {
      badge.textContent = n;
      cart.classList.add('has');
      cart.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart, ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' item' : ' items'));
      if (!rm) {
        cart.animate([{ transform: 'none' }, { transform: 'rotate(-12deg) scale(1.12)' }, { transform: 'rotate(8deg)' }, { transform: 'none' }], { duration: 500, easing: 'ease-out' });
        badge.animate([{ transform: 'scale(1)' }, { transform: 'scale(1.45)' }, { transform: 'scale(1)' }], { duration: 400, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.34,1.5,.64,1)' });
      }
    };
    if (rm) { land(); return; }
    var fly = document.createElement('div');
    fly.className = 'u051-fly';
    fly.innerHTML = bottle(uid + 'f' + n);
    var fr = fly.querySelector('.b0'); fr.setAttribute('fill', 'url(#' + uid + 'c' + cur + ')');
    stage.appendChild(fly);
    var sx = holder.offsetLeft - 17, sy = holder.offsetTop + 70;
    var ex = cart.parentNode.offsetLeft + cart.offsetLeft + 22 - 17, ey = cart.parentNode.offsetTop + cart.offsetTop + 22 - 32;
    var kf = [];
    for (var k = 0; k <= 12; k++) {
      var t = k / 12, x = sx + (ex - sx) * t, y = sy + (ey - sy) * t - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 36;
      kf.push({ transform: 'translate(' + x.toFixed(1) + 'px,' + y.toFixed(1) + 'px) rotate(' + (t * 40) + 'deg) scale(' + (1.2 - t * 0.85).toFixed(2) + ')', opacity: t > 0.92 ? 0 : 1 });
    }
    fly.animate(kf, { duration: 720, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.3,1)' }).onfinish = function () { fly.remove(); land(); };
  });
})();
</script>

<!--
Element: Cart drawer with free-shipping bar
ID: 240 · Category: Commerce & Pricing · Tags: cart, drawer, free-shipping, ecommerce, quantity
What it does: Press Add to cart on a product page and the cart drawer slides in from the right, with the item you just added flashing. The bar at the top is filled toward free shipping, and raising a quantity rolls the subtotal while the bar fills completely and turns into a free-shipping message. Removing an item slides its row out and folds it away, and pressing outside or close tucks the drawer back in.
How to use it: Press Add to cart, then raise a quantity or remove an item
Footprint: fixed size, tall
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#240
-->

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var add = root.querySelector('.u240-add'), ov = root.querySelector('.u240-ov'), dr = root.querySelector('.u240-dr'), x = root.querySelector('.u240-x'), ls = root.querySelector('.u240-ls'), ct = root.querySelector('.u240-ct'), fs = root.querySelector('.u240-fs'), fm = root.querySelector('.u240-fm'), bar = root.querySelector('.u240-bar i'), tt = root.querySelector('.u240-tt');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var IT = [['Linen shirt', 'Ivory · L', 39, 1, '#E7E5E4'], ['Half pants', 'Navy · M', 32, 1, '#BFDBFE'], ['Bucket hat', 'Beige · One size', 19, 1, '#FDE68A']];
  var FREE = 100;
  function won(n) { return '$' + n.toLocaleString('en-US'); }
  var items = [];
  function row(d, flash) {
    var it = { d: d, q: d[3], el: document.createElement('div') };
    it.el.className = 'u240-it' + (flash ? ' is-fl' : '');
    it.el.innerHTML = '<div><div class="u240-in"><span class="u240-th" style="background:linear-gradient(150deg,' + d[4] + ',color-mix(in srgb,' + d[4] + ' 70%,#000))"></span><div class="u240-tx"><b></b><small></small><div class="u240-qt"><button type="button" class="u240-mn">−</button><span></span><button type="button" class="u240-pl">+</button></div></div><div class="u240-pr"><span class="u240-lp"></span><button type="button" class="u240-rm"><svg viewBox="0 0 12 12" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2.5 3.5h7M4.8 3.5V2.3h2.4v1.2M3.5 3.5l.5 6.2h4l.5-6.2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.3" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg></button></div></div></div>';
    it.el.querySelector('b').textContent = d[0]; it.el.querySelector('small').textContent = d[1];
    it.el.querySelector('.u240-mn').setAttribute('aria-label', 'Decrease quantity'); it.el.querySelector('.u240-pl').setAttribute('aria-label', 'Increase quantity'); it.el.querySelector('.u240-rm').setAttribute('aria-label', d[0] + ' remove');
    it.el.querySelector('.u240-mn').addEventListener('click', function () { if (it.q > 1) { it.q--; sum(); } });
    it.el.querySelector('.u240-pl').addEventListener('click', function () { if (it.q < 9) { it.q++; sum(); } });
    it.el.querySelector('.u240-rm').addEventListener('click', function () { items.splice(items.indexOf(it), 1); it.el.classList.add('is-gone'); setTimeout(function () { it.el.remove(); }, rm ? 0 : 600); sum(); });
    return it;
  }
  var cells = [];
  function digits(s) {
    while (cells.length < s.length) { var d = document.createElement('span'); d.className = 'u240-dg'; d.innerHTML = '<span></span>'; tt.appendChild(d); cells.push({ el: d, ch: null }); }
    while (cells.length > s.length) cells.pop().el.remove();
    s.split('').forEach(function (ch, i) {
      var c = cells[i]; if (c.ch === ch) return; var inner = c.el.firstChild;
      if (/\d/.test(ch)) { if (c.ch === null || !/\d/.test(c.ch)) inner.innerHTML = '0<br>1<br>2<br>3<br>4<br>5<br>6<br>7<br>8<br>9'; inner.style.transform = 'translateY(' + (-+ch * 1.2) + 'em)'; } else { inner.textContent = ch; inner.style.transform = 'none'; }
      c.ch = ch;
    });
    tt.setAttribute('aria-label', s);
  }
  function sum() {
    var s = 0, n = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) { s += it.d[2] * it.q; n += it.q; it.el.querySelector('.u240-qt span').textContent = it.q; it.el.querySelector('.u240-lp').textContent = won(it.d[2] * it.q); });
    ct.textContent = n; digits(won(s));
    var ok = s >= FREE; fs.classList.toggle('is-ok', ok); bar.style.width = Math.min(100, s / FREE * 100) + '%';

  }

(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sc = root.querySelector('.u221-sc'), mb = root.querySelector('.u221-mb'), by = root.querySelector('.u221-by'), op = root.querySelector('.u221-op'), ss = root.querySelector('.u221-ss'), ad = root.querySelector('.u221-ad'), adl = ad.querySelector('span'), x = root.querySelector('.u221-x'), ov = root.querySelector('.u221-ov'), br = root.querySelector('.u221-br');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var SZ = [['250', 0], ['260', 0], ['270', 0], ['280', 1]], size = -1, sheet = false, gone = false, T = 0;
  var B = SZ.map(function (z, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u221-sz'; b.setAttribute('role', 'radio'); b.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false'); b.textContent = z[0];
    if (z[1]) { b.disabled = true; var s = document.createElement('small'); s.textContent = 'Sold out'; b.appendChild(s); }
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { size = i; B.forEach(function (o, k) { o.setAttribute('aria-checked', k === i); }); sync(); });
    ss.appendChild(b); return b;
  });
  function sync() { ad.disabled = size < 0; if (!ad.classList.contains('is-ok')) adl.textContent = size < 0 ? 'Choose a size' : 'Add to cart'; }
  function bar() { root.classList.toggle('is-bar', gone || sheet); }
  function setSheet(v) {
    sheet = v; root.classList.toggle('is-sheet', v); op.setAttribute('aria-hidden', !v); bar();
    if (v) setTimeout(function () { (B[size >= 0 ? size : 0]).focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, rm ? 0 : 320);
  }
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { gone = !es[0].isIntersecting; bar(); }, { root: sc }).observe(mb);
  by.addEventListener('click', function () { setSheet(true); });
  mb.addEventListener('click', function () { setSheet(true); });
  x.addEventListener('click', function () { setSheet(false); });
  ov.addEventListener('click', function () { setSheet(false); });
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && sheet) setSheet(false); });
  ad.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (size < 0) return;
    ad.classList.add('is-ok'); adl.textContent = 'Added'; clearTimeout(T);
    T = setTimeout(function () { setSheet(false); setTimeout(function () { ad.classList.remove('is-ok'); size = -1; B.forEach(function (o) { o.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false'); }); sync(); }, 450); }, rm ? 600 : 1100);
  });
  sync();
})();
</script>

  var SHUT = 170, open = false, uid = 'u288' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  dt.id = uid; mr.setAttribute('aria-controls', uid);
  function sync() { lb.textContent = open ? 'Show less' : 'Show more details'; mr.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open); root.classList.toggle('is-open', open); }
  function scrollTo(y, ms) {
    if (rm) { sc.scrollTop = y; return; }
    var y0 = sc.scrollTop, t0 = performance.now();
    (function f(now) { var k = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / ms), e = k < .5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2; sc.scrollTop = y0 + (y - y0) * e; if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f); })(t0);
  }
  mr.addEventListener('click', function () {
    open = !open; sync();
    if (open) { dt.style.height = inn.offsetHeight + 'px'; return; }
    dt.style.height = SHUT + 'px';
    scrollTo(Math.max(0, st.offsetTop - 12), 650);
  });
  sync();
})();
</script>

  var PR = 10, LO = 2, HI = 12;
  var IT = [[0, 4.9, 'SML'], [1, 3.9, 'SM'], [2, 5.9, 'ML'], [3, 6.9, 'SML'], [0, 8.9, 'M'], [1, 2.9, 'SML'], [2, 10.9, 'SM'], [3, 3.4, 'L'], [1, 7.4, 'ML'], [0, 2.5, 'S']];
  function won(v) { return '$' + Math.round(v * PR).toLocaleString('ko-KR'); }
  var cards = IT.map(function (p, i) {
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'u305-it';
    d.innerHTML = '<i style="background:linear-gradient(160deg,' + CC[p[0]] + ',color-mix(in srgb,' + CC[p[0]] + ' 70%,#000))"></i><b></b><small></small>';
    d.querySelector('b').textContent = CN[p[0]] + ' · ' + p[2].split('').join('/'); d.querySelector('small').textContent = won(p[1]);
    gd.appendChild(d); return d;
  });
  var f = { lo: LO, hi: HI, c: [], s: [] }, applied = { lo: LO, hi: HI, c: [], s: [] };
  var SW = CN.map(function (n, i) { var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u305-sw'; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'); b.innerHTML = '<i style="background:' + CC[i] + '"></i>'; b.appendChild(document.createTextNode(n)); b.addEventListener('click', function () { tog(f.c, i); sync(); }); cs.appendChild(b); return b; });
  var ZB = SZ.map(function (z, i) { var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u305-sz'; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'); b.textContent = z; b.addEventListener('click', function () { tog(f.s, i); sync(); }); zs.appendChild(b); return b; });
  function tog(a, i) { var k = a.indexOf(i); if (k >= 0) a.splice(k, 1); else a.push(i); }
  function match(p, q) { return p[1] >= q.lo - 1e-6 && p[1] <= q.hi + 1e-6 && (!q.c.length || q.c.indexOf(p[0]) >= 0) && (!q.s.length || q.s.some(function (s) { return p[2].indexOf(SZ[s]) >= 0; })); }
  var lastN = -1;
  function sync() {
    var n = IT.filter(function (p) { return match(p, f); }).length;
    SW.forEach(function (b, i) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', f.c.indexOf(i) >= 0); }); ZB.forEach(function (b, i) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', f.s.indexOf(i) >= 0); });
    var a = (f.lo - LO) / (HI - LO) * 100, z = (f.hi - LO) / (HI - LO) * 100;
    mn.style.left = a + '%'; mx.style.left = z + '%'; rg.style.left = a + '%'; rg.style.width = (z - a) + '%';
    mn.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', won(f.lo)); mx.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', won(f.hi)); pl.textContent = won(f.lo) + ' – ' + won(f.hi);
    ap.disabled = !n; ap.innerHTML = '';
    if (n) { ap.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Show ')); var b = document.createElement('b'); b.textContent = n; if (n !== lastN && lastN >= 0) b.className = 'is-roll'; ap.appendChild(b); ap.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' items')); } else ap.textContent = 'No matching items';
    lastN = n;
  }
  function apply() {
    applied = { lo: f.lo, hi: f.hi, c: f.c.slice(), s: f.s.slice() };
    var n = 0;
    cards.forEach(function (d, i) { var ok = match(IT[i], applied); d.classList.toggle('is-out', !ok); d.classList.remove('is-pop'); if (ok) { void d.offsetWidth; d.classList.add('is-pop'); d.style.animationDelay = (n * 0.05) + 's'; n++; } });
    ct.textContent = n + '';
    ac.innerHTML = ''; var chips = [];
    applied.c.forEach(function (i) { chips.push([CN[i], function () { tog(applied.c, i); }]); });
    applied.s.forEach(function (i) { chips.push([SZ[i], function () { tog(applied.s, i); }]); });
    if (applied.lo > LO || applied.hi < HI) chips.push([won(applied.lo) + '–' + won(applied.hi), function () { applied.lo = LO; applied.hi = HI; }]);
    chips.forEach(function (c) { var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.setAttribute('aria-label', c[0] + ' — remove filter'); b.appendChild(document.createTextNode(c[0])); b.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M1.5 1.5l6 6M7.5 1.5l-6 6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>'); b.addEventListener('click', function () { c[1](); f = { lo: applied.lo, hi: applied.hi, c: applied.c.slice(), s: applied.s.slice() }; sync(); apply(); }); ac.appendChild(b); });
    bd.textContent = chips.length; bd.classList.toggle('is-on', chips.length > 0);
    gd.style.setProperty('--top', (tp.offsetHeight) + 'px');
  }
  function open(v) { root.classList.toggle('is-open', v); sh.setAttribute('aria-hidden', !v); fb.setAttribute('aria-expanded', v); if (v) { f = { lo: applied.lo, hi: applied.hi, c: applied.c.slice(), s: applied.s.slice() }; lastN = -1; sync(); } }
  function dragOn(th, key) {
    var on = false;
    th.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { on = true; try { th.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (er) {} });
    th.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
      if (!on) return; var r = tr.getBoundingClientRect(), v = LO + Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width)) * (HI - LO); v = Math.round(v * 2) / 2;
      if (key === 'lo') f.lo = Math.min(v, f.hi - 1); else f.hi = Math.max(v, f.lo + 1); sync();
    });
    th.addEventListener('pointerup', function () { on = false; }); th.addEventListener('pointercancel', function () { on = false; });
    th.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { var d = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? .5 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -.5 : 0; if (!d) return; e.preventDefault(); if (key === 'lo') f.lo = Math.max(LO, Math.min(f.lo + d, f.hi - 1)); else f.hi = Math.min(HI, Math.max(f.hi + d, f.lo + 1)); sync(); });
  }
  dragOn(mn, 'lo'); dragOn(mx, 'hi');
  fb.addEventListener('click', function () { open(true); });
  ov.addEventListener('click', function () { open(false); });
  rs.addEventListener('click', function () { f = { lo: LO, hi: HI, c: [], s: [] }; sync(); });
  ap.addEventListener('click', function () { if (ap.disabled) return; apply(); open(false); fb.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && root.classList.contains('is-open')) open(false); });
  apply(); sync();
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 19 / 75 - ID 024 - Price histogram range filter

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #024 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#024 -->
<!--
Element: Price histogram range filter
ID: 024 · Category: Toggles & Pickers · Tags: range, dual-thumb, price, filter, histogram
What it does: Choose a price range with two handles: bars outside the range sink while only the bars inside rise in color. The result count rolls over and the price at the top updates with it.
How to use it: Drag both handles to narrow the range. Arrow keys work too
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#024
-->
<style>
.u024{position:relative;width:340px;padding:4px 8px}
.u024-head{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px}
.u024-head small{display:block;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ux-ink-3)}
.u024-val{display:block;margin-top:4px;font-size:22px;font-weight:780;letter-spacing:-.035em;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.u024-count{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line);font-size:13px;font-weight:650;color:var(--ux-ink-2)}
.u024-roll{position:relative;width:3ch;height:18px;overflow:hidden;text-align:right;color:var(--ux-ink);font-weight:760;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.u024-roll span{display:block;line-height:18px;transition:transform .45s var(--ux-spring),opacity .3s var(--ux-ease)}
.u024-roll span+span{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;top:0}
.u024-hist{position:relative;height:110px;margin:22px 4px 0;display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:4px}
.u024-bar{flex:1;height:calc(var(--h) * 1%);border-radius:6px 6px 3px 3px;background:var(--ux-surface-3);transform-origin:50% 100%;transform:scaleY(.82);transition:background-color .35s var(--ux-ease),transform .55s var(--ux-spring),opacity .35s var(--ux-ease)}
.u024-bar.in{background:linear-gradient(180deg,#2FD8A8,#14B8A6 60%,#0E9F8E);transform:none}
.u024-track{position:relative;height:36px;margin:0 4px}
.u024-rail{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;top:50%;height:6px;margin-top:-3px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface-3)}
.u024-sel{position:absolute;top:50%;height:6px;margin-top:-3px;border-radius:999px;background:#14B8A6;left:calc(var(--a) * 1%);right:calc(100% - var(--b) * 1%)}
.u024-h{position:absolute;top:50%;left:calc(var(--pos) * 1%);width:30px;height:30px;margin:-15px 0 0 -15px;border:0;padding:0;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;cursor:grab;touch-action:none;z-index:2;
  box-shadow:0 0 0 2.5px #14B8A6,0 6px 14px -4px rgba(0,60,50,.4);transition:transform .35s var(--ux-spring),box-shadow .3s var(--ux-ease)}
.u024-h::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:10px;border-radius:50%;background:#14B8A6;transform:scale(.6);transition:transform .35s var(--ux-spring)}
.u024-h:hover{transform:scale(1.08)}
.u024-h.drag{cursor:grabbing;transform:scale(1.18);box-shadow:0 0 0 2.5px #14B8A6,0 0 0 10px rgba(20,184,166,.18),0 6px 14px -4px rgba(0,60,50,.4)}

.u024-h:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:4px}

</style>
<div class="ux u024">
  <div class="u024-head">
    <div><small>Price range</small><span class="u024-val">$53 – $120</span></div>
    <span class="u024-count" aria-live="polite"><span class="u024-roll"><span>48</span><span></span></span> products</span>
  </div>
  <div class="u024-hist" aria-hidden="true"></div>
  <div class="u024-track">
    <span class="u024-rail" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    <span class="u024-sel" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    <button class="u024-h" type="button" role="slider" aria-label="Minimum price" data-k="lo" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"></button>
    <button class="u024-h" type="button" role="slider" aria-label="Maximum price" data-k="hi" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"></button>
  </div>
  <div class="u024-scale" aria-hidden="true"><span>$30</span><span>$105</span><span>$180</span></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var track = root.querySelector('.u024-track');
  var hist = root.querySelector('.u024-hist');
  var val = root.querySelector('.u024-val');
  var roll = root.querySelector('.u024-roll');
  var handles = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u024-h'));
  var STEP = 2;
  var v = { lo: 16, hi: 60 };
  var COUNTS = [2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 9, 11, 14, 16, 15, 13, 12, 10, 11, 9, 8, 6, 5, 6, 4, 3, 3, 2, 1];
  var MAXC = 16;
  var bars = COUNTS.map(function (c) {
    var b = document.createElement('span');
    b.className = 'u024-bar';
    b.style.setProperty('--h', Math.max(8, c / MAXC * 100).toFixed(1));
    hist.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  var won = function (p) { return Math.round((30000 + p / 100 * 150000) / 1000) * 1000; };
  var fmt = function (n) { return '$' + Math.round(n / 1000); };
  var ci = 0, shown = '';
  function setCount(n) {
    if (String(n) === shown) return;
    var up = !shown || n > +shown;
    shown = String(n);
    var cur = roll.children[ci], nxt = roll.children[1 - ci];
    nxt.style.transition = 'none';
    nxt.textContent = n;
    nxt.style.transform = 'translateY(' + (up ? 100 : -100) + '%)'; nxt.style.opacity = '0';
    void nxt.offsetWidth;
    nxt.style.transition = '';
    nxt.style.transform = 'none'; nxt.style.opacity = '1';
    cur.style.transform = 'translateY(' + (up ? -100 : 100) + '%)'; cur.style.opacity = '0';
    ci = 1 - ci;
  }
  function paint() {
    handles[0].style.setProperty('--pos', v.lo);
    handles[1].style.setProperty('--pos', v.hi);
    root.style.setProperty('--a', v.lo);
    root.style.setProperty('--b', v.hi);
    [['lo', 0], ['hi', 1]].forEach(function (x) {
      handles[x[1]].setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String(v[x[0]]));
      handles[x[1]].setAttribute('aria-valuetext', '$' + Math.round(won(v[x[0]]) / 1000));
    });
    val.textContent = fmt(won(v.lo)) + ' – ' + fmt(won(v.hi));
    var n = 0;
    bars.forEach(function (b, i) {
      var c = (i + 0.5) / bars.length * 100;
      var inside = c >= v.lo && c <= v.hi;
      b.classList.toggle('in', inside);
      if (inside) n += COUNTS[i];
    });
    setCount(n);
  }
  function posFromX(x) {
    var r = track.getBoundingClientRect();
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((x - r.left) / r.width * 100 / STEP) * STEP));
  }
  function drag(h, e) {
    var p = posFromX(e.clientX);
    if (h.dataset.k === 'lo') v.lo = Math.min(p, v.hi - STEP * 3); else v.hi = Math.max(p, v.lo + STEP * 3);
    paint();
  }
  handles.forEach(function (h) {
    h.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { h.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); h.classList.add('drag'); drag(h, e); });
    h.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (h.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)) drag(h, e); });
    h.addEventListener('pointerup', function () { h.classList.remove('drag'); });
    h.addEventListener('pointercancel', function () { h.classList.remove('drag'); });
    h.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      var d = (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') ? STEP : (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') ? -STEP : 0;
      if (!d) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      if (h.dataset.k === 'lo') v.lo = Math.max(0, Math.min(v.lo + d, v.hi - STEP * 3));
      else v.hi = Math.min(100, Math.max(v.hi + d, v.lo + STEP * 3));
      paint();
    });
  });
  paint();
})();
</script>

Element: Product color picker
ID: 020 · Category: Toggles & Pickers · Tags: swatch, color, product, radio, liquid
What it does: Pick a color and it fills the tumbler from the bottom while the glow behind takes on the same color. The selection ring glides to your pick and the color name rolls over.
How to use it: Press the color dots below. Arrow keys work too
Footprint: fixed size, square

<style>
.u020{--c:#2E3A59;--n:#2E3A59;position:relative;width:320px;height:350px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center}
.u020-stage{position:relative;width:320px;height:232px;display:grid;place-items:center}
.u020-glow{position:absolute;left:50%;top:52%;width:200px;height:200px;margin:-100px 0 0 -100px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--c);filter:blur(42px);opacity:.38;transition:background-color .8s var(--ux-ease)}

.u020-lid::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:30%;right:30%;top:-7px;height:9px;border-radius:5px 5px 0 0;background:#3A3A42}
