<div class="ux u020">
  <div class="u020-stage" aria-hidden="true">
    <span class="u020-glow"></span>
    <span class="u020-shadow"></span>
    <div class="u020-cup">
      <span class="u020-lid"></span>
      <span class="u020-body"><span class="u020-new"></span><span class="u020-mark">LUCIAN</span><span class="u020-shine"></span></span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <p class="u020-name" aria-live="polite"><span>Midnight<small>Stainless tumbler</small></span></p>
  <div class="u020-row" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Color">
    <span class="u020-ring" aria-hidden="true"></span>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var row = root.querySelector('.u020-row');
  var ring = root.querySelector('.u020-ring');
  var layer = root.querySelector('.u020-new');
  var name = root.querySelector('.u020-name');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var COLORS = [['Midnight', '#2E3A59'], ['Sage', '#7FA38A'], ['Coral', '#F07C6A'], ['Lavender', '#A796E8'], ['Butter', '#F2C86B'], ['Cloud', '#E9E6E0']];
  var cur = 0, busy = 0;
  var sws = COLORS.map(function (c, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button';
    b.className = 'u020-sw';
    b.style.setProperty('--s', c[1]);
    b.setAttribute('role', 'radio');
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', c[0]);
    b.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(i === 0));
    b.tabIndex = i === 0 ? 0 : -1;
    row.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  function moveRing(i) { ring.style.transform = 'translate(' + (sws[i].offsetLeft - 6) + 'px,' + (sws[i].offsetTop - 6) + 'px)'; }
  function rollName(i) {
    var old = name.lastElementChild;
    var s = document.createElement('span');
    s.innerHTML = COLORS[i][0] + '<small>Stainless tumbler</small>';
    s.style.transform = 'translateY(100%)'; s.style.opacity = '0';
    name.appendChild(s);
    void s.offsetWidth;
    s.style.transform = 'none'; s.style.opacity = '1';
    old.style.transform = 'translateY(-100%)'; old.style.opacity = '0';
    setTimeout(function () { old.remove(); }, 500);
  }
  function choose(i, focus) {
    if (i === cur) return;
    cur = i;
    sws.forEach(function (b, k) { b.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(k === i)); b.tabIndex = k === i ? 0 : -1; });
    if (focus) sws[i].focus();
    moveRing(i);
    rollName(i);
    root.style.setProperty('--n', COLORS[i][1]);
    root.querySelector('.u020-glow').style.backgroundColor = COLORS[i][1];
    clearTimeout(busy);
    layer.classList.remove('go'); void layer.offsetWidth;
    if (rm) { root.style.setProperty('--c', COLORS[i][1]); return; }
    layer.classList.add('go');
    busy = setTimeout(function () {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      root.style.setProperty('--c', COLORS[i][1]);
      layer.classList.remove('go');
    }, 720);
  }
  sws.forEach(function (b, i) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { choose(i); });
    b.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      var d = (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') ? 1 : (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') ? -1 : 0;
      if (d) { e.preventDefault(); choose((i + d + sws.length) % sws.length, true); }
    });
  });
  ring.style.transition = 'none';
  moveRing(0);
  requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(function () { ring.style.transition = ''; }); });
})();
</script>


##############################################################################
NORDA COMPONENT 21 / 75 - ID 018 - Rolling quantity stepper
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #018 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#018 -->
<!--
Element: Rolling quantity stepper
ID: 018 · Category: Toggles & Pickers · Tags: stepper, quantity, odometer, counter, cart
What it does: Press plus or minus and the number rolls one notch like an odometer. The total counts along smoothly, and at the minimum or maximum the stepper shakes its head.
How to use it: Press + and −. It will not go below 1 or above 12
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#018
-->
<style>
.u018{position:relative;width:320px;height:240px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:22px}
.u018-item{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;width:280px}
.u018-th{width:56px;height:56px;flex:none;border-radius:16px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:linear-gradient(150deg,#3A2A22,#7A4B2E 60%,#C98A55);box-shadow:inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.2)}
.u018-th svg{width:26px;height:26px;fill:none;stroke:#FFE7C7;stroke-width:1.8;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}
.u018-item b{display:block;font-size:16px;font-weight:720;letter-spacing:-.02em}
.u018-item small{display:block;margin-top:3px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ux-ink-3);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.u018-row{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;width:280px}
.u018-step{display:flex;align-items:center;height:60px;padding:6px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 16px 30px -22px var(--ux-shadow-strong)}
.u018-step.is-shake{animation:u018-no .42s var(--ux-ease)}
@keyframes u018-no{20%{transform:translateX(-6px)}40%{transform:translateX(5px)}60%{transform:translateX(-3px)}80%{transform:translateX(2px)}}
.u018-b{width:48px;height:48px;border:0;padding:0;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ux-surface-2);color:var(--ux-ink);display:grid;place-items:center;transition:transform .4s var(--ux-spring),background-color .25s var(--ux-ease),opacity .3s var(--ux-ease)}
.u018-b:hover{background:var(--ux-surface-3)}
.u018-b:active{transform:scale(.86)}
.u018-b:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:2px}
.u018-b[aria-disabled="true"]{opacity:.35}
.u018-b svg{width:20px;height:20px;fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:2.4;stroke-linecap:round}
.u018-b.pl{background:var(--ux-ink);color:var(--ux-page)}
.u018-b.pl:hover{background:var(--ux-ink)}
.u018-num{display:flex;justify-content:center;width:56px;height:34px;overflow:hidden;font-size:26px;font-weight:780;line-height:34px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;letter-spacing:-.02em}
.u018-col{display:flex;flex-direction:column;transition:transform .55s var(--ux-spring),width .4s var(--ux-ease),opacity .3s var(--ux-ease)}
.u018-col span{height:34px;text-align:center}
.u018-col.is-hide{width:0!important;opacity:0}
.u018-sum{text-align:right}
.u018-sum small{display:block;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ux-ink-3)}
.u018-sum b{display:block;margin-top:2px;font-size:21px;font-weight:780;letter-spacing:-.03em;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.u018-sr{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip-path:inset(50%);white-space:nowrap}
</style>
<div class="ux u018">
  <div class="u018-item">
    <span class="u018-th" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M7 8h10l-1.2 11a2 2 0 0 1-2 1.8h-3.6a2 2 0 0 1-2-1.8z"/><path d="M6 8h12M9 8l.8-4h4.4l.8 4M10 12.5h4"/></svg></span>
    <div><b>Cold brew 500 ml</b><small>$12.90 · Ships today</small></div>
  </div>
  <div class="u018-row">
    <div class="u018-step" role="group" aria-label="Quantity">
      <button class="u018-b mi" type="button" aria-label="Remove one"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path d="M5 10h10"/></svg></button>
      <span class="u018-num" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"><span class="u018-sr"></span><span class="u018-col" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="u018-col" aria-hidden="true"></span></span>
      <button class="u018-b pl" type="button" aria-label="Add one"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path d="M10 5v10M5 10h10"/></svg></button>
    </div>
    <div class="u018-sum"><small>Total</small><b class="u018-total">$25.80</b></div>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var step = root.querySelector('.u018-step');
  var mi = root.querySelector('.mi'), pl = root.querySelector('.pl');
  var cols = root.querySelectorAll('.u018-col');
  var sr = root.querySelector('.u018-sr');
  var total = root.querySelector('.u018-total');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var PRICE = 12900, MIN = 1, MAX = 12;
  var q = 2, shown = q * PRICE, raf = 0;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(cols, function (c) {
    for (var d = 0; d < 10; d++) { var s = document.createElement('span'); s.textContent = d; c.appendChild(s); }
    c.style.width = '17px';
  });
  function countTo(target) {
    cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    var from = shown, t0 = performance.now(), D = rm ? 1 : 520;
    (function f(now) {
      var x = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / D);
      shown = Math.round(from + (target - from) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - x, 3)));
      total.textContent = '$' + (shown / 1000).toFixed(2);
      if (x < 1 && root.isConnected) raf = requestAnimationFrame(f);
    })(t0);
  }
  function render() {
    var tens = Math.floor(q / 10), ones = q % 10;
    cols[0].style.transform = 'translateY(' + (-tens * 34) + 'px)';
    cols[0].classList.toggle('is-hide', tens === 0);
    cols[1].style.transform = 'translateY(' + (-ones * 34) + 'px)';
    mi.setAttribute('aria-disabled', String(q <= MIN));
    pl.setAttribute('aria-disabled', String(q >= MAX));
    sr.textContent = q + (q === 1 ? ' item' : ' items');
    countTo(q * PRICE);
  }
  function bump(d) {
    var n = q + d;
    if (n < MIN || n > MAX) {
      step.classList.remove('is-shake'); void step.offsetWidth; step.classList.add('is-shake');
      return;
    }
    q = n;
    render();
  }
  mi.addEventListener('click', function () { bump(-1); });
  pl.addEventListener('click', function () { bump(1); });
  step.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); bump(1); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); bump(-1); }
  });
  render();
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 22 / 75 - ID 261 - Review filter by star bars

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #261 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#261 -->
<!--
Element: Review filter by star bars
ID: 261 · Category: Social Proof · Tags: reviews, rating, histogram, filter, ecommerce
What it does: In a product review summary, the average rating fills the stars down to the decimal, and bars for 5 down to 1 star grow to their share. Press a bar and only that row stays bold while the review list below switches to reviews with that rating; press it again to return to all reviews. Each review's Helpful button adds one when pressed.
How to use it: Press a star bar to filter reviews, and try Helpful too
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#261
-->
<style>
.u261{position:relative;width:340px;height:340px;border-radius:30px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 30px 60px -36px var(--ux-shadow-strong);color:var(--ux-ink)}
.u261-tp{position:absolute;left:18px;right:18px;top:18px;display:flex;gap:16px;align-items:center}
.u261-av{flex:none;width:96px;text-align:center}
.u261-av b{display:block;font-size:36px;line-height:1;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:-.04em;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}

.u261-av small{display:block;margin-top:5px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ux-ink-3)}

.u261-br:hover,.u261-br.g-hov{background:var(--ux-surface-2)}
.u261-br:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:1px}

.u261-br>span:last-child{width:30px;text-align:right;color:var(--ux-ink-3);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}

.u261.is-f .u261-br.is-on{opacity:1;background:var(--ux-surface-2)}

.u261-hd span{margin-left:6px;font-size:10px;color:var(--ux-ink-3);font-weight:750}
.u261-ls{position:absolute;left:14px;right:14px;top:156px;bottom:12px;overflow:hidden}
.u261-rv{padding:10px 11px;margin-bottom:7px;border-radius:13px;background:var(--ux-surface-2);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line);animation:u261-in .4s cubic-bezier(.3,1.2,.5,1) backwards}
@keyframes u261-in{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px)}}
.u261-rv header{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:var(--ux-ink-3)}
.u261-rv header i{font-style:normal;color:#F59E0B;letter-spacing:-.5px}
.u261-rv p{margin:4px 0 0;font-size:11px;line-height:1.4;font-weight:650}
.u261-hp{margin-left:auto;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;height:22px;padding:0 8px;border:0;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line);color:var(--ux-ink-2);font:inherit;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;transition:background .2s,color .2s}
.u261-hp[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--ux-ink);color:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:none}
.u261-hp svg{width:10px;height:10px;transition:transform .35s cubic-bezier(.3,1.8,.5,1)}

<div class="ux u261">
  <div class="u261-tp"><div class="u261-av"><b>4.6</b><span class="u261-st" aria-hidden="true">★★★★★<i>★★★★★</i></span><small>1,248 reviews</small></div><div class="u261-bs"></div></div>
  <div class="u261-hd"><span class="u261-tt"></span></div>
  <div class="u261-ls" aria-live="polite"></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var bs = root.querySelector('.u261-bs'), ls = root.querySelector('.u261-ls'), tt = root.querySelector('.u261-tt'), star = root.querySelector('.u261-st i');
  var N = [902, 228, 62, 32, 24], TOTAL = 1248;
  var RV = [[5, 'K.', 'My second order. The color matches the photos exactly.'], [5, 'L.', 'Lighter than expected and very breathable.'], [4, 'P.', 'Runs a little large. Size down one.'], [4, 'C.', 'Fast shipping and neat packaging.'], [3, 'J.', 'Fine, but it wrinkles a bit easily.'], [2, 'H.', 'Shrank slightly after washing.'], [1, 'Y.', 'Arrived with a missing button.']];
  var help = RV.map(function (r, i) { return [12, 8, 21, 3, 5, 9, 14][i]; }), mine = RV.map(function () { return false; });
  var B = N.map(function (n, i) {
    var s = 5 - i, b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u261-br'; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    b.innerHTML = '<span></span><em><i></i></em><span></span>';
    b.firstChild.textContent = s + '★'; b.lastChild.textContent = Math.round(n / TOTAL * 100) + '%';
    b.querySelector('i').style.transitionDelay = (0.15 + i * 0.07) + 's';
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { pick(cur === s ? 0 : s); });
    bs.appendChild(b); return b;
  });
  var cur = -1;
  function stars(n) { return '★★★★★'.slice(0, n) + '☆☆☆☆☆'.slice(0, 5 - n); }
  function pick(s) {
    if (s === cur) return;
    cur = s; root.classList.toggle('is-f', s > 0);
    B.forEach(function (b, i) { var on = 5 - i === s; b.classList.toggle('is-on', on); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on); });
    tt.textContent = s ? s + '-star reviews' : 'All reviews';
    ls.innerHTML = '';
    RV.forEach(function (r, i) {
      if (s && r[0] !== s) return;
      var d = document.createElement('article'); d.className = 'u261-rv'; d.style.animationDelay = ls.children.length * 0.06 + 's';
      d.innerHTML = '<header><i aria-label="' + r[0] + '/5">' + stars(r[0]) + '</i><span></span><button type="button" class="u261-hp"><svg viewBox="0 0 10 10" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M1.5 4.5h1.6v4H1.5zM3.1 8.5h3.8l1.5-3.4-.4-.8H5.6l.4-2.2-.6-.6L3.1 4.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.1" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg><span></span></button></header><p></p>';
      d.querySelector('header>span').textContent = r[1]; d.querySelector('p').textContent = r[2];
      var hb = d.querySelector('.u261-hp');
      function hs() { hb.setAttribute('aria-pressed', mine[i]); hb.lastChild.textContent = 'Helpful ' + (help[i] + (mine[i] ? 1 : 0)); }
      hb.addEventListener('click', function () { mine[i] = !mine[i]; hs(); });
      hs(); ls.appendChild(d);
    });
  }
  function fill() { star.style.width = (4.6 / 5 * 100) + '%'; B.forEach(function (b, i) { b.querySelector('i').style.transform = 'scaleX(' + (N[i] / N[0]).toFixed(3) + ')'; }); }
  pick(0);
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) { var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { if (es[0].isIntersecting) { io.disconnect(); requestAnimationFrame(fill); } }); io.observe(root); } else fill();
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 23 / 75 - ID 016 - Star rating
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #016 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#016 -->
<!--
Element: Star rating
ID: 016 · Category: Toggles & Pickers · Tags: rating, stars, hover, feedback, radio
What it does: Stars fill in ahead of time as far as the cursor goes, and when you press they pop up one after another from the left with a sparkle. A matching word and color change with the score.
How to use it: Move over the stars and press to rate. Arrow keys work too
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#016
-->
<style>
.u016{--gold:linear-gradient(160deg,#FFD66B,#FFAA2C 60%,#FF7A45);position:relative;width:330px;height:220px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:16px}
.u016-q{margin:0;font-size:15px;font-weight:650;color:var(--ux-ink-2)}
.u016-stars{display:flex;gap:6px;border-radius:16px}
.u016-stars:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:6px}
.u016-s{position:relative;width:52px;height:52px;padding:0;border:0;background:none;transition:transform .35s var(--ux-spring)}
.u016-s svg{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;overflow:visible}
.u016-s .bg{fill:var(--ux-surface-3);stroke:var(--ux-line-2);stroke-width:1}
.u016-s .fg{fill:url(#u016-g);transform-origin:50% 55%;transform:scale(0);transition:transform .4s var(--ux-spring)}
.u016-s.is-pre .fg{transform:scale(.72);opacity:.55}
.u016-s.is-on .fg{transform:scale(1);opacity:1}
.u016-s:hover{transform:scale(1.12) rotate(-6deg)}
.u016-s.is-pop{animation:u016-pop .55s var(--ux-spring)}
@keyframes u016-pop{0%{transform:scale(1)}35%{transform:scale(1.32) rotate(12deg)}100%{transform:scale(1)}}
.u016-s i{position:absolute;left:50%;top:50%;width:6px;height:6px;margin:-3px;border-radius:50%;background:#FFB547;opacity:0;pointer-events:none}
.u016-s.is-pop i{animation:u016-spark .6s var(--ux-ease) forwards}
@keyframes u016-spark{0%{opacity:1;transform:rotate(var(--a)) translateY(-14px) scale(1)}100%{opacity:0;transform:rotate(var(--a)) translateY(-40px) scale(.3)}}
.u016-out{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:10px;height:40px}
.u016-score{font-size:34px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.04em;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;transition:color .4s var(--ux-ease)}
.u016-word{position:relative;height:24px;width:132px;overflow:hidden;font-size:16px;font-weight:700}
.u016-word span{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;line-height:24px;white-space:nowrap;transition:transform .5s var(--ux-spring),opacity .3s var(--ux-ease)}
</style>
<div class="ux u016">
  <p class="u016-q">How was your order?</p>
  <div class="u016-stars" role="slider" tabindex="0" aria-label="Rating" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="5" aria-valuenow="0" aria-valuetext="Not rated">
    <svg width="0" height="0" style="position:absolute" aria-hidden="true"><defs><linearGradient id="u016-g" x1="0" y1="0" x2=".6" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#FFD66B"/><stop offset=".6" stop-color="#FFAA2C"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#FF7A45"/></linearGradient></defs></svg>
  </div>
  <div class="u016-out" aria-hidden="true"><span class="u016-score">0.0</span><span class="u016-word"><span>Tap a star to rate</span></span></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var wrap = root.querySelector('.u016-stars');
  var score = root.querySelector('.u016-score');
  var word = root.querySelector('.u016-word');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var gid = 'u016-g-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  root.querySelector('linearGradient').id = gid;
  var WORDS = ['Tap a star to rate', 'Poor', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Great', 'Excellent'];
  var COLORS = ['var(--ux-ink-3)', '#E5484D', '#F07A3E', '#E8A317', '#2FB37A', '#7B61FF'];
  var STAR = 'M26 4.5l6.4 13.2 14.4 2-10.5 10.1 2.6 14.3L26 37.3l-12.9 6.8 2.6-14.3L5.2 19.7l14.4-2z';
  var stars = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    var b = document.createElement('span');
    b.className = 'u016-s';
    var sparks = '';
    for (var k = 0; k < 5; k++) sparks += '<i style="--a:' + (k * 72) + 'deg"></i>';
    b.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 52 52"><path class="bg" d="' + STAR + '" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path class="fg" d="' + STAR + '" style="fill:url(#' + gid + ')"/></svg>' + sparks;
    b.dataset.v = i + 1;
    wrap.appendChild(b);
    stars.push(b);
  }
  var val = 0;
  function preview(n) { stars.forEach(function (s, i) { s.classList.toggle('is-pre', i < n && i >= val); }); }
  function setWord(n) {
    var old = word.lastElementChild;
    var s = document.createElement('span');
    s.textContent = WORDS[n];
    s.style.color = COLORS[n];
    s.style.transform = 'translateY(100%)'; s.style.opacity = '0';
    word.appendChild(s);
    void s.offsetWidth;
    s.style.transform = 'none'; s.style.opacity = '1';
    old.style.transform = 'translateY(-100%)'; old.style.opacity = '0';
    setTimeout(function () { old.remove(); }, 500);
  }
  function set(n) {
    if (n === val) return;
    var prev = val;
    val = n;
    stars.forEach(function (s, i) {
      var on = i < n;
      s.classList.remove('is-pre');
      if (on && i >= prev) {
        setTimeout(function () {
          if (!root.isConnected) return;
          s.classList.add('is-on');
          if (!rm) { s.classList.remove('is-pop'); void s.offsetWidth; s.classList.add('is-pop'); }
        }, rm ? 0 : (i - prev) * 70);
      } else if (!on) s.classList.remove('is-on', 'is-pop');
    });
    score.textContent = n.toFixed(1);
    score.style.color = n ? COLORS[n] : '';
    setWord(n);
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String(n));
    wrap.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', n ? n + ' stars, ' + WORDS[n] : 'Not rated');
  }
  stars.forEach(function (s) {
    s.addEventListener('pointerenter', function () { preview(+s.dataset.v); });
    s.addEventListener('click', function () { set(+s.dataset.v); });
  });
  wrap.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { preview(0); });
  wrap.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var d = { ArrowRight: 1, ArrowUp: 1, ArrowLeft: -1, ArrowDown: -1 }[e.key];
    if (e.key === 'Home') { e.preventDefault(); set(0); }
    if (e.key === 'End') { e.preventDefault(); set(5); }
    if (d) { e.preventDefault(); set(Math.max(0, Math.min(5, val + d))); }
    if (/^[0-5]$/.test(e.key)) set(+e.key);
  });
})();
</script>

  <div class="u264-in-w">
    <h3>Create account</h3>
    <label>Email</label>
    <div class="u264-fd"><input class="u264-in" type="email" aria-label="Email" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="you@example.com"><svg class="u264-ck" viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="10" r="10"/><path d="M6 10.3l2.6 2.6L14 7.5"/></svg></div>

  </div>
  <div class="u264-ft"><small>We’ll send you a sign-in link</small><button type="button" class="u264-go" disabled>Continue</button></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var inp = root.querySelector('.u264-in'), dms = root.querySelector('.u264-dms'), fx = root.querySelector('.u264-fx'), sg = root.querySelector('.u264-sg'), go = root.querySelector('.u264-go');
  var DOM = ['gmail.com', 'naver.com', 'kakao.com', 'icloud.com', 'outlook.com', 'hanmail.net', 'daum.net', 'yahoo.com', 'hotmail.com'];
  DOM.slice(0, 4).forEach(function (d) { var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u264-dm'; b.textContent = '@' + d; b.addEventListener('click', function () { inp.value = inp.value.split('@')[0] + '@' + d; inp.focus({ preventScroll: true }); check(); }); dms.appendChild(b); });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(dms.children, function (b, i) { b.style.transitionDelay = i * 0.04 + 's'; });
  function dist(a, b) {
    var d = [], i, j;
    for (i = 0; i <= a.length; i++) { d[i] = [i]; }
    for (j = 0; j <= b.length; j++) d[0][j] = j;
    for (i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) for (j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) {
      d[i][j] = Math.min(d[i - 1][j] + 1, d[i][j - 1] + 1, d[i - 1][j - 1] + (a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1));
      if (i > 1 && j > 1 && a[i - 1] === b[j - 2] && a[i - 2] === b[j - 1]) d[i][j] = Math.min(d[i][j], d[i - 2][j - 2] + 1);
    }
    return d[a.length][b.length];
  }
  var fixTo = '';
  function check() {
    var v = inp.value.trim(), at = v.indexOf('@'), dom = at >= 0 ? v.slice(at + 1).toLowerCase() : '';
    root.classList.toggle('is-at', at > 0 && !dom);
    var best = null;
    if (/\.[a-z]{2,}$/.test(dom) && DOM.indexOf(dom) < 0) DOM.forEach(function (d) { var k = dist(dom, d); if (k <= 2 && (!best || k < best[1])) best = [d, k]; });
    fixTo = best ? v.slice(0, at + 1) + best[0] : '';
    root.classList.toggle('is-typo', !!best); fx.tabIndex = best ? 0 : -1;
    if (best) {
      sg.innerHTML = ''; var d = best[0], k = 0;
      while (k < d.length && d[k] === dom[k]) k++;
      var e = 0; while (e < d.length - k && d[d.length - 1 - e] === dom[dom.length - 1 - e]) e++;
      sg.appendChild(document.createTextNode(v.slice(0, at + 1) + d.slice(0, k))); var m = document.createElement('mark'); m.textContent = d.slice(k, d.length - e); sg.appendChild(m); sg.appendChild(document.createTextNode(d.slice(d.length - e)));
    }
    var ok = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[a-z]{2,}$/i.test(v) && !best;
    root.classList.toggle('is-ok', ok); go.disabled = !ok;
  }
  fx.addEventListener('click', function () { if (!fixTo) return; inp.value = fixTo; inp.classList.remove('is-fix'); void inp.offsetWidth; inp.classList.add('is-fix'); check(); inp.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
  inp.addEventListener('input', check);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var cc = root.querySelector('.u209-cc'), fl = cc.querySelector('em'), code = cc.querySelector('b'), inp = root.querySelector('.u209-in'), ht = root.querySelector('.u209-ht'), sr = root.querySelector('.u209-sr'), ls = root.querySelector('.u209-ls'), go = root.querySelector('.u209-go');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var C = [['KR', '🇰🇷', 'South Korea', '+82', [3, 4, 4], '010-1234-5678'], ['US', '🇺🇸', 'United States', '+1', [3, 3, 4], '(415) 555-0132'], ['JP', '🇯🇵', 'Japan', '+81', [3, 4, 4], '090-1234-5678'], ['GB', '🇬🇧', 'United Kingdom', '+44', [5, 6], '07700 900123']];
  var cur = 0;
  var O = C.map(function (c, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u209-op'; b.setAttribute('role', 'option');
    b.innerHTML = '<em aria-hidden="true">' + c[1] + '</em><span></span><small>' + c[3] + '</small>'; b.querySelector('span').textContent = c[2];
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { pick(i); setOpen(false); inp.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
    ls.appendChild(b); return b;
  });
  function fmt(d) {
    var g = C[cur][4], out = [], k = 0;
    g.forEach(function (n) { if (k < d.length) out.push(d.slice(k, k + n)); k += n; });
    if (k < d.length) out[out.length - 1] += d.slice(k);
    if (C[cur][0] === 'US' && d.length > 3) return '(' + out[0] + ') ' + out.slice(1).join('-');
    return out.join(C[cur][0] === 'GB' ? ' ' : '-');
  }
  function need() { return C[cur][4].reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0); }
  function check() {
    var d = inp.value.replace(/\D/g, ''); inp.value = fmt(d);
    var ok = d.length === need(), bad = d.length > 0 && !ok && (d.length > need() || ht.dataset.blur === '1');
    root.classList.toggle('is-ok', ok); root.classList.toggle('is-bad', bad); go.disabled = !ok;
    ht.textContent = C[cur][2] + ' numbers have ' + need() + ' digits';
  }
  function pick(i) {
    if (i === cur && code.textContent) return;
    var d = inp.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
    cur = i; fl.textContent = C[i][1]; code.textContent = C[i][3]; inp.placeholder = C[i][5];
    O.forEach(function (o, k) { o.setAttribute('aria-selected', k === i); });
    if (!rm) { fl.classList.remove('is-spin'); void fl.offsetWidth; fl.classList.add('is-spin'); setTimeout(function () { fl.classList.remove('is-spin'); }, 460); }
    ht.dataset.blur = d ? '1' : ''; inp.value = d; check();
  }
  function setOpen(v) { root.classList.toggle('is-open', v); cc.setAttribute('aria-expanded', v); if (v) { sr.value = ''; filter(); setTimeout(function () { sr.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 60); } }
  function filter() { var q = sr.value.trim().toLowerCase(); O.forEach(function (o, i) { o.hidden = q && (C[i][2] + C[i][0] + C[i][3]).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0; }); }
  cc.addEventListener('click', function () { setOpen(!root.classList.contains('is-open')); });
  sr.addEventListener('input', filter);
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && root.classList.contains('is-open')) { setOpen(false); cc.focus(); } });
  inp.addEventListener('input', function () { ht.dataset.blur = ''; check(); });
  inp.addEventListener('blur', function () { ht.dataset.blur = '1'; check(); });
  pick(0);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sc = root.querySelector('.u242-sc'), cb = root.querySelector('.u242-cb'), cr = root.querySelector('.u242-cr'), ul = root.querySelector('.u242-ul'), bd = root.querySelector('.u242-bd');

  var SEC = [['Popular', [['Salmon poke', '$14.90'], ['Chicken salad', '$11.90']]], ['Salads', [['Caesar salad', '$10.90'], ['Cobb salad', '$12.50'], ['Greek salad', '$11.50']]], ['Poke', [['Tuna poke', '$15.90'], ['Tofu poke', '$12.90']]], ['Wraps', [['Chicken wrap', '$9.90'], ['Avocado sandwich', '$10.50']]], ['Drinks', [['Lemonade', '$4.50'], ['Kombucha', '$5.00']]], ['Desserts', [['Greek yogurt', '$4.90'], ['Oat cookie', '$2.50']]]];
  var COL = ['#FDBA74', '#86EFAC', '#FCA5A5', '#FDE68A', '#93C5FD', '#F9A8D4'];
  var uid = 'u242' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  var S = [], C = [];
  SEC.forEach(function (s, i) {
    var sx = document.createElement('section'); sx.className = 'u242-sx'; sx.id = uid + i; sx.innerHTML = '<h4></h4>'; sx.firstChild.textContent = s[0];
    s[1].forEach(function (m, k) { var it = document.createElement('div'); it.className = 'u242-it'; it.innerHTML = '<div><b></b><span></span></div><i style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,' + COL[i] + ',color-mix(in srgb,' + COL[i] + ' 60%,#fff))"></i>'; it.querySelector('b').textContent = m[0]; it.querySelector('span').textContent = m[1]; sx.appendChild(it); });
    bd.appendChild(sx); S.push(sx);
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u242-ch'; b.textContent = s[0]; b.setAttribute('aria-controls', sx.id);
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { go(i); });
    cr.appendChild(b); C.push(b);
  });
  var cur = -1, lock = 0;
  function mark(i) {
    if (i === cur) return; cur = i;
    C.forEach(function (b, k) { b.setAttribute('aria-current', k === i ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    var b = C[i]; ul.style.width = b.offsetWidth + 'px'; ul.style.transform = 'translateX(' + b.offsetLeft + 'px)';
    var x = b.offsetLeft - (cr.clientWidth - b.offsetWidth) / 2, x0 = cr.scrollLeft, t0 = performance.now();
    x = Math.max(0, Math.min(cr.scrollWidth - cr.clientWidth, x));
    (function f(now) { var k = rm ? 1 : Math.min(1, (now - t0) / 320), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3); cr.scrollLeft = x0 + (x - x0) * e; if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f); })(t0);
  }
  function barH() { return cb.offsetHeight; }
  function spy() {
    cb.classList.toggle('is-stuck', cb.offsetTop - sc.scrollTop <= 0.5);
    if (Date.now() < lock) return;
    var y = sc.scrollTop + barH() + (sc.clientHeight - barH()) * 0.3, i = 0;
    S.forEach(function (s, k) { if (s.offsetTop <= y) i = k; });
    if (sc.scrollTop >= sc.scrollHeight - sc.clientHeight - 2) i = S.length - 1;
    mark(i);
  }
  function go(i) {
    mark(i); lock = Date.now() + (rm ? 0 : 700);
    var to = S[i].offsetTop - barH() + 2, y0 = sc.scrollTop, t0 = performance.now();
    (function f(now) { var k = rm ? 1 : Math.min(1, (now - t0) / 600), e = k < .5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2; sc.scrollTop = y0 + (to - y0) * e; if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f); })(t0);
  }
  sc.addEventListener('scroll', spy, { passive: true });
  mark(0); requestAnimationFrame(function () { cur = -1; mark(0); spy(); });
})();
</script>

</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var bg = root.querySelector('.u286-bg'), vw = root.querySelector('.u286-vw'), bk = root.querySelector('.u286-bk'), tt = root.querySelector('.u286-tt'), mn = root.querySelector('.u286-mn');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var TREE = {t: 'Menu', c: [{t: 'Product', c: [{t: 'Collaboration'}, {t: 'AI tools', c: [{t: 'Summaries'}, {t: 'Translation'}, {t: 'Meeting notes'}]}, {t: 'Integrations'}]}, {t: 'Pricing'}, {t: 'Customers', c: [{t: 'Startups'}, {t: 'Enterprise'}]}, {t: 'Blog'}, {t: 'Support'}]};
  var stack = [TREE], open = false;
  var ARW = '<svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 2.5L9.5 7 5 11.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
  function render(dir) {
    var node = stack[stack.length - 1], old = vw.querySelector('.u286-ls:not(.is-out)');
    var ul = document.createElement('ul'); ul.className = 'u286-ls';
    node.c.forEach(function (ch, i) {
      var li = document.createElement('li'), b = document.createElement('button');
      b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u286-it'; b.style.transitionDelay = (0.04 * i + 0.05) + 's';
      b.innerHTML = '<span></span>' + (ch.c ? ARW : ''); b.firstChild.textContent = ch.t;
      if (ch.c) b.setAttribute('aria-label', ch.t + ' submenu');
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { if (ch.c) { stack.push(ch); render(1); } });
      li.appendChild(b); ul.appendChild(li);
    });
    if (dir && !rm) ul.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (dir * 100) + '%)';
    vw.appendChild(ul);
    if (old) {
      old.classList.add('is-out');
      if (rm) old.remove(); else { old.style.transform = 'translateX(' + (-dir * 30) + '%)'; old.style.opacity = 0; setTimeout(function () { old.remove(); }, 400); }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(function () { requestAnimationFrame(function () { ul.style.transform = ''; ul.classList.add('is-in'); }); });
    tt.textContent = node.t; bk.hidden = stack.length < 2;
  }
  bk.addEventListener('click', function () { if (stack.length > 1) { stack.pop(); render(-1); } });
  bg.addEventListener('click', function () {
    open = !open; root.classList.toggle('is-open', open);
    bg.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open); bg.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu');
    if (open) { stack = [TREE]; vw.innerHTML = ''; render(0); }
  });
  bg.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Open menu');
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var cs = root.querySelector('.u271-cs'), pl = root.querySelector('.u271-pl'), tl = root.querySelector('.u271-tl');
  var CH = ['All', 'New', 'Improved', 'Fixed'], CC = ['', '#7C3AED', '#2563EB', '#EA580C'];
  var E = [[1, 'v3.2', 'Oct 14', 'AI now summarizes meeting notes', 'When a meeting ends, decisions and to-dos are boiled down to three lines.', 1], [3, 'v3.1.2', 'Oct 9', 'Cursor jumping while typing Korean', 'Fixed final consonants being typed twice in Safari.'], [2, 'v3.1', 'Oct 2', 'Search is twice as fast', 'Results show up instantly even for teams with thousands of docs.'], [1, 'v3.0', 'Sep 24', 'Dark mode', 'Turn it on in Settings › Display. It can follow your system, too.', 2], [3, 'v2.9.4', 'Sep 18', 'Duplicate notifications', 'Removed the double alerts that appeared when reopening the mobile app.'], [2, 'v2.9', 'Sep 10', 'Shared link previews', 'Paste a link and its title and thumbnail appear with it.']];
  var B = CH.map(function (c, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u271-ch'; b.setAttribute('role', 'radio');
    var n = i ? E.filter(function (e) { return e[0] === i; }).length : E.length;
    b.innerHTML = '<span></span><i>' + n + '</i>'; b.firstChild.textContent = c;
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { pick(i); });
    b.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { var d = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0; if (d) { e.preventDefault(); var k = (i + d + CH.length) % CH.length; pick(k); B[k].focus(); } });
    cs.appendChild(b); return b;
  });
  var R = E.map(function (e, i) {
    var w = document.createElement('div'); w.className = 'u271-en';
    w.innerHTML = '<div><div class="u271-in" style="--c:' + CC[e[0]] + '"><span class="u271-dt' + (i ? '' : ' is-new') + '"></span><div class="u271-mt"><b></b><span></span><span class="u271-tg"></span></div><h4></h4><p></p>' + (e[5] ? '<div class="u271-im k' + e[5] + '" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i><em></em></div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    var mt = w.querySelector('.u271-mt');
    mt.children[0].textContent = e[1]; mt.children[1].textContent = '· ' + e[2]; mt.children[2].textContent = CH[e[0]];
    if (!i) { var nw = document.createElement('span'); nw.className = 'u271-nw'; nw.textContent = 'New'; mt.appendChild(nw); }
    w.querySelector('h4').textContent = e[3]; w.querySelector('p').textContent = e[4];
    tl.appendChild(w); return w;
  });
  var cur = -1;
  function pill() { pl.style.transform = 'translateX(calc(' + cur * 100 + '% + ' + cur * 2 + 'px))'; }
  function pick(i) {
    if (i === cur) return;
    cur = i;
    B.forEach(function (b, k) { b.classList.toggle('is-on', k === i); b.setAttribute('aria-checked', k === i); b.tabIndex = k === i ? 0 : -1; });
    pill();
    R.forEach(function (w, k) { var off = i && E[k][0] !== i; w.classList.toggle('is-off', off); w.setAttribute('aria-hidden', off); });
  }
  pick(0);
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 33 / 75 - ID 292 - Filter-and-reflow gallery
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #292 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#292 -->
<!--
Element: Filter-and-reflow gallery
ID: 292 · Category: Sections & Content · Tags: filter, shuffle, grid, layout, transition
What it does: Pick a filter and the tiles that don’t match shrink away while the rest slide forward in order to fill the gaps. Tiles that come back pop up in their own spot, and the count beside the chips rolls to the number left.
How to use it: Filter by shape with the chips at the top
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#292
-->
<style>
.u292{position:relative;width:340px;height:350px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:16px;border-radius:28px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 30px 60px -36px var(--ux-shadow-strong);overflow:hidden}
.u292-fs{display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.u292-f{height:32px;padding:0 12px;border:0;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface-2);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:750;color:var(--ux-ink-2);transition:background-color .25s,color .25s}
.u292-f[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--ux-ink);color:var(--ux-page)}
.u292-n{margin:8px 0 0;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ux-ink-3);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.u292-g{position:relative;flex:1;margin-top:8px}
.u292-t{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:68px;height:68px;border-radius:16px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:var(--bg);transition:transform .6s cubic-bezier(.3,1.25,.4,1),opacity .35s}
.u292-t.out{opacity:0}
.u292-t svg{width:30px;height:30px;fill:rgba(255,255,255,.9)}
</style>
<div class="ux u292">
  <div class="u292-fs" role="group" aria-label="Shape filter"></div>
  <p class="u292-n" aria-live="polite"></p>
  <div class="u292-g"></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var fs = root.querySelector('.u292-fs'), g = root.querySelector('.u292-g'), nEl = root.querySelector('.u292-n');
  var CATS = ['All', 'Circle', 'Square', 'Triangle'];
  var SHAPE = ['', '<circle cx="15" cy="15" r="12"/>', '<rect x="4" y="4" width="22" height="22" rx="4"/>', '<path d="M15 3l13 23H2z"/>'];
  var KINDS = [1, 2, 3, 1, 3, 2, 2, 1, 3, 1, 2, 3];
  var BG = ['#FF8787', '#FFA94D', '#FFD43B', '#69DB7C', '#38D9A9', '#4DABF7', '#748FFC', '#DA77F2', '#F783AC', '#A9E34B', '#3BC9DB', '#9775FA'];
  var cat = 0;
  var tiles = KINDS.map(function (k, i) {
    var t = document.createElement('div');
    t.className = 'u292-t';
    t.style.setProperty('--bg', BG[i]);
    t.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 30 30" aria-hidden="true">' + SHAPE[k] + '</svg>';
    t.setAttribute('role', 'img'); t.setAttribute('aria-label', CATS[k]);
    g.appendChild(t);
    return { el: t, k: k, slot: i, shown: true };
  });
  var chips = CATS.map(function (c, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u292-f'; b.textContent = c; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(i === 0));
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { if (cat !== i) apply(i); });
    fs.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  function xy(slot) { return [(slot % 4) * 76, Math.floor(slot / 4) * 76]; }
  function apply(c) {
    cat = c;
    chips.forEach(function (b, i) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(i === c)); });
    var n = 0;
    tiles.forEach(function (t) {
      var keep = c === 0 || t.k === c;
      var was = t.shown;
      t.shown = keep;
      if (keep) {
        var p = xy(n);
        t.el.style.transitionDelay = (n * 35) + 'ms';
        if (!was) { t.el.style.transition = 'none'; t.el.style.transform = 'translate(' + p[0] + 'px,' + p[1] + 'px) scale(0)'; void t.el.offsetWidth; t.el.style.transition = ''; }
        t.el.classList.remove('out');
        t.el.style.transform = 'translate(' + p[0] + 'px,' + p[1] + 'px)';
        t.el.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
        n += 1;
      } else {
        var cur = t.el.style.transform.replace(/ scale\([^)]*\)/, '');
        t.el.style.transitionDelay = '0ms';
        t.el.classList.add('out');
        t.el.style.transform = cur + ' scale(.2)';
        t.el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      }
    });
    nEl.textContent = n + ' showing';
  }
  tiles.forEach(function (t, i) { var p = xy(i); t.el.style.transition = 'none'; t.el.style.transform = 'translate(' + p[0] + 'px,' + p[1] + 'px)'; });
  void g.offsetWidth;
  tiles.forEach(function (t) { t.el.style.transition = ''; });
  apply(0);
})();
</script>

<style>
.u049{--px:0;--py:0;position:relative;width:320px;height:460px;border-radius:30px;overflow:hidden;isolation:isolate;color:#fff;background:#1B1440;box-shadow:0 30px 60px -30px var(--ux-shadow-strong);transition:transform .6s var(--ux-ease),box-shadow .6s var(--ux-ease)}

    <div class="u049-k">ISSUE 12 · 6 MIN</div>
    <h3 class="u049-t">Trust in a<br>short transition</h3>
    <p class="u049-sum">A 150-millisecond response is enough to make a screen feel alive. Deciding where it settles and where it springs is the work of design.</p>
    <div class="u049-foot"><span class="u049-av" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="u049-by">Jiwoo Han<small>Motion designer</small></span><button class="u049-cta" type="button">Read <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 8h10M9 4l4 4-4 4"/></svg></button></div>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var bm = root.querySelector('.u049-bm'), cta = root.querySelector('.u049-cta');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var gid = 'u049-s' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  root.querySelector('linearGradient').id = gid;
  root.querySelector('.u049-l1 rect').setAttribute('fill', 'url(#' + gid + ')');
  function open(v) { root.classList.toggle('is-open', v); }
  root.addEventListener('pointerenter', function (e) { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') open(true); });
  root.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
    if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse' || rm) return;
    var r = root.getBoundingClientRect();
    root.style.setProperty('--px', ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * 2 - 1).toFixed(3));
    root.style.setProperty('--py', ((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * 2 - 1).toFixed(3));
  });
  root.addEventListener('pointerleave', function (e) {
    if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse') return;
    root.style.setProperty('--px', 0); root.style.setProperty('--py', 0);
    if (!root.contains(document.activeElement)) open(false);
  });
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
    open(!root.classList.contains('is-open'));
  });
  var quiet = false;
  root.addEventListener('focusin', function () { if (!quiet) open(true); });
  root.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) { if (!root.contains(e.relatedTarget) && !root.matches(':hover')) open(false); });
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.target === root && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); cta.focus(); }
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { quiet = true; root.focus(); quiet = false; open(false); }
  });
  bm.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var on = bm.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== 'true';
    bm.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
    bm.setAttribute('aria-label', on ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark');
  });
  cta.addEventListener('click', function () {
    cta.firstChild.textContent = 'Opening ';
    setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) cta.firstChild.textContent = 'Read '; }, 1200);
  });
})();
</script>

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #328 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#328 -->
<!--
Element: Card that expands into detail
ID: 328 · Category: Sections & Content · Tags: shared-element, flip, card, detail, navigation
What it does: Tap a destination card and it grows from where it sits to fill the screen: the photo stretches into the header and the title moves to the big-title spot. The body rises in line by line, and closing retraces the same path to shrink back into the card.
How to use it: Tap a card to open it, and use the close button at the top left to go back
Footprint: fixed size, tall
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#328
-->
<style>
.u328{position:relative;width:300px;height:500px;border-radius:30px;background:var(--ux-surface-2);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 30px 60px -36px var(--ux-shadow-strong);overflow:hidden}
.u328-k{margin:0;padding:20px 18px 12px;font-size:22px;font-weight:880;letter-spacing:-.04em}
.u328-ls{display:grid;gap:12px;padding:0 14px}
.u328-card{position:relative;height:118px;border:0;padding:0;border-radius:20px;overflow:hidden;text-align:left;color:#fff;transition:transform .3s var(--ux-spring),opacity .2s}
.u328-card:active{transform:scale(.97)}
.u328-card.hide{opacity:0}
.u328-card .im{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--bg)}
.u328-card .tx{position:absolute;left:16px;bottom:14px}
.u328-card b{display:block;font-size:20px;font-weight:880;letter-spacing:-.03em;text-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(0,0,0,.25)}
.u328-card span{font-size:12.5px;font-weight:650;opacity:.9}
.u328-dt{position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:3;background:var(--ux-surface);visibility:hidden;overflow:hidden}
.u328.is-open .u328-dt{visibility:visible}
.u328-hero{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:300px;height:250px;background:var(--bg);transform-origin:0 0}
.u328-ttl{position:absolute;left:20px;top:192px;color:#fff;transform-origin:0 100%}
.u328-ttl b{display:block;font-size:30px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:-.04em;text-shadow:0 2px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.25)}
.u328-ttl span{font-size:14px;font-weight:700;opacity:.9}
.u328-body{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;top:250px;padding:18px 20px}
.u328-body>*{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px)}
.u328.is-in .u328-body>*{opacity:1;transform:none;transition:opacity .4s,transform .6s var(--ux-spring)}
.u328.is-in .u328-body>:nth-child(2){transition-delay:.06s}
.u328.is-in .u328-body>:nth-child(3){transition-delay:.12s}
.u328-body p{margin:0;font-size:14px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--ux-ink-2)}
.u328-meta{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:12px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ux-ink-3)}
.u328-meta i{width:24px;height:24px;margin-right:-14px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid var(--ux-surface);background:linear-gradient(135deg,#FFA8A8,#748FFC)}
.u328-go{width:100%;height:46px;margin-top:16px;border:0;border-radius:14px;background:var(--ux-ink);color:var(--ux-page);font-size:14px;font-weight:800}
.u328-x{position:absolute;left:14px;top:14px;z-index:4;width:36px;height:36px;border:0;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;background:rgba(0,0,0,.35);color:#fff;-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(8px);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);opacity:0;transform:scale(.6);transition:opacity .25s,transform .4s var(--ux-spring);pointer-events:none}
.u328.is-in .u328-x{opacity:1;transform:none;pointer-events:auto}

<div class="ux u328">
  <p class="u328-k">Where to next?</p>
  <div class="u328-ls"></div>
  <div class="u328-dt" role="dialog" aria-modal="false">
    <div class="u328-hero"></div>
    <div class="u328-ttl"><b></b><span></span></div>
    <div class="u328-body"><p></p><div class="u328-meta"><i></i><i></i><i></i><span></span></div><button class="u328-go" type="button">Book now</button></div>
  </div>
  <button class="u328-x" type="button" aria-label="Close"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 4l8 8M12 4l-8 8"/></svg></button>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var ls = root.querySelector('.u328-ls'), dt = root.querySelector('.u328-dt'), hero = root.querySelector('.u328-hero'), ttl = root.querySelector('.u328-ttl'), x = root.querySelector('.u328-x');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var PLACES = [['Iceland', '7 days chasing auroras', 'Ice caves, black sand beaches and green light slicing across the night sky. A full loop of the island along the Ring Road.'], ['Morocco', 'Three nights in the desert', 'A journey that starts in the alleys of Marrakech and ends counting stars on the dunes of the Sahara.'], ['Kyoto', 'Temples in autumn leaves', 'Walk empty gardens early in the morning, then rest slowly in a tea house in the afternoon.']];
  var BG = ['radial-gradient(60% 40% at 60% 30%,#63E6BE,transparent 70%),linear-gradient(#0B1340,#1E3A5F 60%,#0B0C12)', 'radial-gradient(30% 22% at 75% 30%,#FFE066,transparent 70%),linear-gradient(#F59F00,#E8590C 50%,#C2410C 51%,#9A3412)', 'radial-gradient(50% 40% at 30% 70%,#E03131,transparent 70%),linear-gradient(135deg,#FFA94D,#C92A2A 60%,#5C3D2E)'];
  var cards = PLACES.map(function (p, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u328-card';
    b.innerHTML = '<span class="im"></span><span class="tx"><b></b><span></span></span>';
    b.querySelector('.im').style.setProperty('--bg', BG[i]);
    b.querySelector('b').textContent = p[0];
    b.querySelector('.tx span').textContent = p[1];
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { open(i); });
    ls.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  var cur = -1, busy = false;
  function rect(el) { var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(), rr = root.getBoundingClientRect(), k = root.offsetWidth / (rr.width || 1); return { x: (r.left - rr.left) * k, y: (r.top - rr.top) * k, w: r.width * k, h: r.height * k }; }
  function frames(c) {
    var clip0 = 'inset(' + c.y + 'px ' + (300 - c.x - c.w) + 'px ' + (500 - c.y - c.h) + 'px ' + c.x + 'px round 20px)', clip1 = 'inset(0px 0px 0px 0px round 30px)';
    return {
      dt: [{ clipPath: clip0 }, { clipPath: clip1 }],
      hero: [{ transform: 'translate(' + c.x + 'px,' + c.y + 'px) scale(' + (c.w / 300) + ',' + (c.h / 250) + ')' }, { transform: 'none' }],
      ttl: [{ transform: 'translate(' + (c.x + 16 - 20) + 'px,' + (c.y + c.h - 14 - 242) + 'px) scale(.68)' }, { transform: 'none' }]
    };
  }
  function run(f, reverse, done) {
    var o = { duration: 560, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.3,1.05,.35,1)', fill: 'both', direction: reverse ? 'reverse' : 'normal' };
    if (rm || !dt.animate) { done(); return; }
    dt.animate(f.dt, o); hero.animate(f.hero, o);
    ttl.animate(f.ttl, o).onfinish = done;
  }
  function open(i) {
    if (busy || cur >= 0) return;
    busy = true; cur = i;
    var p = PLACES[i];
    hero.style.setProperty('--bg', BG[i]);
    ttl.querySelector('b').textContent = p[0]; ttl.querySelector('span').textContent = p[1];
    dt.querySelector('p').textContent = p[2];
    dt.querySelector('.u328-meta span').textContent = (120 + i * 87) + ' interested';
    dt.setAttribute('aria-label', p[0]);
    var c = rect(cards[i]);
    root.classList.add('is-open');
    cards[i].classList.add('hide');
    run(frames(c), false, function () { busy = false; root.classList.add('is-in'); x.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
  }
  x.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (busy || cur < 0) return;
    busy = true;
    root.classList.remove('is-in');
    var c = rect(cards[cur]), i = cur;
    setTimeout(function () {
      run(frames(c), true, function () {
        root.classList.remove('is-open'); cards[i].classList.remove('hide');
        dt.getAnimations && dt.getAnimations().forEach(function (a) { a.cancel(); });
        hero.getAnimations && hero.getAnimations().forEach(function (a) { a.cancel(); });
        ttl.getAnimations && ttl.getAnimations().forEach(function (a) { a.cancel(); });
        busy = false; cur = -1; cards[i].focus({ preventScroll: true });
      });
    }, rm ? 0 : 160);
  });
})();
</script>

Element: Blur-up photo loading
ID: 258 · Category: Sections & Content · Tags: image-loading, blur-up, lqip, gallery, performance
What it does: When the photo gallery opens, each tile first shows a tiny blurry preview while a thin bar below fills as data arrives.     b.className = 'u061-th'; b.type = 'button';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Open ' + p[0]);
    b.innerHTML = art(p, 't' + i);
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { open(i); });
    grid.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  var cur = -1, isOpen = false, z = 1, tx = 0, ty = 0;
  function rectOf(i) { var t = ths[i]; return { l: grid.offsetLeft + t.offsetLeft, t: grid.offsetTop + t.offsetTop, w: t.offsetWidth, h: t.offsetHeight }; }
  function caption() {
    cap.querySelector('small').textContent = P[cur][1];
    cap.querySelector('b').textContent = P[cur][0];
    cap.querySelector('em').textContent = (cur + 1) + ' / ' + P.length;
  }
  function setZoom(nz, ox, oy) {
    var W = view.clientWidth, H = view.clientHeight;
    if (nz > 1) { tx = Math.min(0, Math.max(W - W * nz, ox - (ox - tx) * nz / z)); ty = Math.min(0, Math.max(H - H * nz, oy - (oy - ty) * nz / z)); }
    else { tx = 0; ty = 0; }
    z = nz;
    img.style.transform = 'translate(' + tx + 'px,' + ty + 'px) scale(' + z + ')';
    root.classList.toggle('is-zoom', z > 1);
  }
  function morph(from, to, done) {
    var full = { l: 0, t: 0, w: root.clientWidth, h: root.clientHeight };
    var a = from || full, b = to || full;
    if (rm) { done && done(); return; }
    view.animate([
      { left: a.l + 'px', top: a.t + 'px', width: a.w + 'px', height: a.h + 'px', borderRadius: from ? '18px' : '30px' },
      { left: b.l + 'px', top: b.t + 'px', width: b.w + 'px', height: b.h + 'px', borderRadius: to ? '18px' : '30px' }
    ], { duration: 520, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)' }).onfinish = function () { done && done(); };
  }
  function open(i) {
    if (isOpen) return;
    isOpen = true; cur = i;
    img.innerHTML = art(P[i], 'v');
    setZoom(1);
    caption();
    root.classList.add('is-open');
    view.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
    ths[i].classList.add('away');
    morph(rectOf(i), null, function () { root.classList.add('is-ready'); xBtn.focus({ preventScroll: true }); });
  }
  function close() {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    var i = cur;
    root.classList.remove('is-ready', 'is-zoom');
    setZoom(1);
    view.style.transform = '';
    root.style.removeProperty('--so');
    scrim.style.opacity = '0';
    morph(null, rectOf(i), function () {
      isOpen = false;
      root.classList.remove('is-open');
      scrim.style.opacity = '';
      view.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      ths[i].classList.remove('away');
      ths[i].focus({ preventScroll: true });
    });
  }
  function go(d) {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    var n = (cur + d + P.length) % P.length;
    ths[cur].classList.remove('away');
    cur = n;
    ths[cur].classList.add('away');
    setZoom(1);
    var next = document.createElement('div');
    next.innerHTML = art(P[n], 'v');
    if (!rm) img.animate([{ transform: 'translateX(' + (d * 40) + 'px)', opacity: .2 }, { transform: 'none', opacity: 1 }], { duration: 420, easing: 'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)' });
    img.innerHTML = next.innerHTML;
    caption();
  }
  xBtn.addEventListener('click', close);
  scrim.addEventListener('click', close);
  view.addEventListener('dblclick', function (e) {
    if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
    var r = view.getBoundingClientRect(), k = view.clientWidth / r.width;
    img.classList.remove('now');
    setZoom(z > 1 ? 1 : 2, (e.clientX - r.left) * k, (e.clientY - r.top) * k);
  });
  view.addEventListener('wheel', function (e) {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var r = view.getBoundingClientRect(), k = view.clientWidth / r.width;
    img.classList.add('now');
    setZoom(Math.max(1, Math.min(3, z * (e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : 0.89))), (e.clientX - r.left) * k, (e.clientY - r.top) * k);
  }, { passive: false });
  var sx = 0, sy = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, mode = '';
  view.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) {
    if (e.target.closest('button') || !isOpen) return;
    sx = e.clientX; sy = e.clientY; ox = tx; oy = ty; mode = 'wait';
    view.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  });
  view.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
    if (!mode) return;
    var r = view.getBoundingClientRect(), k = view.clientWidth / r.width;
    var dx = (e.clientX - sx) * k, dy = (e.clientY - sy) * k;
    if (mode === 'wait' && Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 6) mode = z > 1 ? 'pan' : Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx) ? 'down' : 'side';
    if (mode === 'pan') {
      img.classList.add('now');
      var W = view.clientWidth, H = view.clientHeight;
      tx = Math.min(0, Math.max(W - W * z, ox + dx)); ty = Math.min(0, Math.max(H - H * z, oy + dy));
      img.style.transform = 'translate(' + tx + 'px,' + ty + 'px) scale(' + z + ')';
    } else if (mode === 'down') {
      var d = Math.max(0, dy);
      view.style.transform = 'translateY(' + d + 'px) scale(' + (1 - d / 1600) + ')';
      root.style.setProperty('--so', Math.max(0.2, 1 - d / 300).toFixed(2));
    } else if (mode === 'side') {
      img.classList.add('now');
      img.style.transform = 'translateX(' + dx * 0.5 + 'px)';
    }
  });
  function up(e) {
    if (!mode) return;
    var r = view.getBoundingClientRect(), k = view.clientWidth / r.width;
    var dx = (e.clientX - sx) * k, dy = (e.clientY - sy) * k, m = mode;
    mode = '';
    img.classList.remove('now');
    if (m === 'down') {
      if (dy > 110) { close(); return; }
      view.style.transition = 'transform .4s cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.64,1)';
      view.style.transform = '';
      root.style.removeProperty('--so');
      setTimeout(function () { view.style.transition = ''; }, 420);
    } else if (m === 'side') {
      img.style.transform = '';
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 70) go(dx < 0 ? 1 : -1);
    }
  }
  view.addEventListener('pointerup', up);
  view.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); close(); }
    else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); go(1); }
    else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); go(-1); }
    else if (e.key === 'Tab') { e.preventDefault(); xBtn.focus(); }
  });
})();
</script>

(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;

  }
  [[0, 2, 4, 6], [1, 3, 5, 7]].forEach(function (ids, c) {

    if (rm) Array.prototype.slice.call(tr.children, 4).forEach(function (n) { n.remove(); });
    co.appendChild(tr); wl.appendChild(co);
    co.addEventListener('focusin', function () { co.classList.add('is-hold'); }); co.addEventListener('focusout', function () { co.classList.remove('is-hold'); });
    var hl = null;
    function mark(c) { if (hl === c) return; if (hl) hl.classList.remove('is-hl'); hl = c; if (c) c.classList.add('is-hl'); }
    co.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { var y = e.clientY; mark(Array.prototype.filter.call(tr.children, function (c) { var r = c.getBoundingClientRect(); return y >= r.top && y <= r.bottom; })[0] || null); });
    co.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { mark(null); });
  });
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { root.classList.toggle('is-off', !es[0].isIntersecting); }).observe(root);
})();

NORDA COMPONENT 40 / 75 - ID 234 - Cycling customer logo wall

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #234 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#234 -->
<!--
Element: Cycling customer logo wall
ID: 234 · Category: Social Proof · Tags: logos, social-proof, customers, cycle, landing
What it does: In a landing page's customer logo grid, every few seconds one cell's logo blurs out upward while a new one sharpens in from below. The same logo never shows in two cells at once, and resting the pointer on a cell stops the swapping while a See story link slides out in that cell.
How to use it: Rest the pointer on a logo cell
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#234

<style>
.u234{position:relative;width:340px;height:340px;border-radius:30px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 30px 60px -36px var(--ux-shadow-strong);color:var(--ux-ink)}

<script>

  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;

    ['mosaic', 'font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.03em', '<rect x="2" y="2" width="5" height="5" rx="1" fill="currentColor"/><rect x="9" y="2" width="5" height="5" rx="2.5" fill="currentColor"/><rect x="2" y="9" width="5" height="5" rx="2.5" fill="currentColor"/><rect x="9" y="9" width="5" height="5" rx="1" fill="currentColor"/>'],
    ['Brisk', 'font-weight:900;letter-spacing:-.05em;font-size:14px', '<path d="M3 13l4-5-2-1 6-5-2 5 3 1z" fill="currentColor"/>'],
    ['Vela', 'font-family:Georgia,serif;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.06em', '<path d="M8 2v12M8 2l5 9H8" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'],
    ['arcus', 'font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.01em', '<path d="M2 12a6 6 0 0 1 12 0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"/>'],
    ['JUNIPER', 'font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.12em;font-size:10.5px', '<circle cx="5.5" cy="10" r="3" fill="currentColor"/><circle cx="10.5" cy="10" r="3" fill="currentColor" opacity=".6"/><circle cx="8" cy="5.5" r="3" fill="currentColor" opacity=".8"/>'],
    ['Oakline', 'font-weight:650', '<path d="M3 13h10M5 13V6M11 13V6M3 6l5-4 5 4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'],
    ['ZEST', 'font-weight:900;font-style:italic;letter-spacing:.04em', '<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M8 2v12M2 8h12" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.2"/>']
  ];
  function logo(i) { var d = document.createElement('span'); d.className = 'u234-lg'; d.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true">' + L[i][2] + '</svg><b style="font-size:13px;' + L[i][1] + '"></b>'; d.querySelector('b').textContent = L[i][0]; return d; }
  var shown = [], cells = [], hold = -1, vis = true, last = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    var c = document.createElement('button'); c.type = 'button'; c.className = 'u234-cl';
    c.appendChild(logo(i)); var g = document.createElement('span'); g.className = 'u234-go'; g.textContent = 'See story →'; c.appendChild(g);
    c.setAttribute('aria-label', L[i][0] + ' customer story');
    (function (k) { c.addEventListener('pointerenter', function () { hold = k; }); c.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { if (hold === k) hold = -1; }); c.addEventListener('focus', function () { hold = k; }); c.addEventListener('blur', function () { if (hold === k) hold = -1; }); })(i);
    gd.appendChild(c); cells.push(c); shown.push(i);
  }
  function swap() {
    var pool = cells.map(function (c, k) { return k; }).filter(function (k) { return k !== hold && k !== last; });
    var k = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)], free = L.map(function (l, n) { return n; }).filter(function (n) { return shown.indexOf(n) < 0; });
    var n = free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)], c = cells[k], old = c.querySelector('.u234-lg'), nw = logo(n);
    last = k; shown[k] = n; c.setAttribute('aria-label', L[n][0] + ' customer story');
    old.classList.add('is-out'); nw.classList.add('is-in'); c.insertBefore(nw, c.firstChild);
    void nw.offsetWidth; nw.classList.remove('is-in');
    setTimeout(function () { old.remove(); }, 560);
  }
  (function loop() {
    if (!root.isConnected) return;
    if (vis && hold < 0 && !rm) swap();
    setTimeout(loop, 1600);
  })();
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { vis = es[0].isIntersecting; }).observe(root);
})();
</script>

NORDA COMPONENT 41 / 75 - ID 243 - Tap-to-verify security badges
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #243 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#243 -->
<!--
Element: Tap-to-verify security badges

What it does: A row of security and compliance badges at the bottom of a product page gets a passing sheen now and then. <script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var gd = root.querySelector('.u243-gd');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var BG = [['ISO 27001', 'Info security', 'Certified by ISO', 'All services · until Mar 2027'], ['SOC 2', 'Security & uptime', 'Type II audit report', 'Audited externally every year'], ['GDPR', 'Privacy', 'EU privacy rules', 'DPA available'], ['ISMS-P', 'Korean infosec', 'Certified by KISA', 'Until Nov 2026']];
  var COL = ['#2563EB', '#7C3AED', '#0D9488', '#E11D48'];
  var SHIELD = '<svg viewBox="0 0 42 46" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M21 3l15 5.5v11.5c0 11-6.6 18.8-15 22.5C12.6 38.8 6 31 6 20V8.5z" fill="color-mix(in srgb,currentColor 14%,transparent)" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M14.5 22.5l4.5 4.5 9-9.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
  BG.forEach(function (g, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('div'); b.className = 'u243-bg'; b.style.setProperty('--c', COL[i]); b.style.setProperty('--d', (i * 1.1) + 's'); b.tabIndex = 0; b.setAttribute('role', 'button'); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'); b.setAttribute('aria-label', g[0] + ' — details');
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="u243-f"><span class="u243-sd">' + SHIELD + '</span><b></b><small></small></div><div class="u243-b"><b></b><span></span><small></small><button type="button" class="u243-vf" tabindex="-1"><i aria-hidden="true"></i><em style="font-style:normal"></em></button></div>';
    b.querySelector('.u243-f b').textContent = g[0]; b.querySelector('.u243-f small').textContent = g[1];
    b.querySelector('.u243-b b').textContent = g[0]; b.querySelector('.u243-b span').textContent = g[2]; b.querySelector('.u243-b small').textContent = g[3];
    var vf = b.querySelector('.u243-vf'), vl = vf.querySelector('em'); vl.textContent = 'Verify';
    function flip() { var on = !b.classList.contains('is-flip'); b.classList.toggle('is-flip', on); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on); vf.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1; }
    b.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target.closest('.u243-vf')) return; flip(); });
    b.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && e.target === b) { e.preventDefault(); flip(); } });
    vf.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.stopPropagation(); if (vf.classList.contains('is-busy') || vf.classList.contains('is-ok')) return;
      vf.classList.add('is-busy'); vl.textContent = 'Checking…';
      setTimeout(function () { vf.classList.remove('is-busy'); vf.classList.add('is-ok'); vl.textContent = 'Valid · just checked'; }, rm ? 0 : 900);
    });
    gd.appendChild(b);
  });
})();
</script>

(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var ln = root.querySelector('.u215-ln'), wg = root.querySelector('.u215-wg'), ms = root.querySelector('.u215-ms'), qs = root.querySelector('.u215-qs'), ip = root.querySelector('.u215-ip input');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var QK = ['I want to change plans', 'Where do I get receipts?', 'Talk to a person'];
  var AN = [['You can switch anytime in Settings › Billing. Unused time is prorated.', 'Changing your plan'], ['You can download them monthly from your billing history.', 'How to get receipts']];
  var busy = false;
  var Q = QK.map(function (q, i) { var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u215-qk'; b.textContent = q; b.tabIndex = -1; b.addEventListener('click', function () { ask(i); }); qs.appendChild(b); return b; });
  function add(el) { ms.appendChild(el); ms.scrollTop = ms.scrollHeight; return el; }
  function bub(cls, txt) { var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'u215-b ' + cls; d.textContent = txt; return add(d); }
  function ask(i) {
    if (busy) return; busy = true; Q[i].disabled = true;
    bub('me', QK[i]);
    var ty = document.createElement('div'); ty.className = 'u215-ty'; ty.innerHTML = '<i></i><i></i><i></i>';
    setTimeout(function () { add(ty); }, rm ? 0 : 300);
    setTimeout(function () {
      ty.remove();
      if (i < 2) {
        var d = bub('bt', AN[i][0]), ar = document.createElement('div'); ar.className = 'u215-ar'; ar.innerHTML = '<i aria-hidden="true"></i><div><b></b><small></small></div>'; ar.querySelector('b').textContent = AN[i][1]; ar.querySelector('small').textContent = 'Help article · 2 min read'; d.appendChild(ar); ms.scrollTop = ms.scrollHeight;
      } else {
        var s = document.createElement('div'); s.className = 'u215-sy'; s.textContent = 'Minji joined the chat'; add(s);
        setTimeout(function () { bub('ag', 'Hi, I’m Minji. What can I help with?'); }, rm ? 0 : 600);
      }
      busy = false;
    }, rm ? 0 : 1300);
  }
  function setOpen(v) {
    root.classList.toggle('is-open', v); wg.setAttribute('aria-hidden', !v); ln.setAttribute('aria-expanded', v); ln.setAttribute('aria-label', v ? 'Close chat' : 'Open chat');
    Q.forEach(function (b) { b.tabIndex = v ? 0 : -1; }); ip.tabIndex = v ? 0 : -1;
    if (v) setTimeout(function () { Q[0].focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, rm ? 0 : 350);
  }
  ln.addEventListener('click', function () { setOpen(!root.classList.contains('is-open')); });
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && root.classList.contains('is-open')) { setOpen(false); ln.focus(); } });
})();
</script>

  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var A = {
    'Ideas to improve onboarding': [['p', 'Three things are worth trying first.'], ['li', 'Let people <b>start on the first screen</b> and cut the explaining'], ['li', 'Show the <b>steps left</b> with a progress bar'], ['li', 'Keep the skip button <b>in the same place</b>'], ['p', 'The first one cuts drop-off the most.']],
    'What’s a good button transition time?': [['p', 'For most cases <b>150–250ms</b> is enough.'], ['li', 'Start press feedback <b>within 100ms</b>'], ['li', 'Large moving panels: <b>around 300ms</b>'], ['p', 'Shorter feels faster, but too short and the change disappears.']]
  };
  var ts = [], state = 'idle', last = null, stopFn = null;
  function clear() { ts.forEach(clearTimeout); ts = []; }
  function later(fn, ms) { ts.push(setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) fn(); }, ms)); }
  function setCtl(s) {
    state = s;
    root.classList.toggle('is-gen', s === 'gen');
    if (s === 'gen') ctl.innerHTML = '<i aria-hidden="true"></i>Stop';
    else if (s === 'done') ctl.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M11.5 7a4.5 4.5 0 1 1-1.3-3.2M11.5 2v2.5H9"/></svg>Regenerate';
    ctl.setAttribute('aria-disabled', String(s === 'idle'));
    sugs.forEach(function (b) { b.disabled = s === 'gen'; });
  }
  function ask(q) {
    clear(); last = q;
    log.innerHTML = "";
    var qe = document.createElement('div'); qe.className = 'u086-q'; qe.textContent = q; log.appendChild(qe);
    var a = document.createElement('div'); a.className = 'u086-a';
    a.innerHTML = '<span class="u086-av" aria-hidden="true"></span><div class="u086-body"><span class="u086-dots" aria-label="Thinking"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span></div>';
    log.appendChild(a);
    var body = a.querySelector('.u086-body');
    setCtl('gen');
    var tokens = [];
    A[q].forEach(function (blk) {
      tokens.push({ open: blk[0] });
      blk[1].split(/(<b>.*?<\/b>|\s+)/).filter(Boolean).forEach(function (t) { tokens.push({ t: t }); });
    });
    var i = 0, el = null, ol = null, caret = document.createElement('span');
    caret.className = 'u086-caret'; caret.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    stopFn = function () { clear(); caret.remove(); var s = document.createElement('span'); s.className = 'u086-stop'; s.textContent = '— Stopped here'; body.appendChild(s); setCtl('done'); };
    later(function () {
      body.innerHTML = '';
      (function tick() {
        var n = rm ? tokens.length : 1 + Math.round(Math.random());
        while (n-- > 0 && i < tokens.length) {
          var tk = tokens[i++];
          if (tk.open === 'p') { el = document.createElement('p'); body.appendChild(el); ol = null; }
          else if (tk.open === 'li') { if (!ol) { ol = document.createElement('ol'); body.appendChild(ol); } el = document.createElement('li'); ol.appendChild(el); }
          else if (/^\s+$/.test(tk.t)) el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
          else { var w = document.createElement('span'); w.className = 'u086-w'; w.innerHTML = tk.t; el.appendChild(w); }
        }
        if (el) el.appendChild(caret);
        if (i < tokens.length) later(tick, 45 + Math.random() * 60);
        else later(function () { caret.remove(); setCtl('done'); }, 600);
      })();
    }, rm ? 200 : 1200);
  }
  sugs.forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { if (state !== 'gen') ask(b.textContent); }); });
  ctl.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (state === 'gen' && stopFn) stopFn();
    else if (state === 'done' && last) ask(last);
  });
  ask('Ideas to improve onboarding');
})();
</script>

Element: Offline message queue
