<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var sw = root.querySelector('.u091-sw'), bt = root.querySelector('.u091-bt b'), bs = root.querySelector('.u091-bt small'), sub = root.querySelector('.u091-ti small');
  var list = root.querySelector('.u091-list'), form = root.querySelector('.u091-cmp'), inp = root.querySelector('.u091-in'), send = root.querySelector('.u091-send');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var CLOCK = '<circle cx="7" cy="7" r="5.2"/><path d="M7 4.4V7l1.8 1.2"/>', DBL = '<path d="M1.6 7.4l2.4 2.4L9 4.8M6.4 9.4l.4.4 5-5"/>';
  var state = 'off', ts = [], min = 14, replied = false;
  function later(fn, ms) { ts.push(setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) fn(); }, ms)); }
  function clear() { ts.forEach(clearTimeout); ts = []; }
  function setState(s) { state = s; root.classList.remove('off', 'rec', 'on', 'back'); root.classList.add(s); sw.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(s !== 'off')); sub.textContent = s === 'off' ? 'Disconnected' : s === 'rec' ? 'Connecting' : 'Online'; }
  function queued() { return list.querySelectorAll('.u091-r.q'); }
  function banner(b, s) { bt.textContent = b; bs.textContent = s; }
  function offText() { var n = queued().length; banner('You’re offline', n ? n + ' waiting · sends when online' : 'Messages send once you’re connected'); }
  function row(text, me, q) {
    var r = document.createElement('div');
    r.className = 'u091-r' + (me ? ' me' : '') + (q ? ' q' : '');
    r.innerHTML = '<div class="u091-m"></div>' + (me ? '<span class="u091-meta"><span></span><svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" aria-hidden="true"></svg></span>' : '');
    r.firstChild.textContent = text;
    if (me) mark(r, q);
    list.appendChild(r);
    while (list.children.length > 9) list.firstChild.remove();
    return r;
  }
  function mark(r, q) {
    r.classList.toggle('q', q);
    r.querySelector('.u091-meta span').textContent = q ? 'Queued' : '2:' + min + ' PM';
    r.querySelector('.u091-meta svg').innerHTML = q ? CLOCK : DBL;
  }
  row('Could you check the draft before the afternoon review?', false);
  row('Sure, looking now', true, false);
  row('Maybe raise the color contrast a bit', true, true);
  function flush() {
    var q = Array.prototype.slice.call(queued()), n = q.length;
    q.forEach(function (r, i) {
      later(function () { min++; mark(r, false); r.classList.remove('pop'); void r.offsetWidth; r.classList.add('pop'); }, rm ? 0 : 300 + i * 380);
    });
    banner('Back online', n ? 'Sent ' + n + (n === 1 ? ' queued message' : ' queued messages') : 'Messages go through in real time again');
    later(function () {
      setState('on');
      if (n && !replied) {
        replied = true;
        var t = row('', false); t.firstChild.innerHTML = '<span class="u091-typ" aria-label="Typing"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>'; t.firstChild.style.padding = '0';
        later(function () { t.firstChild.style.padding = ''; t.firstChild.textContent = 'Sounds good, I’ll raise it and repost'; }, rm ? 0 : 1300);
      }
    }, rm ? 600 : 1500 + n * 380);
  }
  function reconnect() {
    clear(); setState('rec');
    banner('Reconnecting…', 'Found a signal');
    later(function () { setState('back'); flush(); }, rm ? 300 : 1300);
  }
  function goOffline() { clear(); setState('off'); offText(); }
  sw.addEventListener('click', function () { if (state === 'off') reconnect(); else goOffline(); });
  function sync() { send.setAttribute('aria-disabled', String(!inp.value.trim())); }
  inp.addEventListener('input', sync);
  inp.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter') return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!e.isComposing && e.keyCode !== 229) form.requestSubmit();
  });
  form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var v = inp.value.trim();
    if (!v) return;
    inp.value = ''; sync();
    if (state === 'off' || state === 'rec') {
      row(v, true, true);
      if (state === 'off') offText();
    } else {
      min++;
      var r = row(v, true, false);
      r.querySelector('.u091-meta span').textContent = 'Sending';
      later(function () { mark(r, false); }, rm ? 0 : 450);
    }
  });
  function onNet() {
    if (!root.isConnected) { window.removeEventListener('online', onNet); window.removeEventListener('offline', onNet); return; }
    if (navigator.onLine) { if (state === 'off') reconnect(); } else if (state !== 'off') goOffline();
  }
  window.addEventListener('online', onNet);
  window.addEventListener('offline', onNet);
  offText();
})();
</script>

What it does: Swipe a message right and the reply arrow fills; let go and a quote line rises above the composer. Typing turns the mic into a send button, and the sent bubble rises out of the composer before the other person’s typing dots and reply arrive.

<div class="ux u078">
  <div class="u078-top"><i aria-hidden="true"></i><span><b>Harin Yoon</b><small>Active now</small></span></div>
  <div class="u078-log" aria-live="polite"></div>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var log = root.querySelector('.u078-log'), reply = root.querySelector('.u078-reply'), rb = reply.querySelector('b'), rt = reply.querySelector('em'), rx = root.querySelector('.u078-rx');
  var form = root.querySelector('.u078-bar'), input = root.querySelector('.u078-in'), send = root.querySelector('.u078-send');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var target = null, ts = [];
  var ARROW = '<svg viewBox="0 0 14 14"><path d="M5.5 3.5L2 7l3.5 3.5M2 7h6.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 3.5 3.5"/></svg>';
  function esc(s) { return s.replace(/[&<>]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;' }[c]; }); }
  function bubble(text, me, quote, who) {
    var m = document.createElement('div');
    m.className = 'u078-m in' + (me ? ' me' : '');
    m.innerHTML = '<span class="u078-rep" aria-hidden="true">' + ARROW + '</span><span class="u078-bub">' + (quote ? '<span class="u078-q">' + esc(quote) + '</span>' : '') + esc(text) + '</span>' + (me ? '' : '<button class="u078-rb" type="button" aria-label="Reply to this message">' + ARROW + '</button>');
    m.setAttribute('data-t', text); m.setAttribute('data-w', who || (me ? 'Me' : 'Harin Yoon'));
    log.appendChild(m);
    m.addEventListener('animationend', function () { m.classList.remove('in'); });
    if (!me) wire(m);
    while (log.children.length > 7) log.firstChild.remove();
    return m;
  }
  function setReply(m) {
    target = m;
    rb.textContent = 'Replying to ' + m.getAttribute('data-w');
    rt.textContent = m.getAttribute('data-t');
    reply.classList.add('on'); rx.tabIndex = 0;
    input.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }
  function clearReply() { target = null; reply.classList.remove('on'); rx.tabIndex = -1; }
  function wire(m) {
    var bub = m.querySelector('.u078-bub'), rep = m.querySelector('.u078-rep'), sx = 0, dx = 0, down = false;
    bub.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { if (e.button !== 0) return; down = true; sx = e.clientX; dx = 0; bub.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); m.classList.add('drag'); });
    bub.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
      if (!down) return;
      dx = Math.max(0, e.clientX - sx); var d = dx > 60 ? 60 + (dx - 60) * 0.3 : dx;
      bub.style.setProperty('--dx', d + 'px'); m.style.setProperty('--ro', Math.min(1, dx / 60).toFixed(2));
      rep.classList.toggle('full', dx >= 60);
    });
    var end = function () { if (!down) return; down = false; m.classList.remove('drag'); bub.style.setProperty('--dx', '0px'); m.style.setProperty('--ro', 0); rep.classList.remove('full'); if (dx >= 60) setReply(m); };
    bub.addEventListener('pointerup', end); bub.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
    m.querySelector('.u078-rb').addEventListener('click', function () { setReply(m); });
  }
  input.addEventListener('input', function () {
    var on = !!input.value.trim();
    send.classList.toggle('go', on);
    send.setAttribute('aria-label', on ? 'Send' : 'Voice message');
  });
  rx.addEventListener('click', function () { clearReply(); input.focus(); });
  input.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && target) { e.preventDefault(); clearReply(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.isComposing && e.keyCode !== 229) { e.preventDefault(); form.requestSubmit ? form.requestSubmit() : form.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { cancelable: true })); }
  });
  form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var v = input.value.trim();
    if (!v) { if (!rm) send.animate([{ transform: 'scale(1)' }, { transform: 'scale(1.15)' }, { transform: 'scale(1)' }], { duration: 300 }); return; }
    bubble(v, true, target && target.getAttribute('data-t'));
    input.value = ''; send.classList.remove('go'); send.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Voice message');
    clearReply();
    ts.forEach(clearTimeout); ts = [];
    var old = log.querySelector('.u078-ty'); if (old) old.remove();
    ts.push(setTimeout(function () {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      var ty = document.createElement('div'); ty.className = 'u078-ty u078-m in'; ty.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Harin Yoon is typing'); ty.innerHTML = '<i></i><i></i><i></i>';
      log.appendChild(ty);
      ts.push(setTimeout(function () { if (!root.isConnected) return; ty.remove(); bubble(['Great, let’s do that!', 'Got it 👍', 'Oh, that’s nice'][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)], false); }, rm ? 300 : 1500));
    }, rm ? 100 : 500));
  });
  bubble('Draft v3 is up! I cut the button transition to 0.3s', false).classList.remove('in');
  bubble('Feels much lighter', true).classList.remove('in');
  bubble('Could you check dark mode too?', false).classList.remove('in');
})();
</script>

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #032 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#032 -->

Element: Drag-and-drop upload

What it does: Drag files in and the dashed border starts marching as the area grows; each dropped file fills its own progress bar in the list below. Finished files draw a check, and failed ones can be retried.

.u032{position:relative;width:340px;height:420px}

<div class="ux u032">
  <button class="u032-zone" type="button">
    <svg class="u032-dash" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100%" height="100%" rx="25"/></svg>
    <span class="u032-orb" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 28 28"><path d="M8.5 20.5H7a5 5 0 0 1-.6-10 7 7 0 0 1 13.4-1.6A5.5 5.5 0 0 1 21 20.5h-1.5M14 13v10M10.5 16.5L14 13l3.5 3.5"/></svg></span>

  <input class="u032-file" type="file" multiple tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">
  <ul class="u032-list" aria-live="polite"></ul>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var zone = root.querySelector('.u032-zone');
  var pick = root.querySelector('.u032-file');
  var list = root.querySelector('.u032-list');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var TYPE = { pdf: '#E5484D', png: '#3D8BFF', jpg: '#22A06B', jpeg: '#22A06B', fig: '#8B6CFF' };
  function size(b) { return b > 1048576 ? (b / 1048576).toFixed(1) + ' MB' : Math.max(1, Math.round(b / 1024)) + ' KB'; }
  function run(row, bytes, fail) {
    row.className = 'u032-row';
    var st = row.querySelector('.u032-st');
    var sm = row.querySelector('small');
    st.innerHTML = '<span class="u032-spin" aria-hidden="true"></span>';
    var p = 0, t0 = performance.now(), D = rm ? 300 : 1800 + Math.random() * 900;
    (function f(now) {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      p = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / D);
      var shown = fail ? Math.min(p, 0.62) : p;
      row.style.setProperty('--p', shown.toFixed(3));
      sm.textContent = size(bytes * shown) + ' / ' + size(bytes) + ' · ' + Math.round(shown * 100) + '%';
      if (fail && p >= 0.62) {
        row.classList.add('fail');
        sm.textContent = 'Connection lost · stopped at ' + Math.round(shown * 100) + '%';
        st.outerHTML = '<button class="u032-retry" type="button">Retry</button>';
        row.querySelector('.u032-retry').addEventListener('click', function () { var r = row.querySelector('.u032-retry'); r.outerHTML = '<span class="u032-st"></span>'; run(row, bytes, false); });
        return;
      }
      if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f);
      else { row.classList.add('done'); sm.textContent = size(bytes) + ' · Uploaded'; st.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3.5 8.4l3 3 6-6.4"/></svg>'; }
    })(t0);
  }
  function add(name, bytes, fail) {
    var ext = (name.split('.').pop() || '').toLowerCase();
    var li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'u032-row';
    li.innerHTML = '<span class="u032-ic" style="--c:' + (TYPE[ext] || '#8E8A97') + '" aria-hidden="true">' + (ext.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase() || 'FILE') + '</span><span class="u032-meta"><b></b><small></small></span><span class="u032-st"></span><span class="u032-bar" aria-hidden="true"></span>';
    li.querySelector('b').textContent = name;
    list.insertBefore(li, list.firstChild);
    while (list.children.length > 3) list.lastElementChild.remove();
    run(li, bytes, fail);
  }
  zone.addEventListener('click', function () { pick.click(); });
  pick.addEventListener('change', function () {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(pick.files, function (f) { add(f.name, f.size, false); });
    pick.value = '';
  });
  var depth = 0;
  zone.addEventListener('dragenter', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); depth++; root.classList.add('is-over'); });
  zone.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
  zone.addEventListener('dragleave', function () { if (--depth <= 0) { depth = 0; root.classList.remove('is-over'); } });
  zone.addEventListener('drop', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault(); depth = 0; root.classList.remove('is-over');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(e.dataTransfer.files, function (f) { add(f.name, f.size, false); });
  });
  add('brand-guide.pdf', 18.4 * 1048576, true);
  add('main-banner@2x.png', 4.2 * 1048576, false);
})();
</script>

.u075-st{width:30px;height:30px;flex:none;display:grid;place-items:center}
.u075-st svg{width:18px;height:18px;fill:none;stroke:var(--ux-mint);stroke-width:2.8;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round}
.u075-st path{stroke-dasharray:18;stroke-dashoffset:18;transition:stroke-dashoffset .35s var(--ux-ease)}
.u075-f.ok .u075-st path{stroke-dashoffset:0}
.u075-retry{width:30px;height:30px;border:0;padding:0;border-radius:9px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ux-danger) 12%,transparent);color:var(--ux-danger);animation:u075-in .4s var(--ux-spring)}
.u075-retry svg{stroke:currentColor;width:16px;height:16px}
.u075-retry path{stroke-dasharray:none;stroke-dashoffset:0}
.u075-retry:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--ux-accent);outline-offset:2px}
@keyframes u075-in{from{transform:scale(0)}}
</style>
<div class="ux u075">
  <button class="u075-add" type="button"><svg viewBox="0 0 18 18" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9 12.5V3.5M5 7l4-4 4 4M3 13v1.5h12V13"/></svg>Upload files</button>
  <section class="u075-pan" aria-label="Uploads" aria-hidden="true">
    <button class="u075-hd" type="button" aria-expanded="true" tabindex="-1"><span class="u075-ring" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 38 38"><circle class="t" cx="19" cy="19" r="16"/><circle class="p" cx="19" cy="19" r="16" pathLength="100"/></svg><i><svg viewBox="0 0 18 18"><path d="M4 9.4l3.2 3.2L14 5.6"/></svg></i></span><span class="u075-ht" aria-live="polite"><b></b><small></small></span><svg class="u075-chev" viewBox="0 0 18 18" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 7l4 4 4-4"/></svg></button>
    <div class="u075-body"><ul></ul></div>
  </section>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var add = root.querySelector('.u075-add'), pan = root.querySelector('.u075-pan'), hd = root.querySelector('.u075-hd'), ul = root.querySelector('.u075-body ul');
  var hb = hd.querySelector('b'), hs = hd.querySelector('small');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var F = [['Brand guide.pdf', 12.4, 'PDF', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FF6B8B,#E5484D)', 1.6], ['Main video.mp4', 48, 'MP4', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#7B61FF,#3D8BFF)', 0.9], ['Icon set.zip', 3.2, 'ZIP', 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FFB547,#FF7A45)', 2.4]];
  var files = [], raf = 0, last = 0, failed = false;
  function build() {
    ul.innerHTML = '';
    files = F.map(function (f) {
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'u075-f';
      li.innerHTML = '<span class="u075-ic" style="--c:' + f[3] + '" aria-hidden="true">' + f[2] + '</span><span class="u075-fx"><b><span></span><small></small></b><span class="u075-bar" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"><i></i></span></span><span class="u075-st"><svg viewBox="0 0 18 18" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 9.4l3.2 3.2L14 5.6"/></svg></span>';
      li.querySelector('b span').textContent = f[0];
      li.querySelector('.u075-bar').setAttribute('aria-label', f[0] + ' progress');
      ul.appendChild(li);
      return { li: li, p: 0, size: f[1], speed: f[4], state: 'up' };
    });
  }
  function paint() {
    var tot = 0, done = 0, remain = 0, active = 0;
    files.forEach(function (f) {
      tot += f.size; done += f.size * f.p;
      f.li.querySelector('.u075-bar').style.setProperty('--p', f.p.toFixed(3));
      f.li.querySelector('.u075-bar').setAttribute('aria-valuenow', Math.round(f.p * 100));
      f.li.querySelector('small').textContent = f.state === 'bad' ? 'Failed · ' + Math.round(f.p * 100) + '%' : f.state === 'ok' ? f.size + 'MB' : Math.round(f.p * 100) + '%';
      if (f.state === 'up') { active++; remain = Math.max(remain, (1 - f.p) / (f.speed * 0.18)); }
    });
    var all = done / tot, ok = files.filter(function (f) { return f.state === 'ok'; }).length, bad = files.filter(function (f) { return f.state === 'bad'; }).length;
    hd.querySelector('.u075-ring').style.setProperty('--p', all.toFixed(3));
    root.classList.toggle('is-done', ok === files.length);
    if (ok === files.length) { hb.textContent = 'Uploaded ' + ok + ' files'; hs.textContent = 'Total ' + tot.toFixed(1) + 'MB'; }
    else if (active) { hb.textContent = 'Uploading ' + active + (active === 1 ? ' file' : ' files'); hs.textContent = Math.round(all * 100) + '% · about ' + Math.max(1, Math.ceil(remain)) + 's left'; }
    else if (bad) { hb.textContent = bad + (bad === 1 ? ' file failed' : ' files failed'); hs.textContent = 'Press retry to resume'; }
  }
  function loop(now) {
    if (!root.isConnected) return;
    var dt = Math.min(64, now - last) / 1000; last = now;
    var running = false;
    files.forEach(function (f) {
      if (f.state !== 'up') return;
      running = true;
      f.p = Math.min(1, f.p + dt * f.speed * 0.18 * (rm ? 4 : 1));
      if (!failed && f.size === 48 && f.p >= 0.64) { failed = true; f.state = 'bad'; f.li.classList.add('bad'); retryBtn(f); }
      else if (f.p >= 1) { f.state = 'ok'; f.li.classList.add('ok'); }
    });
    paint();
    if (running) raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    else add.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'false');
  }
  function retryBtn(f) {
    var st = f.li.querySelector('.u075-st'), b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u075-retry'; b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Retry Main video');
    b.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M13 8a5 5 0 1 1-1.5-3.6M13 2.5v2.5h-2.5"/></svg>';
    st.innerHTML = ''; st.appendChild(b);
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      f.state = 'up'; f.li.classList.remove('bad');
      st.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 18 18" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 9.4l3.2 3.2L14 5.6"/></svg>';
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf); last = performance.now(); raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
      hd.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    });
  }
  add.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (add.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true') return;
    add.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    failed = false; build();
    root.classList.add('is-on'); root.classList.remove('is-fold');
    pan.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false'); hd.tabIndex = 0; hd.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    cancelAnimationFrame(raf); last = performance.now(); raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  });
  hd.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var fold = !root.classList.contains('is-fold');
    root.classList.toggle('is-fold', fold);
    hd.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!fold));
  });
  add.click();
})();
</script>

  <nav class="u250-nv"><a href="#" aria-current="page">General</a><a href="#">Orders</a><a href="#">Products</a></nav>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var inp = root.querySelector('.u250-in'), sw = root.querySelector('.u250-sw'), cs = root.querySelector('.u250-cs'), br = root.querySelector('.u250-br'), tx = root.querySelector('.u250-tx'), cx = root.querySelector('.u250-cx'), sv = root.querySelector('.u250-sv');
  var COL = ['#E11D48', '#F59E0B', '#10B981', '#6366F1'];
  var C = COL.map(function (c, i) { var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u250-cl'; b.setAttribute('role', 'radio'); b.style.background = c; b.setAttribute('aria-label', c + ' color'); b.addEventListener('click', function () { now.color = i; paint(); diff(); }); cs.appendChild(b); return b; });
  var saved = { name: inp.value, alert: false, color: 0 }, now = { name: inp.value, alert: false, color: 0 };
  function paint() { inp.value = now.name; sw.setAttribute('aria-checked', now.alert); C.forEach(function (b, i) { b.setAttribute('aria-checked', i === now.color); }); }
  function changed() { return ['name', 'alert', 'color'].filter(function (k) { return now[k] !== saved[k]; }); }
  function diff() {
    var n = changed().length;
    br.classList.remove('is-ok', 'is-warn');
    tx.textContent = 'Unsaved changes: ' + n + '';
    root.classList.toggle('is-dirty', n > 0); cx.tabIndex = sv.tabIndex = n ? 0 : -1;
  }
  inp.addEventListener('input', function () { now.name = inp.value; diff(); });
  sw.addEventListener('click', function () { now.alert = !now.alert; paint(); diff(); });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u250-nv a'), function (a) {
    a.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (!changed().length) return;
      tx.textContent = 'Save or discard first';
      br.classList.remove('is-warn'); void br.offsetWidth; br.classList.add('is-warn');
      setTimeout(function () { if (br.classList.contains('is-warn')) diff(); }, 1600);
      sv.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    });
  });
  cx.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var ch = changed(); now = { name: saved.name, alert: saved.alert, color: saved.color }; paint();
    ch.forEach(function (k) { var el = k === 'name' ? inp : k === 'alert' ? sw : C[now.color]; el.classList.remove('is-flash'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('is-flash'); });
    diff();
  });
  sv.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (sv.classList.contains('is-busy')) return;
    sv.classList.add('is-busy');
    setTimeout(function () {
      sv.classList.remove('is-busy'); saved = { name: now.name, alert: now.alert, color: now.color };
      br.classList.remove('is-warn'); br.classList.add('is-ok'); tx.textContent = 'Saved';
      setTimeout(function () { root.classList.remove('is-dirty'); cx.tabIndex = sv.tabIndex = -1; }, 900);
    }, 750);
  });
  paint(); diff();
})();
</script>

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #355 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#355 -->
<!--
Element: Cloud autosave indicator
ID: 355 · Category: Forms & Input · Tags: autosave, status, editor, offline, sync
What it does: Start typing and three dots bob inside a cloud to show ‘Saving’; pause and they turn into a check that reads ‘Saved just now’. As time passes it switches to relative times like ‘Saved 10s ago’, and going offline draws a slash through the cloud with ‘Saved on this device’. Reconnect and a spinning arrow syncs it back.
How to use it: Type in the body, and toggle offline with the switch below
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#355
-->
<style>
.u355{position:relative;width:340px;height:330px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:16px;border-radius:28px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 30px 60px -36px var(--ux-shadow-strong);overflow:hidden}
.u355-hd{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}
.u355-tt{flex:1;min-width:0;height:34px;border:0;background:none;font-size:18px;font-weight:850;letter-spacing:-.03em;color:var(--ux-ink);outline:none}

.u355-st svg{width:24px;height:20px;overflow:visible}
.u355-cl{fill:none;stroke:currentColor;stroke-width:1.7;stroke-linejoin:round}
.u355-d{fill:currentColor;opacity:0}
.u355[data-s="saving"] .u355-d{opacity:1;animation:u355-bob .9s ease-in-out infinite}
.u355[data-s="saving"] .u355-d:nth-of-type(2){animation-delay:.15s}
.u355[data-s="saving"] .u355-d:nth-of-type(3){animation-delay:.3s}
@keyframes u355-bob{50%{transform:translateY(-2.5px)}}
.u355-ok{fill:none;stroke:var(--ux-mint);stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round;stroke-dasharray:12;stroke-dashoffset:12;transition:stroke-dashoffset .35s var(--ux-ease)}
.u355[data-s="saved"] .u355-ok{stroke-dashoffset:0}
.u355-sl{stroke:var(--ux-warn);stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-dasharray:30;stroke-dashoffset:30;transition:stroke-dashoffset .35s var(--ux-ease)}
.u355[data-s="local"] .u355-sl{stroke-dashoffset:0}
.u355[data-s="local"] .u355-st{background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ux-warn) 14%,transparent);color:var(--ux-warn)}
.u355-sy{fill:none;stroke:var(--ux-accent);stroke-width:1.8;stroke-linecap:round;opacity:0;transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:50% 50%}

.u355-ft{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:10px}

.u355-off{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;border:0;background:none;padding:0;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ux-ink-2)}
.u355-off i{position:relative;width:36px;height:22px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--ux-surface-3);transition:background-color .3s}

.u355-off[aria-pressed="true"] i{background:var(--ux-warn)}
.u355-off[aria-pressed="true"] i::after{transform:translateX(14px)}
</style>
<div class="ux u355" data-s="saved">
  <div class="u355-hd">
    <input class="u355-tt" type="text" value="Launch meeting" aria-label="Title">
    <span class="u355-st" role="status"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 20" aria-hidden="true"><path class="u355-cl" d="M7 16.5h10.5a4 4 0 0 0 .6-7.95A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 7.4 7.6 4.5 4.5 0 0 0 7 16.5z"/><circle class="u355-d" cx="9" cy="12" r="1.2"/><circle class="u355-d" cx="12.5" cy="12" r="1.2"/><circle class="u355-d" cx="16" cy="12" r="1.2"/><path class="u355-ok" d="M9.3 12l2.2 2.1 4-4.2"/><path class="u355-sl" d="M4 2l17 17"/><path class="u355-sy" d="M15.5 10.5a3.4 3.4 0 1 1-1-2.4M15.5 6.8v1.8h-1.8"/></svg><span class="u355-tx">Saved just now</span></span>
  </div>
  <textarea class="u355-ta" aria-label="Body">Notes for next week’s launch meeting.</textarea>
  <div class="u355-ft"><span class="u355-all">All saved</span><button class="u355-off" type="button" aria-pressed="false"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>Work offline</button></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var tx = root.querySelector('.u355-tx'), ta = root.querySelector('.u355-ta'), tt = root.querySelector('.u355-tt'), off = root.querySelector('.u355-off');
  var offline = false, savedAt = Date.now(), t1 = 0, t2 = 0, dirty = false;
  function state(s, text) { root.setAttribute('data-s', s); tx.textContent = text; }
  function ago() {
    var s = Math.floor((Date.now() - savedAt) / 1000);
    if (s < 10) return 'Saved just now';
    if (s < 60) return 'Saved ' + s + 's ago';
    return 'Saved ' + Math.floor(s / 60) + ' min ago';
  }
  function edited() {
    clearTimeout(t1); clearTimeout(t2);
    if (offline) { dirty = true; state('local', 'Saved on this device'); return; }
    state('saving', 'Saving');
    t1 = setTimeout(function () {
      t2 = setTimeout(function () { if (!root.isConnected) return; savedAt = Date.now(); state('saved', ago()); }, 900);
    }, 500);
  }
  ta.addEventListener('input', edited);
  tt.addEventListener('input', edited);
  off.addEventListener('click', function () {
    offline = !offline;
    off.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(offline));
    clearTimeout(t1); clearTimeout(t2);
    if (offline) { state('local', 'Saved on this device'); return; }
    state('sync', 'Syncing');
    t2 = setTimeout(function () { if (!root.isConnected) return; dirty = false; savedAt = Date.now(); state('saved', ago()); }, 1300);
  });
  var iv = setInterval(function () {
    if (!root.isConnected) { clearInterval(iv); return; }
    if (root.getAttribute('data-s') === 'saved') tx.textContent = ago();
  }, 1000);
})();
</script>

Element: Error widget that plugs back in
ID: 090 · Category: Empty & Error States · Tags: error, retry, backoff, countdown, recovery
What it does: A widget that failed to load waits with its plug pulled, while the ring around the button shrinks to show the time until it retries on its own. <script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var uid = 'u090' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  var pill = root.querySelector('.u090-pill'), cd = root.querySelector('.u090-cd'), dots = root.querySelectorAll('.u090-dots i'), err = root.querySelector('.u090-err');
  var more = root.querySelector('.u090-more'), det = root.querySelector('.u090-det'), code = det.querySelector('code'), okv = root.querySelector('.u090-ok'), sum = root.querySelector('.u090-sum b');
  var go = root.querySelector('.u090-go'), lab = go.querySelector('span'), ring = root.querySelector('.u090-ring circle+circle'), bars = root.querySelector('.u090-bars');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  det.id = uid + '-det'; more.setAttribute('aria-controls', det.id);
  [['Mon', .42], ['Tue', .55], ['Wed', .38], ['Thu', .7], ['Fri', .64], ['Sat', .88], ['Sun', .76]].forEach(function (d, i) {
    var s = document.createElement('span');
    s.innerHTML = '<i style="--h:' + d[1] + ';--d:' + i + '"></i><em>' + d[0] + '</em>';
    bars.appendChild(s);
  });
  var ts = [], tries = 0, st = 'wait';
  function clear() { ts.forEach(clearTimeout); ts = []; }
  function later(fn, ms) { ts.push(setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) fn(); }, ms)); }
  function setS(s, txt, c) { st = s; root.setAttribute('data-s', s); pill.textContent = txt; pill.style.setProperty('--c', c); go.setAttribute('aria-disabled', String(s === 'try')); }
  function detail() { code.textContent = 'GET /api/sales/week\n503 Service Unavailable\nRequest ID 7f3a-c21e · attempt ' + (tries + 1) + '/3'; }
  function countdown(sec) {
    clear();
    setS('wait', 'Failed to load', 'var(--ux-danger)');
    lab.textContent = 'Retry now';
    ring.style.transition = 'none'; ring.style.strokeDashoffset = 0; ring.getBoundingClientRect();
    ring.style.transition = 'stroke-dashoffset ' + sec + 's linear'; ring.style.strokeDashoffset = 100;
    var left = sec;
    (function t() {
      if (left <= 0) return attempt();
      cd.textContent = 'Retrying automatically in ' + left + 's';
      left--; later(t, 1000);
    })();
  }
  function attempt() {
    if (st !== 'wait') return;
    clear(); tries++;
    setS('try', 'Reconnecting', 'var(--ux-warn)');
    lab.textContent = 'Connecting';
    cd.textContent = 'Attempt ' + (tries + 1) + '…';
    dots[tries].className = 'cur';
    later(function () {
      if (tries === 1) {
        dots[tries].className = 'f';
        root.classList.remove('zap'); void root.offsetWidth; root.classList.add('zap');
        if (!rm) err.animate([{ transform: 'translateX(0)' }, { transform: 'translateX(-7px)' }, { transform: 'translateX(6px)' }, { transform: 'translateX(-3px)' }, { transform: 'none' }], { duration: 420, easing: 'ease-out' });
        detail();
        countdown(8);
      } else {
        dots[tries].className = 'ok';
        setS('ok', 'OK', 'var(--ux-mint)');
        cd.textContent = 'Connected on attempt 3';
        lab.textContent = 'Replay the error';
        later(reveal, rm ? 0 : 750);
      }
    }, rm ? 400 : 1500);
  }
  function reveal() {
    var hadFocus = err.contains(document.activeElement);
    root.classList.add('show');
    okv.removeAttribute('aria-hidden'); err.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    if (hadFocus) go.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    var t0 = performance.now(), dur = rm ? 1 : 900;
    (function f(now) {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      var k = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3);
      sum.textContent = '₩' + (48.2 * e).toFixed(1) + 'M';
      if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(f);
    })(t0);
  }
  function reset() {
    clear(); tries = 0;
    root.classList.remove('show', 'zap');
    okv.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); err.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
    dots.forEach(function (d, i) { d.className = i === 0 ? 'f' : ''; });
    detail();
    countdown(5);
  }
  go.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (st === 'wait') attempt();
    else if (st === 'ok') reset();
  });
  more.addEventListener('click', function () { more.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(more.getAttribute('aria-expanded') !== 'true')); });
  detail();
  countdown(5);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var ls = root.querySelector('.u238-ls');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var S = [['API', 99.98], ['Dashboard', 100], ['Payments', 99.71], ['Webhooks', 99.95]];
  var INC = {0: [[11, 'Slow responses', '12 min']], 2: [[18, 'Some card payments failed', '41 min'], [26, 'Payment confirmation delayed', '9 min']], 3: [[22, 'Retry delays', '15 min']]};
  var tt = document.createElement('div'); tt.className = 'u238-tt'; tt.setAttribute('role', 'tooltip'); root.appendChild(tt);
  S.forEach(function (s, si) {
    var it = document.createElement('div'); it.className = 'u238-it';
    it.innerHTML = '<div class="u238-tp"><button type="button" class="u238-nm" aria-expanded="false"><svg viewBox="0 0 9 9" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 1.5l3 3-3 3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg><span></span></button><em></em></div><div class="u238-bar"></div><div class="u238-ax"><span></span><span></span></div><div class="u238-hx"><div></div></div>';
    it.querySelector('.u238-nm span').textContent = s[0]; it.querySelector('em').textContent = s[1] + '%';
    var ax = it.querySelectorAll('.u238-ax span'); ax[0].textContent = '30 days ago'; ax[1].textContent = 'Today';
    var bar = it.querySelector('.u238-bar'), inc = INC[si] || [], B = [];
    for (var d = 0; d < 30; d++) {
      var hit = inc.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === d; })[0], b = document.createElement('i');
      if (hit) b.className = parseInt(hit[2], 10) > 30 ? 'e' : 'w';
      b.style.transitionDelay = (d * 0.018 + si * 0.06) + 's'; b._hit = hit; b._d = d;
      bar.appendChild(b); B.push(b);
    }
    function show(x) {
      var r = bar.getBoundingClientRect(), d = Math.max(0, Math.min(29, Math.floor((x - r.left) / r.width * 30))), b = B[d];
      B.forEach(function (o) { o.classList.toggle('is-hl', o !== b); });
      var ago = 29 - d, up = b._hit ? (100 - parseInt(b._hit[2], 10) / 14.4).toFixed(2) : '100.00';
      tt.innerHTML = '<b></b><span></span>'; tt.firstChild.textContent = ago ? ago + ' days ago' : 'Today';
      tt.lastChild.textContent = b._hit ? b._hit[1] + ' · ' + b._hit[2] : 'Uptime ' + up + '%' + ' · No incidents';
      var rr = root.getBoundingClientRect(), k = 340 / (rr.width || 340), br = b.getBoundingClientRect();
      tt.style.left = Math.max(70, Math.min(270, (br.left + br.width / 2 - rr.left) * k)) + 'px'; tt.style.top = ((br.top - rr.top) * k - 8) + 'px'; tt.style.bottom = 'auto'; tt.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-100%)';
      tt.classList.add('is-on');
    }
    bar.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { show(e.clientX); });
    bar.addEventListener('pointerover', function (e) { show(e.clientX); });
    bar.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { tt.classList.remove('is-on'); B.forEach(function (o) { o.classList.remove('is-hl'); }); });
    var hx = it.querySelector('.u238-hx>div');
    if (!inc.length) { var p0 = document.createElement('p'); p0.innerHTML = '<i style="background:#10B981"></i><span></span>'; p0.lastChild.textContent = 'No incidents in the last 30 days'; hx.appendChild(p0); }
    inc.slice().reverse().forEach(function (x) { var p = document.createElement('p'); p.innerHTML = '<i style="background:' + (parseInt(x[2], 10) > 30 ? '#E11D48' : '#F59E0B') + '"></i><span></span><small></small>'; p.querySelector('span').textContent = (29 - x[0]) + ' days ago · ' + x[1] + ' (' + x[2] + ')'; p.querySelector('small').textContent = 'Resolved'; hx.appendChild(p); });
    var nm = it.querySelector('.u238-nm');
    nm.addEventListener('click', function () { var o = !it.classList.contains('is-open'); it.classList.toggle('is-open', o); nm.setAttribute('aria-expanded', o); });
    ls.appendChild(it);
  });
  if (rm || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) root.classList.add('is-in');
  else { var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { if (es[0].isIntersecting) { io.disconnect(); requestAnimationFrame(function () { root.classList.add('is-in'); }); } }); io.observe(root); }
})();
</script>

Element: Command palette

What it does: The search pill unfolds downward into a palette; as you type, results filter with the matching letters highlighted. The highlight bar glides to the chosen item, and running it folds the palette back into a pill.
How to use it: Type to filter commands. Choose with the up and down arrows, run with Enter and fold it with Esc. Click the folded pill or press Ctrl/⌘+K to reopen it

.u046{position:relative;width:360px;height:460px}

.u046-field{position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;height:56px;flex:none;padding:0 14px 0 18px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ux-line);cursor:text;transition:border-color .3s}

<div class="ux u046">
  <div class="u046-pal">
    <label class="u046-field">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="M16 16l4.5 4.5"/></svg>

  </div>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var input = root.querySelector('.u046-in'), list = root.querySelector('.u046-list');
  var hl = root.querySelector('.u046-hl'), key = root.querySelector('.u046-key');
  var done = root.querySelector('.u046-done'), field = root.querySelector('.u046-field');
  var body = root.querySelector('.u046-body');
  var uid = 'u046-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  var mac = /Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform || '');
  var I = {
    doc: '<path d="M7 3.5h7l4 4V20a.5.5 0 0 1-.5.5h-10A.5.5 0 0 1 7 20z"/><path d="M14 3.5v4h4"/>',
    plus: '<path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/>',
    moon: '<path d="M19 14.5A7.5 7.5 0 0 1 9.5 5a7.5 7.5 0 1 0 9.5 9.5z"/>',
    user: '<circle cx="10" cy="8.5" r="3.5"/><path d="M3.5 19.5c.8-3.4 3.4-5 6.5-5 1.6 0 3 .4 4.2 1.3M18 14v6M15 17h6"/>',
    out: '<path d="M12 15V4M8 8l4-4 4 4M5 14v5a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h12a1 1 0 0 0 1-1v-5"/>'
  };
  var DATA = [
    { g: 'Recent files', n: 'Brand guide 2026', ic: 'doc', c: 'linear-gradient(140deg,#8B6CFF,#5B8CFF)' },
    { g: 'Recent files', n: 'Spring campaign landing', ic: 'doc', c: 'linear-gradient(140deg,#FFB547,#FF7A45)' },
    { g: 'Recent files', n: 'Quarterly report draft', ic: 'doc', c: 'linear-gradient(140deg,#22C3A6,#0EA5E9)' },
    { g: 'Commands', n: 'Create new document', ic: 'plus', k: mac ? '⌘N' : 'Ctrl N' },
    { g: 'Commands', n: 'Toggle dark mode', ic: 'moon', k: mac ? '⌘⇧L' : 'Ctrl ⇧L' },
    { g: 'Commands', n: 'Invite teammates', ic: 'user' },
    { g: 'Commands', n: 'Export as PDF', ic: 'out', k: mac ? '⌘E' : 'Ctrl E' }
  ];
  var opts = [], act = 0, closed = false, doneT = 0;
  function esc(s) { return s.replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; }); }
  function hit(name, q) {
    if (!q) return esc(name);
    var i = name.toLowerCase().indexOf(q);
    return esc(name.slice(0, i)) + '<mark>' + esc(name.slice(i, i + q.length)) + '</mark>' + esc(name.slice(i + q.length));
  }
  function render() {
    var q = input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    var found = DATA.filter(function (d) { return !q || d.n.toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0; });
    var html = '', g = '', n = 0;
    found.forEach(function (d) {
      if (d.g !== g) { g = d.g; html += '<li class="u046-g" role="presentation">' + g + '</li>'; }
      html += '<li class="u046-op" role="option" id="' + uid + '-' + n + '" data-n="' + esc(d.n) + '" style="--i:' + n + '" aria-selected="false"><span class="u046-ic' + (d.c ? '' : ' plain') + '"' + (d.c ? ' style="--c:' + d.c + '"' : '') + ' aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">' + I[d.ic] + '</svg></span><span>' + hit(d.n, q) + '</span>' + (d.k ? '<kbd class="u046-kbd">' + d.k + '</kbd>' : '') + '</li>';
      n++;
    });
    if (!n) html = '<li class="u046-empty" role="presentation"><b>No results for ‘' + esc(input.value.trim()) + '’</b>Try another word</li>';
    list.innerHTML = html;
    opts = Array.prototype.slice.call(list.querySelectorAll('.u046-op'));
    opts.forEach(function (o, i) {
      o.addEventListener('pointermove', function () { if (act !== i) mark(i); });
      o.addEventListener('click', function () { mark(i); run(); });
    });
    hl.style.opacity = n ? '1' : '0';
    mark(0, true);
  }
  function mark(i, instant) {
    if (!opts.length) { input.removeAttribute('aria-activedescendant'); return; }
    act = (i + opts.length) % opts.length;
    opts.forEach(function (o, k) { o.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(k === act)); });
    input.setAttribute('aria-activedescendant', opts[act].id);
    if (instant) { hl.style.transition = 'none'; }
    var o = opts[act], top = o.offsetTop + 6;
    hl.style.setProperty('--y', top + 'px');
    if (instant) { void hl.offsetWidth; hl.style.transition = ''; }
    if (top + o.offsetHeight > body.scrollTop + body.clientHeight) body.scrollTo({ top: top + o.offsetHeight - body.clientHeight + 6, behavior: instant ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
    else if (top < body.scrollTop + 30) body.scrollTo({ top: act === 0 ? 0 : top - 30, behavior: instant ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
  }
  function run() {
    if (!opts[act]) return;
    var name = opts[act].getAttribute('data-n');
    opts[act].animate([{ transform: 'scale(.97)' }, { transform: 'none' }], { duration: 260, easing: 'ease-out' });
    done.textContent = 'Ran ‘' + name + '’';
    done.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(doneT);
    doneT = setTimeout(function () { if (root.isConnected) setClosed(true); }, 650);
  }
  function setClosed(v) {
    closed = v;
    root.classList.toggle('is-closed', v);
    input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!v));
    key.textContent = v ? (mac ? '⌘K' : 'Ctrl K') : 'esc';
    if (v) { input.value = ''; input.blur(); setTimeout(function () { if (closed) { done.classList.remove('show'); render(); } }, 500); }
    else { done.classList.remove('show'); input.focus(); }
  }
  input.addEventListener('input', function () { if (closed) setClosed(false); render(); });
  input.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); if (closed) setClosed(false); else mark(act + (e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1)); }
    else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.isComposing && e.keyCode !== 229) { e.preventDefault(); if (closed) setClosed(false); else run(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Escape' && !closed) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); setClosed(true); }
  });
  field.addEventListener('pointerdown', function () { if (closed) { clearTimeout(doneT); setClosed(false); } });
  function onKey(e) {
    if (!root.isConnected) { document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey); return; }
    if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K')) {
      var a = document.activeElement;
      if (a && a !== document.body && !root.contains(a)) return;
      e.preventDefault(); setClosed(!closed);
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  render();
})();
</script>

    <small>Lumen Design</small>
    <h3>Home</h3>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var tog = root.querySelector('.u040-tog');
  var its = Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('.u040-it'));
  var hl = root.querySelector('.u040-hl');
  var h3 = root.querySelector('.u040-main h3');
  function place(it) { hl.style.setProperty('--y', it.parentNode.offsetTop + 'px'); }
  tog.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var w = !root.classList.contains('is-wide');
    root.classList.toggle('is-wide', w);
    tog.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(w));
    tog.setAttribute('aria-label', w ? 'Collapse menu' : 'Expand menu');
  });
  its.forEach(function (it, i) {
    it.addEventListener('click', function () {
      its.forEach(function (x) { x.removeAttribute('aria-current'); });
      it.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
      place(it);
      h3.classList.add('swap');
      setTimeout(function () { if (!root.isConnected) return; h3.textContent = it.querySelector('span').textContent; h3.classList.remove('swap'); }, 180);
    });
    it.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      var d = e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? -1 : 0;
      if (d) { e.preventDefault(); its[(i + d + its.length) % its.length].focus(); }
    });
  });
  place(its[0]);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var ls = root.querySelector('.u273-ls'), pn = root.querySelector('.u273-pn'), bd = root.querySelector('.u273-bd'), ix = root.querySelector('.u273-ix'), pv = root.querySelector('.u273-pv'), nx = root.querySelector('.u273-nx'), x = root.querySelector('.u273-x'), ov = root.querySelector('.u273-ov');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var C = [['Seoyeon Kim', 'Studio Moment', 'Pro', '$1,284', 3], ['Junho Lee', 'Plant Lab', 'Team', '$3,920', 12], ['Haeun Park', 'Daily Florist', 'Free', '$0', 0], ['Minjun Choi', 'Runway Coffee', 'Pro', '$642', 2], ['Yuna Jung', 'Notebook Club', 'Team', '$2,160', 7]];
  var COL = ['#EC4899', '#0EA5E9', '#10B981', '#F59E0B', '#8B5CF6'];
  var ACT = ['Upgraded their plan', 'Invited 2 teammates', 'Changed payment method'];
  var R = C.map(function (c, i) {
    var li = document.createElement('li'), b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u273-rw';
    b.innerHTML = '<span class="u273-av" style="background:' + COL[i] + '"></span><div><b></b><small></small></div><em></em>';
    b.querySelector('.u273-av').textContent = c[0].charAt(0); b.querySelector('b').textContent = c[0]; b.querySelector('small').textContent = c[1]; b.querySelector('em').textContent = c[2];
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { show(i, 0); });
    li.appendChild(b); ls.appendChild(li); return b;
  });
  var cur = -1;
  function page(i) {
    var c = C[i], d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'u273-pg';
    d.innerHTML = '<span class="u273-av" style="background:' + COL[i] + '"></span><h4></h4><small></small><div class="u273-kv"><div><small></small><b></b></div><div><small></small><b></b></div></div><h5></h5>';
    d.querySelector('.u273-av').textContent = c[0].charAt(0); d.querySelector('h4').textContent = c[0]; d.querySelector('small').textContent = c[1] + ' · ' + c[2];
    var kv = d.querySelectorAll('.u273-kv div'); kv[0].querySelector('small').textContent = 'Lifetime value'; kv[0].querySelector('b').textContent = c[3]; kv[1].querySelector('small').textContent = 'Seats'; kv[1].querySelector('b').textContent = c[4] + '';
    d.querySelector('h5').textContent = 'Recent activity';
    ACT.forEach(function (a, k) { if ((i + k) % 3 === 2) return; var p = document.createElement('div'); p.className = 'u273-ac'; p.textContent = a; d.appendChild(p); });
    return d;
  }
  function show(i, dir) {
    var open = root.classList.contains('is-open');
    if (i === cur && open) return;
    var old = bd.firstChild, nw = page(i);
    if (old && open && dir && !rm) {
      nw.classList.add('is-in'); nw.style.transform = 'translateY(' + (dir * 40) + 'px)'; nw.style.opacity = '0'; bd.appendChild(nw); void nw.offsetWidth;
      nw.classList.remove('is-in'); nw.style.transform = ''; nw.style.opacity = ''; old.style.transform = 'translateY(' + (-dir * 40) + 'px)'; old.style.opacity = '0';
      setTimeout(function () { old.remove(); }, 420);
    } else { bd.innerHTML = ''; bd.appendChild(nw); }
    cur = i;
    R.forEach(function (b, k) { b.classList.toggle('is-on', k === i); b.setAttribute('aria-current', k === i ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    ix.textContent = (i + 1) + ' of ' + C.length; pv.disabled = i === 0; nx.disabled = i === C.length - 1;
    root.classList.add('is-open'); pn.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
  }
  function close() { root.classList.remove('is-open'); pn.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); if (cur >= 0) R[cur].focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
  pv.addEventListener('click', function () { if (cur > 0) show(cur - 1, -1); });
  nx.addEventListener('click', function () { if (cur < C.length - 1) show(cur + 1, 1); });
  x.addEventListener('click', close); ov.addEventListener('click', close);
  root.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!root.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') close();
    if (e.key === 'j' || e.key === 'J') nx.click();
    if (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K') pv.click();
  });
})();
</script>

.u255-cd.is-land{position:absolute;z-index:10;margin:0;transition:left .28s cubic-bezier(.3,1.2,.5,1),top .28s cubic-bezier(.3,1.2,.5,1),transform .28s}
.u255-ph{margin-bottom:6px;border-radius:11px;border:1.5px dashed color-mix(in srgb,var(--ux-accent) 50%,transparent);background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--ux-accent) 6%,transparent);transition:height .2s cubic-bezier(.3,1.2,.5,1)}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){.u255-cd,.u255-cd.is-land,.u255-ph,.u255-cd em{transition:none}.u255-ch b.is-pop{animation:none}}
</style>
<div class="ux u255"><div class="u255-bd"></div></div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var bd = root.querySelector('.u255-bd');
  var COL = ['To do', 'In progress', 'Done'], CC = ['#94A3B8', '#6366F1', '#10B981'];
  var CD = [[0, 'Pricing page draft', 'Design', '#EC4899'], [0, 'Onboarding email copy', 'Marketing', '#F59E0B'], [0, 'Checkout error logs', 'Dev', '#0EA5E9'], [1, 'Landing hero', 'Design', '#EC4899'], [1, 'Speed up the API', 'Dev', '#0EA5E9'], [2, 'Connect domain', 'Dev', '#0EA5E9']];
  var cols = COL.map(function (n, i) {
    var c = document.createElement('div'); c.className = 'u255-col'; c.dataset.c = i; c.setAttribute('role', 'list'); c.setAttribute('aria-label', n);
    c.innerHTML = '<div class="u255-ch"><i style="background:' + CC[i] + '"></i><span></span><b></b></div>'; c.querySelector('span').textContent = n;
    bd.appendChild(c); return c;
  });
  function counts(pop) { cols.forEach(function (c, i) { var b = c.querySelector('b'), n = c.querySelectorAll('.u255-cd:not(.is-lift):not(.is-land)').length + (c.querySelector('.u255-ph') ? 1 : 0); if (b.textContent != n && pop && pop.indexOf(i) >= 0) { b.classList.remove('is-pop'); void b.offsetWidth; b.classList.add('is-pop'); } b.textContent = n; }); }
  CD.forEach(function (d) {
    var e = document.createElement('div'); e.className = 'u255-cd'; e.tabIndex = 0; e.setAttribute('role', 'listitem');
    e.innerHTML = '<b></b><span style="background:' + d[3] + '"></span><em aria-hidden="true"></em>'; e.querySelector('b').textContent = d[1]; e.querySelector('span').textContent = d[2];
    cols[d[0]].appendChild(e); bind(e);
  });
  counts();
  function k() { return root.offsetWidth / (root.getBoundingClientRect().width || 1); }
  function local(x, y) { var r = root.getBoundingClientRect(), s = k(); return { x: (x - r.left) * s, y: (y - r.top) * s }; }
  var drag = null;
  function bind(e) {
    e.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (ev) {
      if (drag) return;
      var s = k(), r = e.getBoundingClientRect(), p = local(ev.clientX, ev.clientY), at = local(r.left, r.top);
      var ph = document.createElement('div'); ph.className = 'u255-ph'; ph.style.height = (r.height * s) + 'px';
      e.parentNode.insertBefore(ph, e);
      drag = { e: e, ph: ph, dx: p.x - at.x, dy: p.y - at.y, from: +e.parentNode.dataset.c, w: r.width * s };
      e.style.width = drag.w + 'px'; e.style.left = at.x + 'px'; e.style.top = at.y + 'px'; e.classList.add('is-lift'); root.appendChild(e);
      try { e.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId); } catch (er) {}
      ev.preventDefault();
    });
    e.addEventListener('pointermove', function (ev) {
      if (!drag || drag.e !== e) return;
      var p = local(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
      e.style.left = (p.x - drag.dx) + 'px'; e.style.top = (p.y - drag.dy) + 'px';
      var col = null; cols.forEach(function (c) { var r = c.getBoundingClientRect(); if (ev.clientX >= r.left && ev.clientX <= r.right) col = c; });
      cols.forEach(function (c) { c.classList.toggle('is-over', c === col && +c.dataset.c !== drag.from); });
      if (!col) return;
      var list = Array.prototype.filter.call(col.querySelectorAll('.u255-cd'), function (c) { return c !== e; }), before = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) { var r = list[i].getBoundingClientRect(); if (ev.clientY < r.top + r.height / 2) { before = list[i]; break; } }
      if (before) { if (drag.ph.nextSibling !== before) col.insertBefore(drag.ph, before); } else if (drag.ph.parentNode !== col || drag.ph.nextSibling) col.appendChild(drag.ph);
      counts();
    });
    function up() {
      if (!drag || drag.e !== e) return;
      var d = drag; drag = null;
      var s = k(), r = d.ph.getBoundingClientRect(), at = local(r.left, r.top);
      e.classList.remove('is-lift'); e.classList.add('is-land'); e.style.left = at.x + 'px'; e.style.top = at.y + 'px';
      cols.forEach(function (c) { c.classList.remove('is-over'); });
      setTimeout(function () { d.ph.parentNode.insertBefore(e, d.ph); d.ph.remove(); e.classList.remove('is-land'); e.style.cssText = ''; counts([d.from, +e.parentNode.dataset.c]); }, 290);
    }
    e.addEventListener('pointerup', up); e.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
    e.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
      var dir = ev.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : ev.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0, c = +e.parentNode.dataset.c, to = c + dir;
      if (!dir || to < 0 || to > 2) return;
      ev.preventDefault(); var h = cols[to].querySelector('.u255-ch'); cols[to].insertBefore(e, h.nextSibling); e.focus(); counts([c, to]);
    });
  }
})();
</script>

    <svg viewBox="0 0 308 140" aria-hidden="true"><rect class="u170-bd" y="6" height="106" fill="color-mix(in srgb,#E03131 12%,transparent)"/><path class="u170-ar" fill="color-mix(in srgb,#E03131 18%,transparent)"/><path class="u170-ln" fill="none" stroke="#E03131" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/><line x1="0" x2="308" y1="112" y2="112" stroke="var(--ux-line-2)"/><g class="u170-tk" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="var(--ux-ink-3)"></g><text x="2" y="14" font-size="10" font-weight="800" fill="var(--ux-ink-3)">Error rate</text></svg>
    <div class="u170-mm" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><b></b></div>
  </div>
  <div class="u170-dt" aria-live="polite"><time></time><span></span></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var ch = root.querySelector('.u170-ch'), ar = root.querySelector('.u170-ar'), ln = root.querySelector('.u170-ln'), bd = root.querySelector('.u170-bd'), tk = root.querySelector('.u170-tk');
  var zm = root.querySelector('.u170-zm'), dt = root.querySelector('.u170-dt'), mmw = root.querySelector('.u170-mm b'), mmi = root.querySelector('.u170-mm i');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var EV = [[540, 'Detected', 'Error rate crossed 5% and paged on-call', '#E03131'], [544, 'Acked', 'On-call engineer acknowledged the alert', '#F76707'], [552, 'Cause', 'Database connection pool was exhausted', '#7048E8'], [561, 'Mitigated', 'Pool enlarged and servers restarted', '#1C7ED6'], [572, 'Recovered', 'Error rate back to normal', '#2B8A3E'], [630, 'Review', 'Post-incident review agreed on follow-ups', '#868E96']];
  var ICON = ['!', '✓', '?', '↓', '✓', '✎'];
  var FULL = [480, 720], ZOOM = [534, 578], W = 308, NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  var dom = FULL.slice(), zoomed = false, sel = -1;
  function rate(m) {
    var base = 0.7 + 0.25 * Math.sin(m / 7) + 0.15 * Math.sin(m / 3.1);
    if (m < 538 || m > 575) return base;
    if (m < 546) return base + 11.7 * (m - 538) / 8;
    if (m < 561) return base + 11.7 - 1.6 * Math.sin((m - 546) / 2.3) * 0.5;
    return base + 11.7 * Math.max(0, 1 - (m - 561) / 11);
  }
  var SER = []; for (var m = 470; m <= 730; m += 1) SER.push([m, rate(m)]);
  function x(m) { return (m - dom[0]) / (dom[1] - dom[0]) * W; }
  function y(v) { return 112 - v / 13.5 * 100; }
  function fmt(m) { var h = Math.floor(m / 60), mm = Math.round(m % 60); return (h < 10 ? '0' : '') + h + ':' + (mm < 10 ? '0' : '') + mm; }
  var evs = EV.map(function (e, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u170-ev';
    b.style.setProperty('--c', e[3]); b.textContent = ICON[i]; b.setAttribute('data-l', e[1]);
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', fmt(e[0]) + ' ' + e[1]);
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { pick(i); });
    b.addEventListener('keydown', function (k) {
      var d = k.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : k.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0;
      if (!d) return;
      k.preventDefault();
      var n = Math.max(0, Math.min(EV.length - 1, (sel < 0 ? i : sel) + d));
      if (!evs[n].classList.contains('hid')) { pick(n); evs[n].focus(); }
    });
    ch.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  var cl = document.createElement('button'); cl.type = 'button'; cl.className = 'u170-cl'; ch.appendChild(cl);
  cl.addEventListener('click', function () { zoomTo(true); });
  function draw() {
    var pts = SER.filter(function (p) { return p[0] >= dom[0] - 2 && p[0] <= dom[1] + 2; }).map(function (p) { return x(p[0]).toFixed(1) + ' ' + y(p[1]).toFixed(1); });
    ln.setAttribute('d', 'M' + pts.join(' L'));
    ar.setAttribute('d', 'M' + pts[0].split(' ')[0] + ' 112 L' + pts.join(' L') + ' L' + pts[pts.length - 1].split(' ')[0] + ' 112Z');
    bd.setAttribute('x', x(540).toFixed(1)); bd.setAttribute('width', (x(572) - x(540)).toFixed(1));
    var span = dom[1] - dom[0], step = [5, 10, 15, 30, 60].filter(function (s) { return s / span * W >= 42; })[0] || 60;
    tk.innerHTML = '';
    for (var t = Math.ceil(dom[0] / step) * step; t <= dom[1]; t += step) {
      var tx = x(t);
      if (tx < 14 || tx > W - 14) continue;
      var l = document.createElementNS(NS, 'line'); l.setAttribute('x1', tx); l.setAttribute('x2', tx); l.setAttribute('y1', 112); l.setAttribute('y2', 116); l.setAttribute('stroke', 'var(--ux-line-2)'); tk.appendChild(l);
      var s = document.createElementNS(NS, 'text'); s.setAttribute('x', tx); s.setAttribute('y', 129); s.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle'); s.textContent = fmt(t); tk.appendChild(s);
    }
    var xs = EV.map(function (e) { return x(e[0]); });
    evs.forEach(function (b, i) {
      var ex = xs[i], gap = Math.min.apply(null, xs.map(function (o, k) { return k === i ? 1e9 : Math.abs(o - ex); }));
      b.style.left = ex.toFixed(1) + 'px'; b.style.display = ex < -20 || ex > W + 20 ? 'none' : '';
      b.style.setProperty('--lo', gap < 27 ? '0' : '1');
    });
    mmw.style.left = ((dom[0] - FULL[0]) / 240 * 100) + '%'; mmw.style.width = ((dom[1] - dom[0]) / 240 * 100) + '%';
  }
  function cluster(show) {
    var group = [];
    if (show) EV.forEach(function (e, i) { if (e[0] >= 538 && e[0] <= 575) group.push(i); });
    evs.forEach(function (b, i) { b.classList.toggle('hid', group.indexOf(i) >= 0); });
    cl.classList.toggle('show', group.length > 0);
    if (group.length) {
      var cx = group.reduce(function (s, i) { return s + x(EV[i][0]); }, 0) / group.length;
      cl.style.left = cx.toFixed(1) + 'px'; cl.textContent = group.length;
      cl.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Events: ' + group.length + ', press to zoom in');
    }
  }
  function detail() {
    var t = dt.querySelector('time'), s = dt.querySelector('span');
    if (sel < 0) { t.textContent = 'Summary'; dt.style.removeProperty('--c'); s.textContent = 'Detected → recovered in 32 min · peak error rate 12.4%'; }
    else { var e = EV[sel]; t.textContent = fmt(e[0]); dt.style.setProperty('--c', e[3]); s.innerHTML = '<b></b>'; s.firstChild.textContent = e[1]; s.appendChild(document.createTextNode(e[2])); }
    dt.classList.remove('fresh'); void dt.offsetWidth; dt.classList.add('fresh');
  }
  function pick(i) {
    sel = sel === i ? -1 : i;
    evs.forEach(function (b, k) { b.classList.toggle('on', k === sel); b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(k === sel)); });
    detail();
  }
  function zoomTo(z) {
    zoomed = z;
    zm.textContent = z ? 'Show all' : 'Zoom to incident';
    if (!z && sel >= 0 && EV[sel][0] >= 538 && EV[sel][0] <= 575) pick(sel);
    cluster(false);
    var from = dom.slice(), to = z ? ZOOM : FULL, t0 = performance.now();
    (function f(now) {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      var k = rm ? 1 : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (now - t0) / 650)), e = k < 0.5 ? 4 * k * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 3) / 2;
      dom = [from[0] + (to[0] - from[0]) * e, from[1] + (to[1] - from[1]) * e];
      draw();
      if (k < 1) { requestAnimationFrame(f); return; }
      if (!z) cluster(true);
    })(t0);
  }
  zm.addEventListener('click', function () { zoomTo(!zoomed); });
  mmi.style.left = ((540 - FULL[0]) / 240 * 100) + '%'; mmi.style.width = (32 / 240 * 100) + '%';
  draw(); cluster(true); detail();
})();
</script>

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #098 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#098 -->
<!--
Element: Live metric with rolling digits
ID: 098 · Category: Widgets & Charts · Tags: kpi, odometer, sparkline, realtime, dashboard
What it does: Each digit of the big number rolls on its own, so when the value changes only the digits that differ spin up or down. The trend line below shifts one step left with every new value, and switching metrics rolls the number up from zero while the line morphs into its new shape.
How to use it: Watch the value change every few seconds. Tap Visitors, Conversion or Revenue below to switch metrics, and pause live updates with the button in the top right
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.

.u098{position:relative;width:340px;height:410px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:20px 18px 16px;border-radius:30px;background:var(--ux-surface);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ux-line),0 30px 60px -36px var(--ux-shadow-strong);overflow:hidden}

.u098.paused .u098-live{background:var(--ux-ink-3);animation:none}

<div class="ux u098">
  <div class="u098-h"><span class="u098-live" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="u098-tt">Live visitors</span><button class="u098-pp" type="button" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Pause live updates"></button></div>
  <div class="u098-num" role="img"></div>
  <div class="u098-dl"><span class="u098-bd"><svg viewBox="0 0 10 10" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 1.5L9 7.5H1z"/></svg><b></b></span><span>vs an hour ago</span></div>
  <div class="u098-sp" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 340 128"><defs><linearGradient class="u098-gd" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-opacity=".26"/><stop offset="1" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient></defs><g class="u098-g"><path class="u098-ar"/><path class="u098-ln"/><circle class="u098-hd" r="5"/></g></svg></div>
  <div class="u098-tabs" role="group" aria-label="Metrics"></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
  var uid = 'u098' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  var num = root.querySelector('.u098-num'), tt = root.querySelector('.u098-tt'), pp = root.querySelector('.u098-pp'), bd = root.querySelector('.u098-bd'), bdv = bd.querySelector('b');
  var g = root.querySelector('.u098-g'), ln = root.querySelector('.u098-ln'), ar = root.querySelector('.u098-ar'), hd = root.querySelector('.u098-hd'), tabs = root.querySelector('.u098-tabs');
  var rm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  root.querySelector('.u098-gd').id = uid; ar.setAttribute('fill', 'url(#' + uid + ')');
  var PLAY = '<path d="M3.5 2.2v9.6L11.6 7z"/>', PAUSE = '<rect x="3" y="2.5" width="3" height="9" rx="1"/><rect x="8" y="2.5" width="3" height="9" rx="1"/>';
  var N = 24, W = 340, H = 128, STEP = (W - 16) / (N - 1);
  function walk(seed, a, vol, n) { var s = seed, v = a, out = []; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280; v += (s / 233280 - 0.48) * vol; out.push(v); } return out; }
  var M = [
    { name: 'Visitors', title: 'Live visitors', v: walk(3, 12400, 90, N), vol: 70, fmt: function (v) { return Math.round(v).toLocaleString('ko-KR'); }, tail: '', base: 12090 },
    { name: 'Conversion', title: 'Live conversion rate', v: walk(8, 3.6, 0.05, N), vol: 0.04, fmt: function (v) { return v.toFixed(2); }, tail: '%', base: 3.71 },
    { name: 'Revenue', title: 'Revenue today', v: walk(5, 8200, 40, N), vol: 30, fmt: function (v) { return (v / 100).toFixed(2); }, tail: 'M', head: '₩', base: 7930 }
  ];
  var k = 0, ys = null, paused = false, seen = true, timer = 0, busy = false, tw = 0, shown = '';
  var tb = M.map(function (m, i) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'u098-tb'; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(i === 0));
    b.innerHTML = '<small></small><b></b><em></em>';
    b.querySelector('small').textContent = m.name;
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { if (i !== k) pick(i); });
    tabs.appendChild(b);
    return b;
  });
  function last(m) { return m.v[m.v.length - 1]; }
  function text(m) { return (m.head || '') + m.fmt(last(m)) + m.tail; }
  function digits(str, fresh) {
    var cols = num.querySelectorAll('.u098-col');
    var same = !fresh && shown.replace(/\d/g, '0') === str.replace(/\d/g, '0');
    if (!same) {
      num.innerHTML = '';
      str.split('').forEach(function (ch, i) {
        var s = document.createElement('span');
        if (/\d/.test(ch)) {
          s.className = 'u098-col'; s.style.setProperty('--d', 0); s.style.setProperty('--i', i);
          s.innerHTML = '<span>0</span><span>1</span><span>2</span><span>3</span><span>4</span><span>5</span><span>6</span><span>7</span><span>8</span><span>9</span>';
        } else { s.className = 'u098-sym' + (ch === 'M' || ch === '%' ? ' sm' : ''); s.textContent = ch; }
        num.appendChild(s);
      });
      void num.offsetWidth;
      cols = num.querySelectorAll('.u098-col');
    }
    var j = 0;
    str.split('').forEach(function (ch) { if (/\d/.test(ch)) cols[j++].style.setProperty('--d', ch); });
    shown = str;
    num.setAttribute('aria-label', M[k].title + ' ' + str);
  }
  function domain(v) { var mn = Math.min.apply(null, v), mx = Math.max.apply(null, v), p = (mx - mn) * 0.35 || 1; return [mn - p, mx + p]; }
  var dom = domain(M[0].v);
  function Y(v) { return 8 + (1 - (v - dom[0]) / (dom[1] - dom[0])) * (H - 16); }
  function path(y) { return y.map(function (v, i) { return (i ? 'L' : 'M') + (i * STEP).toFixed(1) + ' ' + v.toFixed(1); }).join(''); }
  function paint(y) {
    var p = path(y), n = y.length - 1;
    ln.setAttribute('d', p); ar.setAttribute('d', p + 'L' + (n * STEP).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (H + 4) + 'L0 ' + (H + 4) + 'Z');
    hd.setAttribute('cx', (n * STEP).toFixed(1)); hd.setAttribute('cy', y[n].toFixed(1));
  }
  function badge(m) {
    var r = (last(m) - m.base) / m.base, up = r >= 0;
    bd.classList.toggle('dn', !up); bd.style.setProperty('--c', up ? 'var(--ux-mint)' : 'var(--ux-danger)');
    bdv.textContent = Math.abs(r * 100).toFixed(1) + '%';
    tb.forEach(function (b, i) {
      var mm = M[i], rr = (last(mm) - mm.base) / mm.base;
      b.querySelector('b').textContent = text(mm);
      var em = b.querySelector('em'); em.textContent = (rr >= 0 ? '+' : '−') + Math.abs(rr * 100).toFixed(1) + '%'; em.classList.toggle('dn', rr < 0);
    });
  }
  function tick() {
    timer = 0;
    if (!root.isConnected) return;
    if (paused || !seen || busy) return schedule();
    var m = M[k], prev = last(m), mid = (dom[0] + dom[1]) / 2;
    var nv = Math.max(dom[0] + (dom[1] - dom[0]) * 0.12, Math.min(dom[1] - (dom[1] - dom[0]) * 0.12, prev + (Math.random() - 0.5) * m.vol * 2 + (mid - prev) * 0.15));
    M.forEach(function (mm, i) { if (i !== k) { mm.v.push(last(mm) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * mm.vol); mm.v.shift(); } });
    m.v.push(nv);
    ys = ys.concat([Y(nv)]);
    paint(ys);
    digits(text(m)); badge(m);
    if (rm) { m.v.shift(); ys.shift(); paint(ys); return schedule(); }
    busy = true;
    var t0 = performance.now();
    (function f(n) {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      var t = Math.min(1, (n - t0) / 700), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      g.setAttribute('transform', 'translate(' + (-STEP * e).toFixed(2) + ' 0)');
      if (t < 1) return requestAnimationFrame(f);
      m.v.shift(); ys.shift(); g.removeAttribute('transform'); paint(ys); busy = false;
    })(t0);
    schedule();
  }
  function schedule() { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(tick, 2400); }
  function pick(i) {
    k = i;
    tb.forEach(function (b, j) { b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(j === i)); });
    tt.textContent = M[i].title;
    dom = domain(M[i].v);
    var to = M[i].v.map(Y), from = ys.slice(0, to.length), t0 = performance.now();
    cancelAnimationFrame(tw);
    (function f(n) {
      if (!root.isConnected) return;
      var t = rm ? 1 : Math.min(1, (n - t0) / 600), e = t < .5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2;
      ys = from.map(function (y, j) { return y + (to[j] - y) * e; }); paint(ys);
      if (t < 1) tw = requestAnimationFrame(f);
    })(t0);
    digits(text(M[i]), true); badge(M[i]);
    schedule();
  }
  pp.addEventListener('click', function () {
    paused = !paused;
    root.classList.toggle('paused', paused);
    pp.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(paused));
    pp.setAttribute('aria-label', paused ? 'Resume live updates' : 'Pause live updates');
    pp.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" aria-hidden="true">' + (paused ? PLAY : PAUSE) + '</svg>';
  });
  pp.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" aria-hidden="true">' + PAUSE + '</svg>';
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (es) { if (!root.isConnected) return io.disconnect(); seen = es[0].isIntersecting; });
    io.observe(root);
  }
  ys = M[0].v.map(Y); paint(ys);
  digits(text(M[0]), !rm); badge(M[0]);
  schedule();
})();
</script>


##############################################################################
NORDA COMPONENT 66 / 75 - ID 095 - Bar chart that changes shape
##############################################################################

<!-- Lucian UX/UI Prompt #095 · MIT License (c) 2026 Seokwoooo · https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#095 -->
<!--
Element: Bar chart that changes shape
ID: 095 · Category: Widgets & Charts · Tags: chart, bar, morph, tooltip, scrub
What it does: Switch between week, month and year and the bars don’t regrow; their count, width and height carry over into the new shape while the axis numbers and average line move along. Sweep across the bars and only the one under you darkens while the big number and tooltip switch to its value and change from the previous one.
How to use it: Move the mouse or sweep a finger across the bars. Click a bar to pin it, and switch the period with W·M·Y in the top right. On a keyboard, use ←/→ on the bars
Footprint: fixed size, square
Needs: tokens.css (the --ux-* theme tokens and the .ux base). Map the tokens to the project's theme variables.
Live demo: https://lucianlabs.dev/uxui-prompt/#095
-->
<style>

<div class="ux u095">
